function drawDataPlot() {
  const p = curPlot(), out = $('plOut'); if (!out) return;
  if (!p.cores.length || !p.ys.length) { out.innerHTML = `<p class="note">${t('pl.empty')}</p>`; plotLast = null; return; }
  const { kind, spec } = plotSpec(p), r = kind === 'strat' ? buildStratPlot(spec) : buildXYPlot(spec);
  plotLast = { ...r, spec, kind };
  const f3 = (v) => (v == null || !Number.isFinite(v) ? '—' : +v.toPrecision(4));
  out.innerHTML = `<div class="psvg">${r.svg}</div>
    ${r.fits.length ? `<h4>${t('pl.fits')}</h4><table class="dtable mtab"><thead><tr><th>${t('pl.series')}</th><th>${t('pl.eq')}</th><th>R²</th><th>n</th></tr></thead><tbody>${r.fits.map((f) => `<tr><td class="txt">${esc(f.name)}</td><td class="txt">${esc(f.eq)}</td><td>${f3(f.r2)}</td><td>${f.n}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>` : ''}
    <h4>${t('pl.stats')}</h4><table class="dtable mtab"><thead><tr><th>${t('pl.series')}</th><th>n</th><th>${t('pl.mean')}</th><th>${t('pl.sd')}</th><th>${t('pl.min')}</th><th>${t('pl.median')}</th><th>${t('pl.max')}</th>${kind === 'xy' ? '<th>r (X, Y)</th>' : ''}</tr></thead><tbody>${r.stats.map((q) => `<tr><td class="txt">${esc(q.name)}</td><td>${q.n}</td><td>${f3(q.mean)}</td><td>${f3(q.sd)}</td><td>${f3(q.min)}</td><td>${f3(q.median)}</td><td>${f3(q.max)}</td>${kind === 'xy' ? `<td>${f3(q.r)}</td>` : ''}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`;
}
$('dBody').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  if ($('dView').value !== 'plot') return;
  const el = e.target, p = curPlot(), val = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : el.type === 'number' ? (el.value === '' ? null : +el.value) : el.value;
  if (el.id === 'plSel') { S.plotCur = +el.value; renderDataPlot(); return; }
  if (el.dataset.core) { if (el.checked) p.cores.push(el.dataset.core); else p.cores = p.cores.filter((id) => id !== el.dataset.core); }
  else if (el.dataset.p) { p[el.dataset.p] = val; if (el.dataset.p === 'layout' && val === 'strat' && (p.v !== 'depth' && p.v !== 'age')) p.v = 'depth'; }
  else if (el.dataset.yk) { const i = +el.closest('[data-y]').dataset.y; p.ys[i][el.dataset.yk] = val; }
  else return;
  markDirty();
  const rebuild = ['layout', 'fit'].includes(el.dataset.p || el.dataset.yk) || el.dataset.p === 'name';
  if (rebuild) renderDataPlot(); else drawDataPlot();
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const a = e.target.closest('[data-pa]')?.dataset.pa; if (!a || $('dView').value !== 'plot') return;
  const p = curPlot(), cols = plotColumns();
  if (a === 'addy') p.ys.push({ col: cols.find((c) => !p.ys.some((y) => y.col === c)) || cols[0], type: 'line', y2: false, fit: 'none', deg: 2, window: 5, log: false, inv: false, color: '' });
  else if (a === 'dely') p.ys.splice(+e.target.closest('[data-y]').dataset.y, 1);
  else if (a === 'new') { S.plots.push(defaultPlot(S.plots.length + 1)); S.plotCur = S.plots.length - 1; }
  else if (a === 'dup') { S.plots.push({ ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p)), name: p.name + ' (2)' }); S.plotCur = S.plots.length - 1; }
  else if (a === 'del') { S.plots.splice(S.plotCur, 1); S.plotCur = 0; }
  else if (a === 'svg') { if (plotLast) saveFile(`${safe(p.name)}.svg`, plotLast.svg, 'image/svg+xml'); return; }
  else if (a === 'png') { if (plotLast) busy(async () => { const img = new Image(); img.src = 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(plotLast.svg); await img.decode(); const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = img.width * 3; c.height = img.height * 3; const ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height); ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height); const b = await new Promise((r) => c.toBlob(r, 'image/png')); if (b) await saveFile(`${safe(p.name)}.png`, b, 'image/png'); }); return; }
  else if (a === 'csv') {
    const rows = [['core', p.v, 'variable', 'value']];
    for (const c of S.cores.filter((q) => p.cores.includes(q.id))) for (const y of p.ys) { const X = plotCol(c, p.v), Y = plotCol(c, y.col); for (let i = 0; i < X.length; i++) if (X[i] != null && Y[i] != null) rows.push([c.name, X[i], y.col, Y[i]]); }
    saveFile(`${safe(p.name)}.csv`, withProv(toCSV(rows)), 'text/csv'); return;
  }
  markDirty(); renderDataPlot();
});

let dataLimit = 400, dataViewportOn = false;
/** @type {ReturnType<typeof mountTableViewport> | null} */
let dataViewport = null;
export function disposeDataTable() {
  dataViewport?.destroy(); dataViewport = null;
  const active = document.activeElement;
  if (active instanceof HTMLElement && $('dBody').contains(active)) active.blur();
}
const SRC_ORDER = { imp: 0, rec: 1, img: 2, man: 3, calc: 4 };
function dataCores() { const v = $('dCore').value; return v === '__all' ? S.cores : S.cores.filter((c) => c.id === (v || S.curCore)); }
export function renderData() {
  if (mode !== 'data') return;
  const sel = $('dCore'), cur = sel.value || S.curCore;
  sel.innerHTML = S.cores.map((c) => `<option value="${c.id}" ${c.id === cur ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(c.name)}</option>`).join('') + `<option value="__all" ${cur === '__all' ? 'selected' : ''}>${t('data.allCores')}</option>`;
  refreshAllCores();
  // V0.35: the sheet replaces the old «computed columns» view; the free cell sheet stays only for projects that use it
  for (const o of $('dView').options) if (o.value === 'cols' || (o.value === 'cells' && !Object.keys(S.sheet.cells || {}).length)) o.hidden = true;
  if ($('dView').selectedOptions[0]?.hidden) $('dView').value = 'table';
  const view = $('dView').value;
  if (view !== 'table') disposeDataTable();
  if (view === 'cols') renderDataCols(); else if (view === 'cells') renderDataCells(); else if (view === 'plot') renderDataPlot(); else renderDataTable();
}
function tableModel(limit = Infinity, offset = 0) {
  const cores = dataCores(), names = new Map();
  for (const c of cores) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(c.src)) if (!names.has(k)) names.set(k, v);
  const cols = [...names.entries()].sort((a, b) => SRC_ORDER[a[1]] - SRC_ORDER[b[1]] || a[0].localeCompare(b[0]));
  const layers = $('dLayers').checked ? S.geo.layers.filter((L) => S.geo.intervals.some((q) => q.layer === L.id && cores.some((c) => c.id === q.core))) : [];
  const rows = [];
  let totalRows = 0;
  for (const c of cores) {
    const n = (c.depth || c.age || []).length;
    const first = Math.min(n, Math.max(0, offset - totalRows));
    totalRows += n;
    const displayed = Math.min(n - first, Math.max(0, limit - rows.length));
    if (!displayed) continue;
    const intervalNames = new Map();
    const ivs = new Map(layers.map((L) => {
      const intervals = intervalsOnAxis(S.geo.intervals.filter((q) => q.core === c.id && q.layer === L.id), c.depth ? 'depth' : 'age', c);
      for (const q of intervals) intervalNames.set(q, geoCat(q.cat)?.name || '');
      return [L.id, createIntervalLookup(intervals)];
    }));
    for (let i = first; i < first + displayed; i++) {
      const x = c.depth ? c.depth[i] : c.age[i];
      rows.push({ c, i, depth: c.depth?.[i] ?? null, age: c.age?.[i] ?? null, vals: cols.map(([k]) => c.proxies[k]?.[i] ?? null),
        lay: layers.map((L) => intervalNames.get(ivs.get(L.id)(x)) || '') });
    }
  }
  return { cores, cols, layers, rows, totalRows };
}
/* ───────────── V0.35: Data as a spreadsheet — every value editable (flagged), computed columns ───────────── */
let dataEditOn = false, dataFormOpen = null, dataLastCell = null;
/** @param {ReturnType<typeof tableModel>} M @param {number} [offset] */
function tableRowsHTML(M, offset = 0) {
  const multi = M.cores.length > 1;
  return M.rows.map((r, ri) => {
    const x = r.depth ?? r.age;
    return `<tr data-row="${offset + ri}"><td class="rn">${offset + ri + 1}</td>${multi ? `<td class="txt">${esc(r.c.name)}</td>` : ''}<td class="ax">${fmt(r.depth, 4)}</td><td class="ax">${fmt(r.age, 3)}</td>${r.vals.map((v, j) => {
      const [k, src] = M.cols[j], ed = src !== 'man' && cellEdit(r.c, k, x), canEdit = dataEditOn && src !== 'calc' && x != null;
      const tip = ed ? t('ds.modTip', { o: ed.o == null ? '—' : fmt(ed.o, 6), d: (ed.at || '').slice(0, 10) }) : '';
      return `<td${canEdit ? ' contenteditable spellcheck="false"' : ''} class="${ed ? 'mod' : ''}${src === 'calc' ? ' calc' : ''}" data-core="${r.c.id}" data-col="${esc(k)}" data-src="${src}" data-x="${x}"${tip ? ` title="${esc(tip)}"` : ''}>${v == null ? '' : fmt(v, 6)}</td>`;
    }).join('')}${r.lay.map((v) => `<td class="txt">${esc(v)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function renderDataTable() {
  const previous = /** @type {HTMLElement | null} */ ($('dBody').querySelector('.dsgrid'));
  const context = JSON.stringify([S.id, /** @type {HTMLSelectElement} */ ($('dCore')).value,
    /** @type {HTMLInputElement} */ ($('dLayers')).checked]);
  const scrollTop = dataViewportOn && previous?.dataset.context === context ? previous.scrollTop : 0;
  disposeDataTable();
  const M = tableModel(dataViewportOn ? 0 : dataLimit), multi = M.cores.length > 1, nEd = editCount(M.cores);
  $('dInfo').textContent = t('data.info', { r: M.totalRows, c: M.cols.length });
  const colLetter = (i) => cellName(i, 0).replace(/\d+$/, '');
  const fixed = (multi ? 1 : 0) + 2;
  const head = `<tr><th class="rn"></th>${multi ? `<th class="txt">${t('geo.core')}</th>` : ''}<th>${t('r.depth')}</th><th>${t('r.age')}</th>${M.cols.map(([k, src], j) => {
    const calc = S.sheet.columns.findIndex((x) => x.name === k);
    return `<th class="src-${src}" title="${esc(t('data.src.' + src))}"><span class="tag">${colLetter(fixed + j)} · ${t('data.src.' + src)}</span>${esc(k)}${src === 'calc' && calc >= 0 ? ` <button type="button" class="btn small ghost fx" data-fx="${calc}" title="${esc(t('ds.editFx'))}">ƒ</button>` : ''}</th>`;
  }).join('')}${M.layers.map((L) => `<th class="src-lay txt"><span class="tag">${t('data.src.lay')}</span>${esc(L.name)}</th>`).join('')}</tr>`;
  const body = tableRowsHTML(M);
  const cores = S.cores.map((c) => `<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name)}</option>`).join('');
  const f = dataFormOpen, fcol = f?.i != null ? S.sheet.columns[f.i] : null;
  const names = [...new Set(M.cols.map(([k]) => k)), 'depth', 'age'];
  const form = f ? `<div class="dsform">
      <div class="row"><label class="f"><span>${t('ds.type')}</span><select id="dsType" ${fcol ? 'disabled' : ''}><option value="calc" ${f.type !== 'man' ? 'selected' : ''}>${t('ds.calc')}</option><option value="man" ${f.type === 'man' ? 'selected' : ''}>${t('ds.man')}</option></select></label>
      <label class="f" style="flex:1"><span>${t('ds.name')}</span><input type="text" id="dsName" value="${esc(fcol?.name || '')}"></label>
      <label class="f"><span>${t('data.core')}</span><select id="dsCore">${f.type === 'man' ? '' : `<option value="all">${t('data.allCores')}</option>`}${cores}</select></label></div>
      ${f.type !== 'man' ? `<label class="f"><span>${t('ds.formula')}</span><input type="text" id="dsFormula" value="${esc(fcol?.formula || '')}" placeholder="[reflectance_L*] / [reflectance_a*]" spellcheck="false"></label>
      <p class="note">${t('ds.formulaHelp', { f: FUNCTIONS.join(', ') })}</p>
      <div class="dsnames">${names.map((n) => `<button type="button" class="btn small ghost" data-ins="${esc(n)}">${esc(n)}</button>`).join('')}</div>` : `<p class="note">${t('ds.manHelp')}</p>`}
      ${f.type !== 'man' ? `<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" id="dsInterp" ${fcol?.interp === false ? '' : 'checked'}><span>${t('ds.interp')}</span></label>` : ''}
      ${fcol?.error ? `<p class="note err">${esc(fcol.error)}</p>` : ''}
      <div class="row"><button type="button" class="btn small primary" id="dsSave">${t(fcol ? 'ds.save' : 'ds.add')}</button>${fcol ? `<button type="button" class="btn small" id="dsDel">${t('ds.del')}</button>` : ''}<button type="button" class="btn small ghost" id="dsCancel">${t('ds.cancel')}</button></div></div>` : '';
  $('dBody').innerHTML = `<div class="dsbar">
      <label class="chk ${dataEditOn ? 'on' : ''}"><input type="checkbox" id="dsEdit" ${dataEditOn ? 'checked' : ''}><span>${t('ds.edit')}</span></label>
      <label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" id="dsViewport" ${dataViewportOn ? 'checked' : ''}><span>${t('ds.viewport')}</span></label>
      <button type="button" class="btn small" id="dsNewCol">${t('ds.newCol')}</button>
      <span class="dsmod">${nEd ? t('ds.nMod', { n: nEd }) : ''}</span>
      ${nEd || dataViewportOn ? `<button type="button" class="btn small ghost" id="dsRestoreCell" disabled ${nEd ? '' : 'hidden'}>${t('ds.restoreCell')}</button><button type="button" class="btn small ghost" id="dsRestoreAll" ${nEd ? '' : 'hidden'}>${t('ds.restoreAll')}</button>` : ''}
    </div>
    ${dataEditOn ? `<p class="dswarn">${t('ds.warn')}</p>` : ''}${form}
    <div class="dsgrid"><table class="dtable sheet ${dataViewportOn ? 'dsviewport' : ''}" ${dataViewportOn ? `style="width:${50 + (multi ? 180 : 0) + 256 + M.cols.length * 132 + M.layers.length * 180}px"` : ''}>
      ${dataViewportOn ? `<colgroup>${[50, ...(multi ? [180] : []), 128, 128, ...M.cols.map(() => 132), ...M.layers.map(() => 180)].map((width) => `<col style="width:${width}px">`).join('')}</colgroup>` : ''}
      <thead>${head}</thead><tbody>${body}</tbody></table></div>
    ${M.totalRows > dataLimit ? `<p><button class="btn small" id="dMore">${t('data.more', { n: M.totalRows - dataLimit })}</button></p>` : ''}`;
  if (dataViewportOn) {
    const grid = /** @type {HTMLElement} */ ($('dBody').querySelector('.dsgrid')); grid.dataset.context = context;
    dataViewport = mountTableViewport({ grid, body: grid.querySelector('tbody'), rowCount: Math.min(dataLimit, M.totalRows),
      columnCount: fixed + 1 + M.cols.length + M.layers.length, scrollTop,
      renderRows: (start, end) => tableRowsHTML(tableModel(end - start, start), start) });
  }
  if ($('dMore')) $('dMore').onclick = () => { dataLimit += 1000; renderDataTable(); };
  if (f && $('dsCore')) $('dsCore').value = fcol?.core || (f.type === 'man' ? M.cores[0]?.id : 'all') || 'all';
}
$('dBody').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  const target = /** @type {HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement} */ (e.target);
  if (target instanceof HTMLInputElement && target.id === 'dsViewport') { dataViewportOn = target.checked; renderDataTable(); }
  else if (target instanceof HTMLInputElement && target.id === 'dsEdit') { dataEditOn = target.checked; renderDataTable(); }
  else if (target.id === 'dsType') { dataFormOpen = { type: target.value }; renderDataTable(); }
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b) return;
  if (b.id === 'dsNewCol') { dataFormOpen = dataFormOpen ? null : { type: 'calc' }; renderDataTable(); }
  else if (b.dataset.fx != null) { dataFormOpen = { type: 'calc', i: +b.dataset.fx }; renderDataTable(); }
  else if (b.dataset.ins != null) { const i = $('dsFormula'), ins = /^[\w.]+$/.test(b.dataset.ins) ? b.dataset.ins : `[${b.dataset.ins}]`; const p0 = i.selectionStart ?? i.value.length; i.value = i.value.slice(0, p0) + ins + i.value.slice(i.selectionEnd ?? p0); i.focus(); i.selectionStart = i.selectionEnd = p0 + ins.length; }
  else if (b.id === 'dsCancel') { dataFormOpen = null; renderDataTable(); }
  else if (b.id === 'dsDel') { S.sheet.columns.splice(dataFormOpen.i, 1); dataFormOpen = null; refreshAllCores(); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataTable(); }
  else if (b.id === 'dsSave') {
    const name = $('dsName').value.trim(), core = $('dsCore').value; if (!name) { toast(t('ds.needName')); return; }
    if (dataFormOpen.type === 'man') {
      const c = S.cores.find((x) => x.id === core); if (!c) return;
      c.user ||= {}; c.user[name] ||= { d: (c.depth || []).slice(), v: (c.depth || []).map(() => null) };
      dataEditOn = true; refreshCoreData(c);
    } else {
      const formula = $('dsFormula').value.trim(); if (!formula) { toast(t('ds.needFormula')); return; }
      try { parseFormula(formula); } catch (err) { toast(t('ds.badFormula', { e: fxText(err) })); return; }
      const interp = $('dsInterp')?.checked !== false;
      if (dataFormOpen.i != null) Object.assign(S.sheet.columns[dataFormOpen.i], { name, formula, core, interp }); else S.sheet.columns.push({ name, formula, core, interp });
      refreshAllCores();
    }
    dataFormOpen = null; geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataTable();
  }
  else if (b.id === 'dsRestoreAll') { if (!armed(b)) return; for (const c of dataCores()) c.edits = {}; refreshAllCores(); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataTable(); }
  else if (b.id === 'dsRestoreCell' && dataLastCell) {
    const { core, col, x } = dataLastCell, c = S.cores.find((q) => q.id === core);
    if (c?.edits?.[col]) { delete c.edits[col][x]; if (!Object.keys(c.edits[col]).length) delete c.edits[col]; }
    dataLastCell = null; refreshCoreData(c); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataTable();
  }
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('focusin', (e) => {
  const td = e.target.closest?.('td[data-col]'); if (!td) return;
  td.dataset.before = td.textContent;
  dataLastCell = td.classList.contains('mod') ? { core: td.dataset.core, col: td.dataset.col, x: td.dataset.x } : null;
  if ($('dsRestoreCell')) $('dsRestoreCell').disabled = !dataLastCell;
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
  if (!dataViewport || !(e instanceof InputEvent) || e.isComposing || !(e.target instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
  const cell = e.target.closest('td[contenteditable]');
  if (!cell || !cell.children.length) return;
  // Rich pasted markup must not change the fixed row geometry; numeric validation still uses its text.
  const text = cell.textContent || ''; cell.textContent = text;
  if (document.activeElement === cell) window.getSelection()?.collapse(cell.firstChild || cell, text.length);
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  const td = e.target.closest?.('td[contenteditable]'); if (!td) return;
  if (e.key === 'Escape') { td.textContent = td.dataset.before || ''; td.blur(); return; }
  if (dataViewport) {
    const row = td.parentElement, grid = row.closest('.dsgrid');
    const available = Number(grid.dataset.available), index = Number(row.dataset.row);
    let target = index, column = td.cellIndex;
    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
      const editable = [...row.cells].filter((cell) => cell.isContentEditable).map((cell) => cell.cellIndex);
      const position = editable.indexOf(column) + (e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
      column = editable[(position + editable.length) % editable.length];
      if (position < 0) target--; else if (position >= editable.length) target++;
      if (target < 0 || target >= available) return;
    } else if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && ['Home', 'End'].includes(e.key)) {
      const editable = [...row.cells].filter((cell) => cell.isContentEditable);
      target = e.key === 'Home' ? 0 : available - 1;
      column = (e.key === 'Home' ? editable[0] : editable.at(-1)).cellIndex;
    } else {
      if (e.shiftKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
      if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') target++;
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') target--;
      else if (e.key === 'PageDown') target += dataViewport.pageRows();
      else if (e.key === 'PageUp') target -= dataViewport.pageRows();
      else return;
      if (e.key.startsWith('Page')) target = Math.max(0, Math.min(available - 1, target));
    }
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!dataViewport.focusCell(target, column)) td.blur();
    return;
  }
  const move = e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? -1 : 0; if (!move) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const tr = move > 0 ? td.parentElement.nextElementSibling : td.parentElement.previousElementSibling, nx = tr?.children[td.cellIndex];
  if (nx?.isContentEditable) nx.focus(); else td.blur();
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('focusout', (e) => {
  const td = e.target.closest('td[contenteditable]'); if (!td) return;
  const raw = td.textContent.trim(); if (raw === (td.dataset.before || '').trim()) return;
  const c = S.cores.find((x) => x.id === td.dataset.core); if (!c) return;
  const v = raw === '' ? null : +raw.replace(',', '.');
  if (raw !== '' && !Number.isFinite(v)) { td.textContent = td.dataset.before || ''; toast(t('ds.notNumber')); return; }
  const col = td.dataset.col, x = td.dataset.x;
  if (td.dataset.src === 'man') {   // manual column: the value is the data itself
    const u = c.user?.[col]; if (!u) return; const d = +x, k = u.d.indexOf(d);
    if (k >= 0) u.v[k] = v; else { u.d.push(d); u.v.push(v); }
  } else {   // V0.35: any other value is kept as an edit over the original, flagged and reversible
    c.edits ||= {}; const E = (c.edits[col] ||= {}), prev = E[x];
    const orig = prev ? prev.o : (td.dataset.before === '' ? null : +td.dataset.before.replace(',', '.'));
    if (orig != null && v != null && Math.abs(orig - v) <= 1e-12 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(orig))) delete E[x];
    else E[x] = { v, o: orig, at: new Date().toISOString() };
    if (!Object.keys(E).length) delete c.edits[col];
  }
  refreshCoreData(c); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty();
  const ed = cellEdit(c, col, x); td.classList.toggle('mod', !!ed);
  if (ed) td.title = t('ds.modTip', { o: ed.o == null ? '—' : fmt(ed.o, 6), d: ed.at.slice(0, 10) }); else td.removeAttribute('title');
  const n = editCount(dataCores()); const m = $('dBody').querySelector('.dsmod'); if (m) m.textContent = n ? t('ds.nMod', { n }) : '';
  if (dataViewport) {
    for (const id of ['dsRestoreAll', 'dsRestoreCell']) if ($(id)) $(id).hidden = !n;
  } else if (n && !$('dsRestoreAll')) renderDataTable();
});
function renderDataCols() {
  const cores = S.cores.map((c) => `<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name)}</option>`).join('');
  $('dInfo').textContent = '';
  $('dBody').innerHTML = `<h3>${t('data.calcTitle')}</h3><p class="note">${t('data.calcHelp', { f: FUNCTIONS.join(', ') })}</p>
    <div style="display:grid;gap:6px;max-width:1100px">${S.sheet.columns.map((col, i) => `<div class="colrow" data-i="${i}"><input type="text" data-k="name" value="${esc(col.name)}"><input type="text" data-k="formula" value="${esc(col.formula)}"><select data-k="core"><option value="all">${t('data.allCores')}</option>${S.cores.map((c) => `<option value="${c.id}" ${c.id === col.core ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(c.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select><button class="btn small ghost" data-del="${i}">✕</button>${col.error ? `<span class="note" style="grid-column:1/-1;color:var(--danger)">${esc(col.error)}</span>` : ''}</div>`).join('') || `<p class="note">—</p>`}
    <div class="colrow"><input type="text" id="ncName" placeholder="${t('data.newName')}"><input type="text" id="ncFormula" placeholder="[XRF Ba/Ti] / TOC_pct"><select id="ncCore"><option value="all">${t('data.allCores')}</option>${cores}</select><button class="btn small primary" id="ncAdd">${t('data.addCalc')}</button></div></div>
    <h3>${t('data.manTitle')}</h3><p class="note">${t('data.manHelp')}</p>
    <div class="row"><input type="text" id="nmName" placeholder="${t('data.newName')}"><select id="nmCore">${cores}</select><button class="btn small" id="nmAdd">${t('data.addMan')}</button></div>
    <div style="display:grid;gap:4px">${S.cores.flatMap((c) => Object.keys(c.user || {}).map((k) => `<div class="row"><span>${esc(c.name)} · <b>${esc(k)}</b> (${c.user[k].v.filter((v) => v != null).length})</span><button class="btn small ghost" data-delman="${c.id}|${esc(k)}">✕</button></div>`)).join('')}</div>`;
  $('ncAdd').onclick = () => {
    const name = $('ncName').value.trim(), formula = $('ncFormula').value.trim(); if (!name || !formula) return;
    S.sheet.columns.push({ name, formula, core: $('ncCore').value }); refreshAllCores(); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataCols();
  };
  $('nmAdd').onclick = () => {
    const name = $('nmName').value.trim(), c = S.cores.find((x) => x.id === $('nmCore').value); if (!name || !c) return;
    c.user ||= {}; c.user[name] ||= { d: (c.depth || []).slice(), v: (c.depth || []).map(() => null) };
    refreshCoreData(c); markDirty(); $('dView').value = 'table'; $('dCore').value = c.id; renderData();
  };
}
$('dBody').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.colrow[data-i]'); if (!row) return;
  const col = S.sheet.columns[+row.dataset.i]; col[e.target.dataset.k] = e.target.value; refreshAllCores(); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataCols();
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const d = e.target.closest('button')?.dataset; if (!d) return;
  if ((d.del != null || d.delman) && !armed(e.target, '?')) return;
  if (d.del != null) { S.sheet.columns.splice(+d.del, 1); refreshAllCores(); geoRT.dirty = true; markDirty(); renderDataCols(); }
  else if (d.delman) { const [id, k] = d.delman.split('|'), c = S.cores.find((x) => x.id === id); if (c) { delete c.user[k]; refreshCoreData(c); markDirty(); renderDataCols(); } }
});
const CELL_COLS = 10, CELL_ROWS = 40;
function cellResults() {
  return evalCells(S.sheet.cells, (name, coreName) => {
    const c = coreName ? S.cores.find((x) => x.name === coreName || x.id === coreName) : curCore();
    if (!c) throw Object.assign(new Error('noCore'), { key: 'noCore', args: { v: coreName } });
    if (name === 'depth' || name === 'age') return c[name] || [];
    if (!c.proxies[name]) throw Object.assign(new Error('noCol'), { key: 'noCol', args: { v: name } });
    return c.proxies[name];
  });
}
function renderDataCells() {
  const R = cellResults(), show = (v) => (v && v.error ? '#ERR' : typeof v === 'number' ? fmt(v, 6) : v ?? '');
  $('dInfo').textContent = t('data.cellsInfo');
  let h = `<table class="dtable cells"><thead><tr><th></th>${Array.from({ length: CELL_COLS }, (_, c) => `<th>${cellName(c, 0).replace(/\d+/, '')}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>`;
  for (let r = 0; r < CELL_ROWS; r++) {
    h += `<tr><th>${r + 1}</th>`;
    for (let c = 0; c < CELL_COLS; c++) { const ref = cellName(c, r), raw = S.sheet.cells[ref] ?? '', v = R[ref]; h += `<td class="${v && v.error ? 'err' : ''}" title="${esc(v && v.error ? fxText(v.err || v) : raw)}"><input data-ref="${ref}" data-raw="${esc(raw)}" value="${esc(String(raw).startsWith('=') ? show(v) : raw)}"></td>`; }
    h += '</tr>';
  }
  $('dBody').innerHTML = h + `</tbody></table><p class="note">${t('data.cellsHelp')}</p>`;
}
$('dBody').addEventListener('focusin', (e) => { const i = e.target.closest('input[data-ref]'); if (i) i.value = i.dataset.raw; });
$('dBody').addEventListener('focusout', (e) => {
  const i = e.target.closest('input[data-ref]'); if (!i) return;
  const v = i.value.trim(); if (v) S.sheet.cells[i.dataset.ref] = v; else delete S.sheet.cells[i.dataset.ref];
  markDirty(); setTimeout(() => { if (!document.activeElement?.dataset?.ref) renderDataCells(); else { const a = document.activeElement.dataset.ref; renderDataCells(); const n = $('dBody').querySelector(`input[data-ref="${a}"]`); n?.focus(); } }, 0);
});
$('dBody').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target.dataset.ref) e.target.blur(); });
for (const id of ['dCore', 'dView', 'dLayers']) $(id).addEventListener('change', () => { dataLimit = 400; renderData(); });
$('dCsv').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const M = tableModel();
  const rows = [['core', 'depth', 'age', ...M.cols.map(([k]) => k), ...M.layers.map((L) => L.name)], ...M.rows.map((r) => [r.c.name, r.depth, r.age, ...r.vals, ...r.lay])];
  // V0.35: the cells edited by hand are listed in the header (lines starting with # are ignored when the file is read back)
  const notes = [];
  for (const c of M.cores) for (const [col, E] of Object.entries(c.edits || {})) for (const [x, ed] of Object.entries(E)) notes.push(`# edited by hand: ${csvCommentText(c.name)}; ${csvCommentText(col)}; ${c.depth ? 'depth' : 'age'} ${csvCommentText(x)}; original ${csvCommentText(ed.o ?? '')}; new ${csvCommentText(ed.v ?? '')}; ${csvCommentText(ed.at || '')}`);
  saveFile(`${safe(S.name)}_datos.csv`, notes.join('\n') + '\n' + withProv(toCSV(rows)), 'text/csv');
});


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/edit.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool web — V0.42: pure editing algorithms (no DOM, no global state), taken out of app.js so they can be tested
// with synthetic masks. Labels: Int32Array, > 0 one object id, 0 matrix, < 0 stack −v in `stacks` (core.js).
import { codeFor, hasId, idsAt } from './core.js';

/**
 * Scissors: pieces of object `id` inside its bbox [x0, y0, x1, y1] once the pixels in `cut` (Set of indices) are
 * removed. Pieces are 4-connected; cut pixels then join a neighbouring piece. Returns {comp, bw, n, main} with comp[j]
 * = piece (1…n) of bbox pixel j (0 = not the object) and main = the largest piece (it keeps the id).
 */
export function splitPieces(L, w, stacks, bbox, id, cut) {
  const [x0, y0, x1, y1] = bbox, bw = x1 - x0 + 1, bh = y1 - y0 + 1, comp = new Int32Array(bw * bh);   // −1 cut, 0 outside, k ≥ 1 piece
  for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) { const i = y * w + x; if (hasId(L[i], id, stacks)) comp[(y - y0) * bw + x - x0] = cut.has(i) ? -1 : -2; }
  let n = 0; const size = [0];
  for (let j = 0; j < comp.length; j++) {
    if (comp[j] !== -2) continue;
    n++; size.push(0); const q = [j]; comp[j] = n;
    while (q.length) {
      const c = q.pop(), x = c % bw; size[n]++;
      for (const d of [x > 0 ? c - 1 : -1, x < bw - 1 ? c + 1 : -1, c - bw, c + bw]) if (d >= 0 && d < comp.length && comp[d] === -2) { comp[d] = n; q.push(d); }
    }
  }
  for (let changed = true; changed && n;) {   // cut pixels go to the neighbouring piece
    changed = false;
    for (let j = 0; j < comp.length; j++) {
      if (comp[j] !== -1) continue; const x = j % bw;
      for (const d of [x > 0 ? j - 1 : -1, x < bw - 1 ? j + 1 : -1, j - bw, j + bw]) if (d >= 0 && d < comp.length && comp[d] > 0) { comp[j] = comp[d]; changed = true; break; }
    }
  }
  return { comp, bw, n, size, main: n ? size.indexOf(Math.max(...size.slice(1))) : 0 };
}

/** Which object keeps its id and class when merging: the largest with a class given by hand, else the largest. */
export const mergeKeeper = (objs) => [...objs].sort((a, b) => (!!b.cls - !!a.cls) || b.area - a.area)[0];

/**
 * V0.30: groups of overlapping traces of the same ichnotaxon to be merged. objects: Map id → {bbox, cls}; ids: the
 * traces whose class changed. Returns arrays [id, …partners].
 */
export function sameTaxonGroups(L, w, stacks, objects, ids) {
  const groups = [], cls = (id) => objects.get(id)?.cls ?? null;
  for (const id of ids) {
    const o = objects.get(id); if (!o?.bbox) continue;
    const c = cls(id), partners = new Set(), [x0, y0, x1, y1] = o.bbox;
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
      const v = L[y * w + x]; if (v >= 0) continue;
      const st = stacks[-v]; if (!st.includes(id)) continue;
      for (const q of st) if (q !== id && cls(q) === c) partners.add(q);
    }
    if (partners.size) groups.push([id, ...partners]);
  }
  return groups;
}

/** Swap pixel values with the log. Undo walks backward, Redo forward, including repeated writes to one pixel. */
export function swapHistoryPixels(labels, exclude, lm, ex, stacks, index, forward = false) {
  let restored;
  for (let offset = 0; offset < lm.length; offset += 2) {
    const k = forward ? offset : lm.length - 2 - offset;
    const pixel = lm[k], previous = lm[k + 1], current = labels[pixel];
    lm[k + 1] = current < 0 ? stacks[-current] : current;
    if (!Array.isArray(previous)) {
      labels[pixel] = previous;
      continue;
    }
    restored ||= new Map();
    if (!restored.has(previous)) restored.set(previous, codeFor(previous, stacks, index));
    labels[pixel] = restored.get(previous);
  }
  for (let offset = 0; offset < ex.length; offset += 2) {
    const k = forward ? offset : ex.length - 2 - offset;
    const pixel = ex[k], previous = ex[k + 1];
    ex[k + 1] = exclude[pixel];
    exclude[pixel] = previous;
  }
}

/** Evict complete oldest actions, keeping the newest action intact even when it exceeds a limit.
 * @param {import('./state.js').UndoStep[]} steps
 */
export function trimEditHistory(steps, maxSteps = 30, maxPixelBytes = 2e8) {
  const pixelBytes = (step) => 8 * (step.lm.length + step.ex.length);
  let bytes = steps.reduce((total, step) => total + pixelBytes(step), 0);
  let first = 0;
  while (steps.length - first > maxSteps || bytes > maxPixelBytes) {
    const group = steps[first].group;
    let end = first + 1;
    while (group && steps[end]?.group === group) end++;
    if (end === steps.length) break;
    for (; first < end; first++) bytes -= pixelBytes(steps[first]);
  }
  steps.splice(0, first);
}

/** Ids present in a label array (for checks): every object id seen in any pixel, overlaps included. */
export function idsInLabels(L, stacks) { const out = new Set(); for (const v of L) if (v) for (const id of idsAt(v, stacks)) out.add(id); return out; }

/** A saved analysis region uses integer pixel edges within the working image.
 * @param {import('./core.js').Roi | null | undefined} roi
 * @param {number} width
 * @param {number} height
 */
export function validSavedRegion(roi, width, height) {
  if (!roi) return false;
  const { x0, y0, x1, y1 } = roi;
  return [x0, y0, x1, y1].every(Number.isSafeInteger)
    && 0 <= x0 && x0 < x1 && x1 <= width
    && 0 <= y0 && y0 < y1 && y1 <= height;
}

/** Check referenced codes directly in a structurally valid RLE mask, without expanding it.
 * @param {ArrayLike<number> | null | undefined} labels
 * @param {unknown} stacks
 * @param {readonly {id: number}[]} objects
 */
export function hasBrokenMaskRefs(labels, stacks, objects) {
  if (!labels) return false;
  const ids = new Set(objects.map((object) => object.id));
  const checkedStacks = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 2) {
    const code = labels[i];
    if (labels[i + 1] === 0 || code === 0) continue;
    if ((code | 0) !== code) return true;
    if (code > 0) {
      if (!ids.has(code)) return true;
    } else if (!checkedStacks.has(code)) {
      const members = Array.isArray(stacks) ? stacks[-code] : null;
      if (!Array.isArray(members) || !members.length) return true;
      for (const id of members) if ((id | 0) !== id || id <= 0 || !ids.has(id)) return true;
      checkedStacks.add(code);
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** v1/v2 project files → v3 structure: the images become one core; the geochemistry cores become further cores. */
export function upgradeProject(p) {
  if (p.version >= 3) return p;
  const geo = p.geo || {}, old = geo.cores || [], main = old.find((c) => c.id === 'main');
  const cores = [];
  if ((p.sections || []).length || (p.records || []).length || !old.length) {
    cores.push({ id: 'main', name: main?.name?.split(' · ')[0] || p.name || 'Sondeo', lat: main?.lat ?? null, lon: main?.lon ?? null, units: p.sections?.[0]?.units || 'm',
      imp: null, records: p.records || [], logView: p.logView, age: p.age || { text: '', units: 'ka' }, sections: p.sections || [] });
  }
  for (const c of old) if (c.id !== 'main') cores.push({ id: c.id, name: c.name, lat: c.lat, lon: c.lon, waterDepth: c.waterDepth, units: c.units || 'm', source: c.source,
    imp: { depth: c.depth, age: c.age, proxies: c.proxies }, records: [], age: { text: c.ageText || '', units: 'ka' }, sections: [] });
  const { cores: _o, ...g } = geo;
  return { ...p, version: 3, geo: g, cores, curCore: cores[0]?.id };
}

// SedCoreTool web — V0.44: tables written to files and records read from tables. DOM-free and tested
// (tests/app/exportio.test.mjs); app.js only gathers the data and saves the files.

/** Escape user text inside a single-line CSV comment, where normal field quoting is unavailable. */
export const csvCommentText = (value) => String(value).replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f,;|"\u2028\u2029]/gu,
  (c) => '\\u' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).padStart(4, '0'));

/** Provenance lines (start with #, ignored when read back) for an exported CSV. */
export function provenanceLines({ delivery, format, date, project, settings: st = {}, detection = '', age = 'none' }) {
  return [`# SedCoreTool ${delivery} (format ${format}); exported ${date}; project "${csvCommentText(String(project).replace(/"/g, "'"))}"`,
    `# settings: binCm=${csvCommentText(st.binCm)}; minValid=${csvCommentText(st.minValid)}%; includePred=${!!st.includePred}; predThr=${csvCommentText(st.predThr)}; countCandidates=${!!st.countCandidates}; detection=${csvCommentText(detection)}; age model=${csvCommentText(age)}`];
}
export const withProvenance = (csv, info) => [...provenanceLines(info), csv].join('\n');

/** Age-model description for the provenance line. */
export const ageModelText = (m, age = {}) => (m ? `${age.bacon ? `Bacon ${age.center || 'median'}` : 'table'}${m.min ? ' with range' : ''}, ${age.units || 'ka'}` : 'none');

/**
 * Bioturbation table: one row per interval and section. sections = [{name, units, bins}], counted = [[name, class
 * index]], range = age model with min/max, ageUnits = unit or null without a model.
 */
export function bioturbationTable(sections, { counted = [], range = false, ageUnits = null } = {}) {
  const rows = [['section', 'depth_top', 'depth_bottom', 'depth', 'units', 'age', ...(range ? ['age_min', 'age_max'] : []), 'age_units', 'valid_pixels', 'bioturbation_pct', ...counted.map(([n]) => n + '_pct'), 'BI', 'ii', 'BI_manual', 'ii_manual']];
  for (const s of sections) for (const b of s.bins || []) rows.push([s.name, b.dTop, b.dBottom, b.depth, s.units, b.age ?? null, ...(range ? [b.ageMin ?? null, b.ageMax ?? null] : []), ageUnits, b.valid, b.pct, ...counted.map(([, k]) => b.classPct?.[k] ?? null), b.BI ?? null, b.ii ?? null, b.BIm ?? null, b.iim ?? null]);
  return rows;
}

/**
 * Object table: one row per trace with a bounding box. Each item: {section, units, o (object), cls, status, pred,
 * counted, depth(row) → depth}; o.feat holds the descriptors.
 */
export function objectsTable(items) {
  const rows = [['section', 'object_id', 'class', 'status', 'suggested_class', 'confidence', 'depth_top', 'depth_bottom', 'depth_center', 'units', 'area_cm2', 'length_cm', 'width_cm', 'elongation', 'verticality', 'compactness', 'mean_luminance', 'contrast', 'counted']];
  for (const it of items) {
    const o = it.o; if (!o.bbox) continue; const f = o.feat || {};
    rows.push([it.section, o.id, it.cls ?? '', it.status, it.pred ?? '', o.cls ? 1 : o.conf ?? null, it.depth(o.bbox[1]), it.depth(o.bbox[3] + 1), f.cy != null ? it.depth(f.cy + 0.5) : null, it.units,
      f.areaCm2 ?? null, f.majorCm ?? null, f.minorCm ?? null, f.elong ?? null, f.vert ?? null, f.compact ?? null, f.meanL ?? null, f.contrast ?? null, it.counted ? 1 : 0]);
  }
  return rows;
}

/**
 * Series of a record from a parsed table (log.js parseTable): x from column xCol, one series per column in yCols;
 * rows without x or y are left out and points are sorted by x. Series without points are dropped.
 */
export function recordSeries(tab, xCol, yCols, colors, start = 0) {
  return yCols.map((c, k) => {
    const pts = tab.rows.map((r) => [r[xCol], r[c]]).filter((p) => p[0] != null && p[1] != null && Number.isFinite(p[0]) && Number.isFinite(p[1])).sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
    return { name: tab.headers[c], x: pts.map((p) => p[0]), y: pts.map((p) => p[1]), color: colors[(start + k) % colors.length], show: true, reverse: false };
  }).filter((s) => s.x.length);
}

}

    markDirty(); renderFigPanel(); scheduleFigure();

});

/** Crop of one trace (padding 15 %), scaled to `size` px on its long side, with optional outline in the class colour. */
/**
 * Crop of one trace. V0.7: img 'orig' | 'enh' | 'none' (white background); iso = only the trace pixels on white;
 * fillMask = trace filled with its class colour; outline = edge in the class colour.
 */
export function traceCanvas(s, o, size, img, outline, iso = false, fillMask = false) {
  if (!s.orig) {   // V0.1: placeholder until the section's picture is read from disk
    needPixels(s); const p = document.createElement('canvas'); p.width = p.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(size * 0.5));
    const g = p.getContext('2d'); g.fillStyle = '#cfd4d2'; g.fillRect(0, 0, p.width, p.height);
    return { canvas: p, pxPerCm: pxPerCm(s), depth: depthAtRow(s, (o.bbox[1] + o.bbox[3]) / 2), units: s.units };
  }
  const [x0, y0, x1, y1] = o.bbox, pad = Math.round(Math.max(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) * 0.15) + 2;
  const X0 = Math.max(0, x0 - pad), Y0 = Math.max(0, y0 - pad), X1 = Math.min(s.w - 1, x1 + pad), Y1 = Math.min(s.h - 1, y1 + pad);
  const cw = X1 - X0 + 1, ch = Y1 - Y0 + 1, src = img === 'orig' ? s.orig : s.enh || s.orig, lab = s.labels;
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = cw; c.height = ch;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d'), im = ctx.createImageData(cw, ch), d = im.data, col = hex(clsById(o.cls || o.pred)?.color || '#ffffff');
  for (let y = 0; y < ch; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cw; x++) {
    const i = (Y0 + y) * s.w + X0 + x, j = (y * cw + x) * 4;
    const inT = hasId(lab[i], o.id, s.stacks);
    if (img === 'none' || (iso && !inT)) { d[j] = d[j + 1] = d[j + 2] = 255; } else { d[j] = src[i * 4]; d[j + 1] = src[i * 4 + 1]; d[j + 2] = src[i * 4 + 2]; }
    d[j + 3] = 255;
    if (fillMask && inT) { d[j] = col[0]; d[j + 1] = col[1]; d[j + 2] = col[2]; }
    if (outline && inT) {
      const X = X0 + x, Y = Y0 + y;
      const edge = X === 0 || Y === 0 || X === s.w - 1 || Y === s.h - 1 || !hasId(lab[i - 1], o.id, s.stacks) || !hasId(lab[i + 1], o.id, s.stacks) || !hasId(lab[i - s.w], o.id, s.stacks) || !hasId(lab[i + s.w], o.id, s.stacks);
      if (edge) { d[j] = col[0]; d[j + 1] = col[1]; d[j + 2] = col[2]; }
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(im, 0, 0);
  const k = size / Math.max(cw, ch), out = document.createElement('canvas');
  out.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(cw * k)); out.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(ch * k));
  const o2 = out.getContext('2d'); o2.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high'; o2.drawImage(c, 0, 0, out.width, out.height);
  return { canvas: out, pxPerCm: pxPerCm(s) * k, depth: depthAtRow(s, (y0 + y1) / 2), units: s.units };
}

/** Traces of the active core assigned to a class (sections of this core). */
function classTraces(clsId) {
  const out = [];
  const F = S.fig, win = F.secFrom != null || F.secTo != null, base = [...S.sections].sort((a, b) => a.top * UNIT_TO_CM[a.units] - b.top * UNIT_TO_CM[b.units])[0]?.units || 'cm';
  const inWin = (s, o) => { if (!win) return true; const d = depthAtRow(s, (o.bbox[1] + o.bbox[3] + 1) / 2) * UNIT_TO_CM[s.units] / UNIT_TO_CM[base]; return (F.secFrom == null || d >= F.secFrom) && (F.secTo == null || d < F.secTo); };
  for (const s of S.sections) for (const o of s.objects.values()) if (o.cls === clsId && o.bbox && o.area && inWin(s, o)) out.push({ s, o, key: `${s.id}:${o.id}` });
  return out;
}
/** Automatic choice: largest, most typical (closest to the class median of the shape/colour descriptors) or spread in depth. */
function autoPick(list, n, how) {
  if (how === 'typical' && list.length) {
    const V = list.map((x) => vec(x.o.feat)), d = V[0].length;
    const med = Array.from({ length: d }, (_, j) => { const a = V.map((v) => v[j]).sort((p, q) => p - q); return a[Math.floor(a.length / 2)]; });
    const sd = Array.from({ length: d }, (_, j) => { const m = V.reduce((s2, v) => s2 + v[j], 0) / V.length; return Math.sqrt(V.reduce((s2, v) => s2 + (v[j] - m) ** 2, 0) / V.length) || 1; });
    return list.map((x, i) => [x, V[i].reduce((s2, v, j) => s2 + ((v - med[j]) / sd[j]) ** 2, 0)]).sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]).slice(0, n).map((q) => q[0].key);
  }
  if (how === 'spread' && list.length) {
    const byDepth = [...list].sort((a, b) => depthAtRow(a.s, a.o.bbox[1]) - depthAtRow(b.s, b.o.bbox[1]));
    return Array.from({ length: Math.min(n, byDepth.length) }, (_, k) => byDepth[Math.round((k * (byDepth.length - 1)) / Math.max(1, n - 1))].key).filter((v, i, a) => a.indexOf(v) === i);
  }
  return [...list].sort((a, b) => b.o.area - a.o.area).slice(0, n).map((x) => x.key);
}
const niceBar = (pxPerCm, maxPx) => [0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 5, 10].filter((v) => v * pxPerCm <= maxPx).pop() || 0.05;

/** V0.7: versions of each trace shown side by side in the traces figure. */
const TRACE_VIEWS = { enh: ['enh', false, false, false], enhOut: ['enh', true, false, false], orig: ['orig', false, false, false], origOut: ['orig', true, false, false],
  iso: ['orig', false, true, false], isoEnh: ['enh', false, true, false], isoOut: ['orig', true, true, false], mask: ['none', false, false, true], outline: ['none', true, false, false] };
const traceViews = (F) => (F.tviews?.length ? F.tviews : [F.img === 'orig' ? (F.outline ? 'origOut' : 'orig') : F.outline ? 'enhOut' : 'enh']).filter((v) => TRACE_VIEWS[v]);
/** Scale bar of a tile: 'bar' (filled), 'ticks' (alternating segments), 'line' (line with end ticks) or 'none'. */
function traceScale(X, y, T, pxPerCm, sc) {
  const style = sc.style || 'bar'; if (style === 'none') return '';
  const col = sc.color || '#ffffff', ink = sc.ink || '#000000', len = sc.len > 0 ? +sc.len : niceBar(pxPerCm, T * 0.45), bw = len * pxPerCm;
  const pos = sc.pos || 'bl', bx = pos.endsWith('r') ? X + T - 6 - bw : X + 6, by = pos.startsWith('t') ? y + 22 : y + T - 12;
  const txt = `<text x="${(pos.endsWith('r') ? bx + bw : bx).toFixed(1)}" y="${(by - 3).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="${pos.endsWith('r') ? 'end' : 'start'}" fill="${col}" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="0.25" font-size="10" font-weight="600">${len < 1 ? +(len * 10).toFixed(2) + ' mm' : +len.toFixed(2) + ' cm'}</text>`;
  if (style === 'line') return `<path d="M${bx.toFixed(1)} ${by - 2}v6M${bx.toFixed(1)} ${by + 1}h${bw.toFixed(1)}M${(bx + bw).toFixed(1)} ${by - 2}v6" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="2.6" fill="none"/><path d="M${bx.toFixed(1)} ${by - 2}v6M${bx.toFixed(1)} ${by + 1}h${bw.toFixed(1)}M${(bx + bw).toFixed(1)} ${by - 2}v6" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="1.2" fill="none"/>${txt}`;
  if (style === 'ticks') {
    const n = Math.max(2, Math.min(10, Math.round(sc.div) || 4)), seg = bw / n;
    return `<rect x="${bx.toFixed(1)}" y="${by}" width="${bw.toFixed(1)}" height="4" fill="${col}" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="0.6"/>${Array.from({ length: n }, (_, k) => (k % 2 ? '' : `<rect x="${(bx + k * seg).toFixed(1)}" y="${by}" width="${seg.toFixed(1)}" height="4" fill="${ink}"/>`)).join('')}${txt}`;
  }
  return `<rect x="${bx.toFixed(1)}" y="${by}" width="${bw.toFixed(1)}" height="4" fill="${col}" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="0.6"/>${txt}`;
}
/** V0.59: text and background colours of the figures built here, from the core-column style (validated hex colours). */
function figInk() { const st = mergedStyle(colView('figure').style); return { text: st.textColor, sub: st.subColor, bg: st.bgColor }; }
function tracesSVG() {
  const INK = figInk();
  const F = S.fig, T = F.tile, gap = 12, vgap = 4, labW = 190, rows = [], views = traceViews(F), sc = F.scale || {};
  for (const c of S.classes) {
    if (!c.counts && c.id !== 'undet') continue;
    const list = classTraces(c.id), keys = F.picks[c.id]?.length ? F.picks[c.id] : [];
    const chosen = keys.map((k) => list.find((x) => x.key === k)).filter(Boolean);
    if (chosen.length) rows.push({ c, chosen });
  }
  if (!rows.length) return null;
  const nv = views.length, TW = nv * T + (nv - 1) * vgap, head = nv > 1 ? 16 : 0;
  const maxN = Math.max(...rows.map((r) => r.chosen.length)), W = labW + maxN * (TW + gap) + 10, rowH = T + 34, H = head + rows.length * rowH + 10;
  const parts = [`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" font-family="IBM Plex Sans, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="11"><rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="${INK.bg}"/>`];
  if (nv > 1) for (let k = 0; k < maxN; k++) views.forEach((v, vi) => parts.push(`<text x="${labW + k * (TW + gap) + vi * (T + vgap) + T / 2}" y="12" text-anchor="middle" fill="${INK.sub}" font-size="10">${esc(t('fig.tv.' + v))}</text>`));
  rows.forEach((r, ri) => {
    const y = head + 6 + ri * rowH;
    parts.push(`<rect x="8" y="${y + 4}" width="10" height="10" fill="${r.c.color}"/><text x="24" y="${y + 13}" font-size="13" font-style="${r.c.plain ? 'normal' : 'italic'}" font-weight="600" fill="${INK.text}">${esc(r.c.name)}</text>`);
    parts.push(`<text x="24" y="${y + 29}" fill="${INK.sub}">${t('fig.nOf', { n: r.chosen.length, m: classTraces(r.c.id).length })}</text>`);
    r.chosen.forEach((x, k) => {
      let tc0 = null;
      views.forEach((v, vi) => {
        const [img, out, iso, mask] = TRACE_VIEWS[v], tc = traceCanvas(x.s, x.o, T, img, out, iso, mask), X = labW + k * (TW + gap) + vi * (T + vgap);
        const ox = X + (T - tc.canvas.width) / 2, oy = y + (T - tc.canvas.height) / 2; tc0 ||= tc;
        parts.push(`<rect x="${X}" y="${y}" width="${T}" height="${T}" fill="${img === 'none' || iso ? '#ffffff' : '#f0f0ee'}"${img === 'none' || iso ? ' stroke="#d9dedc" stroke-width="0.6"' : ''}/><image x="${ox.toFixed(1)}" y="${oy.toFixed(1)}" width="${tc.canvas.width}" height="${tc.canvas.height}" href="${tc.canvas.toDataURL(mask || img === 'none' ? 'image/png' : 'image/jpeg', 0.92)}"/>`);
        if (vi === 0 || sc.all) parts.push(traceScale(X, y, T, tc.pxPerCm, { ...sc, ...((img === 'none' || iso) && (sc.color || '#ffffff').toLowerCase() === '#ffffff' ? { color: sc.ink || '#000000', ink: '#ffffff' } : {}) }));
      });
      parts.push(`<text x="${labW + k * (TW + gap) + TW / 2}" y="${y + T + 13}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${INK.sub}" font-size="10">${esc(x.s.name)} · ${fmt(tc0.depth, 3)} ${tc0.units}</text>`);
    });
  });
  parts.push('</svg>');
  return parts.join('');
}

/** Whole interpreted core: image with traces, bioturbation, ichnotaxa, indices, records and interval bands. */
function coreSVG() {
  if (!S.sections.length && !S.records.length) return null;
  const lv = colView('figure'), D = compositeData(lv.axis), [from, to] = figAxisWin(D), F = S.fig;
  const { W: imgW, H } = coreFigSize(D);
  const classes = S.classes.map((c, i) => ({ name: c.name, color: c.color, symbol: c.symbol || 'triDown', index: i, counts: c.counts })).filter((c) => c.counts);
  const images = S.sections.length && lv.tracks.image ? coreImages(D, from, to, H, { view: F.views?.core ? lv : undefined, axisPanels: !!F.views?.core, panels: lv.imgPanels, mode: lv.imgMode, hideExcl: lv.hideExcl, w: imgW }) : null, image = images?.[0] || null;   // V0.25
  const boundaries = D.secs.map((x, i) => { const ax = (v) => (D.axis === 'age' ? interpAge(D.model, v * D.f(x)) : v * D.f(x)); return { a: ax(x.top), a1: ax(x.bottom), label: x.name, n: i + 1 }; }).filter((b) => b.a != null);
  const LS = logStyleOf(D, lv), marks = lv.tracks.marks !== false ? compositeMarks(D) : [];
  const taxa = F.taxaStyles?.core ?? lv.style?.taxa;
  const res = buildLogSVG({
    style: LS.style, colWidths: lv.colW || {}, respectTaxonColumnVisibility: !!F.views?.core, title: curCore()?.name || S.name, subtitle: t('fig.coreSub', { n: D.secs.length }), axisLabel: LS.label,
    from, to, height: H, rows: D.rows, classes, tracks: { ...lv.tracks, image: !!image }, image, images, imageWidth: imgW, records: logRecords(D, lv, S.records, lv.records), boundaries, ...geoOverlay(D.axis),
    taxa, taxonPoints: taxa ? compositeTaxonPoints(D) : [],
    taxonClasses: S.classes.map((c, index) => ({ ...c, index })).filter((c) => c.id !== 'artifact'),
    marks, idList: idMode() || marks.length ? idListOf(D.rows, marks) : null, legendClasses: legendOf(classes.filter((c) => D.rows.some((r) => r.classPct?.[c.index] > 0)), marks),
    labels: { secs: t('log.secs'), noData: t('log.noData'), total: t('log.total'), species: t('log.species'), image: t('log.image'), marks: t('id.marks'), idList: t('id.list') },
  });
  syncColOrder('figure', res.columns);   // V0.33
  return res.svg;
}

/* ── V32: core-section figure (versions of the same interval side by side) and ichnotaxa range chart ── */
/** Section picture in one of the figure styles, reduced to ≤ 900 px wide and cached per section. */
function sectionModeCanvas(s, mode, hx = S.fig.hideExcl, opacity = S.fig.alpha) {   // V0.25: hx = paint excluded zones white (per figure)
  opacity = Number.isFinite(opacity) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, opacity)) : S.fig.alpha;
  const key = `${s.logVer}|${mode}|${opacity}|${!!hx}|${S.settings.predThr}|${S.settings.includePred}`, mk = mode + (hx ? '|x' : '');
  s._fc ||= new Map(); if (s._fc.get(mk)?.key === key) return s._fc.get(mk);
  if (!s.orig && !['mask', 'bw'].includes(mode)) { needPixels(s); const p = document.createElement('canvas'); p.width = 1; p.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(s.h / s.w)); const g = p.getContext('2d'); g.fillStyle = '#e4e7e6'; g.fillRect(0, 0, 1, p.height); return { key: 'pending', canvas: p, k: 1 / s.w }; }
  // V0.41: each pixel's colour is computed and averaged straight into the ≤ 900 px picture (no full-size canvas)
  const lab = s.labels, ex = s.exclude, W = s.w;
  const eff = effClassArray(s), cols = S.classes.map((k) => hex(k.color)), photo = !['mask', 'bw'].includes(mode), src = mode === 'orig' ? s.orig : s.enh;
  const k = Math.min(1, 900 / s.w), out = document.createElement('canvas'); out.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(s.w * k)); out.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(s.h * k));
  const px = [0, 0, 0, 255];
  const im = reduceRGBA(s.w, s.h, out.width, out.height, (i) => {
    const j = i * 4;
    if (photo) { px[0] = src[j]; px[1] = src[j + 1]; px[2] = src[j + 2]; } else px[0] = px[1] = px[2] = 255;
    if (ex[i] && (hx || !photo)) { px[0] = px[1] = px[2] = photo ? 255 : 236; return px; }
    const id = topAt(lab[i], s.stacks); if (!id) return px; const q = eff[id]; if (q < 0) return px; const col = cols[q];
    if (mode === 'fill' || mode === 'mask') { px[0] = col[0]; px[1] = col[1]; px[2] = col[2]; }
    else if (mode === 'bw') { px[0] = px[1] = px[2] = 20; }
    else if (mode === 'alpha') { const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, +opacity || 0)); px[0] += (col[0] - px[0]) * a; px[1] += (col[1] - px[1]) * a; px[2] += (col[2] - px[2]) * a; }
    else if (mode === 'outline') { const x = i % W; if (x === 0 || x === W - 1 || !hasId(lab[i - 1], id, s.stacks) || !hasId(lab[i + 1], id, s.stacks) || !hasId(lab[i - W], id, s.stacks) || !hasId(lab[i + W], id, s.stacks)) { px[0] = col[0]; px[1] = col[1]; px[2] = col[2]; } }
    return px;
  });
  out.getContext('2d').putImageData(im, 0, 0);
  const r = { key, canvas: out, k: out.width / s.w }; s._fc.set(mk, r); return r;
}
/** One panel image for depths [from, to) (base units), H × W px. */
export function panelDataURL(D, from, to, H, W, mode, hx = S.fig.hideExcl, opacity = S.fig.alpha) {
  const Hc = Math.min(6000, Math.round(H * 2)), Wc = W * 2, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = Wc; c.height = Hc;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, Wc, Hc);
  const inverse = D.axis === 'age' ? invertModel(D.model) : null;
  for (let r = 0; r < Hc; r++) {
    const coordinate = from + ((r + 0.5) / Hc) * (to - from), dd = D.axis === 'age' ? interpAge(inverse, coordinate) : coordinate;
    if (dd == null) continue;
    const s = D.secs.find((q) => dd >= q.top * D.f(q) && dd < q.bottom * D.f(q)); if (!s) continue;
    const row = Math.floor(rowAtDepth(s, dd / D.f(s))); if (row < s.roi.y0 || row >= s.roi.y1) continue;
    const pc = sectionModeCanvas(s, mode, hx, opacity), k = pc.k;
    ctx.drawImage(pc.canvas, s.roi.x0 * k, Math.min(pc.canvas.height - 1, Math.floor(row * k)), Math.max(1, (s.roi.x1 - s.roi.x0) * k), 1, 0, r, Wc, 1);
  }
  return ['mask', 'bw'].includes(mode) ? c.toDataURL('image/png') : c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.92);
}
/** Identified traces in [from, to): classified objects (centroid) of at least minArea px and the marks. */
export function figTraces(D, from, to) {
  const out = [], ab = clsAbbr(), F = S.fig;
  for (const s of D.secs) {
    const add = (x, y, cid) => {
      const c = clsById(cid); if (!c || c.id === 'artifact' || y < s.roi.y0 || y >= s.roi.y1) return;
      const depth = depthAtRow(s, y) * D.f(s), dd = D.axis === 'age' ? interpAge(D.model, depth) : depth; if (dd == null || dd < from || dd >= to) return;
      if ((F.arrowExcl || []).includes(c.id)) return;   // V0.23: excluded groups (e.g. undetermined)
      out.push({ d: dd, xf: (x - s.roi.x0) / Math.max(1, s.roi.x1 - s.roi.x0), color: c.color, abbr: ab.get(c.name), name: c.name, plain: !!c.plain, k: src === 'obj' ? +F.objScale || 1 : 1 });
    };
    let src = 'obj';
    // V0.23: 'all' = every trace and every mark, 'marks' = only the marks placed by hand, 'objects' = only traces
    if ((F.arrowSrc || 'all') !== 'marks') for (const o of s.objects.values()) { const cid = effClassId(o); if (!cid || !o.stats || o.area < F.minArea) continue; add(o.stats.sx / o.stats.n, o.stats.sy / o.stats.n, cid); }
    src = 'mark';
    if ((F.arrowSrc || 'all') !== 'objects') for (const m of s.marks || []) add(m.x, m.y, m.cls);
  }
  return out;
}
/** V0.2: sections and units of the core without analysing them (for sizes in the figure forms). */
function figLightD() {
  const secs = [...S.sections].sort((a, b) => a.top * UNIT_TO_CM[a.units] - b.top * UNIT_TO_CM[b.units]), base = secs[0]?.units || 'cm';
  return { secs, base, f: (q) => UNIT_TO_CM[q.units] / UNIT_TO_CM[base], axis: 'depth', get rows() { return compositeData().rows; } };
}
/** V0.2: depth window shared by all figures (base units of the core; empty fields = whole core). */
function secFigRange(D) {
  const view = colView('figure');
  let dMin = Math.min(...D.secs.map((s) => s.top * D.f(s))), dMax = Math.max(...D.secs.map((s) => s.bottom * D.f(s)));
  if (!D.secs.length) { const r = D.axis === 'depth' ? rowsRange(D.rows) : [0, 1]; [dMin, dMax] = r; }
  let from = view.depthFrom ?? dMin, to = view.depthTo ?? dMax;
  if (!(to > from)) { from = dMin; to = dMax; }
  return { from, to };
}
/** The depth window on the axis of the figure (age if the column is shown in age). */
function figAxisWin(D) {
  const view = colView('figure');
  if (view.depthFrom == null && view.depthTo == null) return rowsRange(D.rows);
  const { from, to } = secFigRange(D);
  if (D.axis !== 'age') return [from, to];
  const a = interpAge(D.model, from), b = interpAge(D.model, to);
  return a == null || b == null ? rowsRange(D.rows) : [Math.min(a, b), Math.max(a, b)];
}
/** Width/height ratio of the core in the window: (window length in cm) / (core width in cm, median of the sections). */
export function coreRatio(D, from, to) {
  const ws = D.secs.filter((s) => s.bottom * D.f(s) > from && s.top * D.f(s) < to).map((s) => (s.roi.x1 - s.roi.x0) / pxPerCm(s)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const wcm = ws.length ? ws[ws.length >> 1] : 0, hcm = (to - from) * UNIT_TO_CM[D.base];
  return wcm > 0 && hcm > 0 ? hcm / wcm : null;
}
/**
 * V0.2: panel size of the section figure. With «keep proportion» the height follows the width so the core keeps its real
 * shape: H = W · (window length in cm) / (core width in cm, median of the analysis areas of the sections in the window).
 * Above 4000 px the height is capped and the width reduced instead.
 */
function secFigSize(D) {
  const F = S.fig; let W = F.panelW, H = F.panelH, r = null;
  if (colView('figure').keepRatio && D.secs.length) {
    const { from, to } = secFigRange(D);
    r = coreRatio(D, from, to);
    if (r) {
      H = Math.max(50, Math.round(W * r));
      if (H > 4000) { H = 4000; W = Math.max(10, Math.round(H / r)); }
    }
  }
  return { W, H, r };
}
/** Show in the form the panel size actually used (with «keep proportion» one side follows the other). */
function syncSecFigSize() {
  const z = secFigSize(figLightD()), F = S.fig;
  if (colView('figure').keepRatio && z.r) { F.panelW = z.W; F.panelH = z.H; /** @type {HTMLInputElement} */ ($('figPanelW')).value = String(z.W); /** @type {HTMLInputElement} */ ($('figPanelH')).value = String(z.H); $('figRatioNote').textContent = t('fig.ratioNote', { r: fmt(z.r, 3) }); }
  else $('figRatioNote').textContent = '';
}
/** Size of the core figure: image width and column height (with «keep proportion», height = width × ratio, max. 6000 px). */
function coreFigSize(D) {
  const F = S.fig; let W = F.coreImgW || 70, H = F.coreH, r = null;
  if (colView('figure').keepRatio && D.secs.length) {
    const { from, to } = secFigRange(D); r = coreRatio(D, from, to);
    if (r) { H = Math.max(200, Math.round(W * r)); if (H > 6000) { H = 6000; W = Math.max(10, Math.round(H / r)); } }
  }
  return { W, H, r };
}
/**
 * V0.5: marker of an identified trace at (px, py) in the trace colour: 'arrow' (from the upper right), 'triDown'
 * (inverted triangle above the trace), 'tri', 'circle', 'square', 'star', 'cross'. legend = small version centred at (px, py).
 */
function figSymbol(kind, px, py, color, xMax = 1e9, legend = false, k = 1) {
  const F = S.fig, kk = legend ? 1 : k * (+F.markScale || 1);
  if (kk !== 1) return `<g transform="translate(${px.toFixed(1)} ${py.toFixed(1)}) scale(${+kk.toFixed(3)}) translate(${(-px).toFixed(1)} ${(-py).toFixed(1)})">${figSymbol(kind, px, py, color, px + (xMax - px) / kk, legend, 1 / (+F.markScale || 1))}</g>`;
  const f = (v) => v.toFixed(1), c = color, halo = F.markStroke === 'none' ? 'stroke="none"' : `stroke="${F.markStroke || '#fff'}" stroke-width="${F.markStrokeW ?? 0.8}"`;   // V0.23: border of the marker
  if (kind === 'arrow') {
    if (legend) return `<path d="M${f(px + 5)} ${f(py - 6)}L${f(px - 3)} ${f(py + 1)}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M${f(px - 4)} ${f(py + 2)}l5 -1l-2.6 -3z" fill="${c}"/>`;
    const lx = Math.min(xMax, px + 20);
    return `<path d="M${f(lx - 2)} ${f(py - 9)}L${f(px + 3)} ${f(py - 1.5)}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M${f(lx - 2)} ${f(py - 9)}L${f(px + 3)} ${f(py - 1.5)}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.4"/><path d="M${f(px)} ${f(py)}l6.5 -1.2l-3.4 -4z" fill="${c}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="0.5"/>`;
  }
  const s = legend ? 4.5 : 5.5, cy = legend ? py - 4 : kind === 'triDown' ? py - s - 3 : py;
  if (kind === 'triDown') return `<path d="M${f(px - s)} ${f(cy - s)}H${f(px + s)}L${f(px)} ${f(cy + s)}z" fill="${c}" ${halo}/>`;
  if (kind === 'tri') return `<path d="M${f(px - s)} ${f(cy + s)}H${f(px + s)}L${f(px)} ${f(cy - s)}z" fill="${c}" ${halo}/>`;
  if (kind === 'square') return `<rect x="${f(px - s * 0.8)}" y="${f(cy - s * 0.8)}" width="${f(s * 1.6)}" height="${f(s * 1.6)}" fill="${c}" ${halo}/>`;
  if (kind === 'cross') return `<path d="M${f(px - s)} ${f(cy - s)}L${f(px + s)} ${f(cy + s)}M${f(px + s)} ${f(cy - s)}L${f(px - s)} ${f(cy + s)}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3.4"/><path d="M${f(px - s)} ${f(cy - s)}L${f(px + s)} ${f(cy + s)}M${f(px + s)} ${f(cy - s)}L${f(px - s)} ${f(cy + s)}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="1.8"/>`;
  if (kind === 'star') { const p = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => { const r = i % 2 ? s * 0.45 : s * 1.1, a = (Math.PI / 5) * i - Math.PI / 2; return `${f(px + r * Math.cos(a))} ${f(cy + r * Math.sin(a))}`; }); return `<path d="M${p.join('L')}z" fill="${c}" ${halo}/>`; }
  return `<circle cx="${f(px)}" cy="${f(cy)}" r="${f(s * 0.8)}" fill="${c}" ${halo}/>`;
}
/** V0.5–V0.23: markers (and abbreviations) of identified traces over an image panel at x X, width W; y(depth) → px. */
export function arrowMarksSVG(tr, X, W, y) {
  const F = S.fig, out = [], ms = F.markStyle || 'arrowLabel', ov = F.overlap || 'keep', labels = ms === 'arrowLabel';
  const pts = tr.map((q) => ({ q, px: X + Math.min(1, Math.max(0, q.xf)) * W, py: y(q.d) })).sort((a, b) => a.py - b.py);
  for (const t0 of pts) out.push(figSymbol(ms === 'arrowLabel' ? 'arrow' : ms, t0.px, t0.py, t0.q.color, X + W - 4, false, t0.q.k || 1));
  if (labels) {
    const fs = +F.labelSize || 10, kk = +F.markScale || 1;
    const lab = pts.map((t0) => ({ ...t0, lx: Math.min(X + W - 4, t0.px + 20 * kk * (t0.q.k || 1)), ly: t0.py - 10 * kk * (t0.q.k || 1), txt: t0.q.abbr }));
    const out2 = [];
    for (const l of lab) {
      const prev = out2[out2.length - 1], near = prev && l.ly - prev.ly < fs + 1;
      if (!near || ov === 'keep') out2.push(l);
      else if (ov === 'shift') { l.ly = prev.ly + fs + 1; out2.push(l); }
      else if (ov === 'merge') { if (!prev.txt.split(', ').includes(l.txt)) prev.txt += ', ' + l.txt; }
      /* 'hide': the arrow stays, its label is left out */
    }
    const halo = F.labelHalo === false ? '' : ' stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.6" paint-order="stroke"';
    for (const l of out2) out.push(`<text x="${l.lx}" y="${l.ly.toFixed(1)}" font-weight="700" font-size="${fs}" fill="${F.labelColor || '#1c2422'}"${halo}>${esc(l.txt)}</text>`);
  }
  return out.join('');
}
function sectionsSVG() {
  const INK = figInk();
  if (!S.sections.length) return null;
  const F = S.fig, D0 = compositeData(), D = { ...D0, axis: 'depth' };
  const { from, to } = secFigRange(D);
  const panels = FIG_PANELS.filter((p) => F.panels.includes(p)); if (!panels.length) return null;
  const { W, H } = secFigSize(D), gap = 16, x0 = 62, top = 44, tr = figTraces(D, from, to);
  const legend = S.classes.filter((c) => c.id !== 'artifact' && (tr.some((q) => q.name === c.name) || S.sections.some((s) => [...s.objects.values()].some((o) => effClassId(o) === c.id))));
  const ab = clsAbbr(), perRow = Math.max(1, Math.floor((x0 + panels.length * (W + gap)) / 190)), legH = legend.length ? Math.ceil(legend.length / perRow) * 16 + 18 : 0;
  const Wt = Math.max(x0 + panels.length * (W + gap) + 8, 360), Ht = top + H + 30 + legH, y = (v) => +(top + ((v - from) / (to - from)) * H).toFixed(2);
  const out = [`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" width="${Wt}" height="${Ht}" viewBox="0 0 ${Wt} ${Ht}" font-family="IBM Plex Sans, Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif" font-size="10"><rect width="${Wt}" height="${Ht}" fill="${INK.bg}"/>`];
  out.push(`<text transform="translate(14,${top + H / 2}) rotate(-90)" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600" fill="${INK.text}">${esc(logStyleOf(D, colView('figure')).label)}</text>`);
  out.push(`<line x1="${x0 - 8}" y1="${top}" x2="${x0 - 8}" y2="${top + H}" stroke="${INK.text}" stroke-width="0.8"/>`);
  const LS = logStyleOf(D, colView('figure'));
  out.push(`<defs>${noDataDefs(LS.style)}</defs>`);   // V0.68: pattern used by the no-data rectangles
  for (const e of extraTicks(from, to, H, LS.style)) out.push(`<line x1="${x0 - 8 - e.len}" y1="${y(e.v)}" x2="${x0 - 8}" y2="${y(e.v)}" stroke="${INK.text}" stroke-width="0.6"/>`);   // V0.22
  const lblLen = +LS.style.yLabelLen > 0 ? +LS.style.yLabelLen : 5;
  for (const v of verticalTicks(from, to, H, LS.style)) out.push(`<line x1="${x0 - 8 - lblLen}" y1="${y(v)}" x2="${x0 - 8}" y2="${y(v)}" stroke="${INK.text}" stroke-width="0.8"/><text x="${x0 - 10 - lblLen}" y="${y(v) + 3}" text-anchor="end" fill="${INK.text}">${+(v * LS.style.yFactor).toPrecision(6)}</text>`);
  panels.forEach((p, i) => {
    const X = x0 + i * (W + gap), href = panelDataURL(D, from, to, H, W, p);
    out.push(`<text x="${X}" y="${top - 10}" font-weight="700" fill="${INK.text}">${String.fromCharCode(97 + i)}</text><text x="${X + 12}" y="${top - 10}" fill="${INK.text}">${esc(t('fig.p.' + p))}</text>`);
    out.push(`<image x="${X}" y="${top}" width="${W}" height="${H}" preserveAspectRatio="none" href="${href}" xlink:href="${href}"/><rect x="${X}" y="${top}" width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="none" stroke="${INK.sub}" stroke-width="0.6"/>`);
    if (p === 'arrows') out.push(arrowMarksSVG(tr, X, W, y));
    if (LS.style.ndImage) for (const g of noDataGaps(D.rows.filter((r) => r.a1 > from && r.a0 < to))) out.push(noDataRect(LS.style, X, W, y(Math.max(g.a0, from)), y(Math.min(g.a1, to)) - y(Math.max(g.a0, from)), t('log.noData')));   // V0.23
  });
  out.push(`<text x="${x0}" y="${top + H + 18}" fill="${INK.sub}">${esc(curCore()?.name || S.name)} · ${+from.toPrecision(6)}–${+to.toPrecision(6)} ${esc(D.base)}</text>`);
  legend.forEach((c, i) => {
    const lx = 8 + (i % perRow) * 190, ly = top + H + 30 + Math.floor(i / perRow) * 16;
    const symOnly = F.panels.includes('arrows') && (F.markStyle || 'arrowLabel') !== 'arrowLabel';
    out.push(`${symOnly ? figSymbol(F.markStyle, lx + 5, ly + 9, c.color, 1e9, true) : `<rect x="${lx}" y="${ly}" width="10" height="10" fill="${c.color}"/>`}<text x="${lx + 14}" y="${ly + 9}" fill="${INK.text}">${symOnly ? '' : `<tspan font-weight="700">${esc(ab.get(c.name))}</tspan> `}<tspan font-style="${c.plain ? 'normal' : 'italic'}">${esc(c.name)}</tspan></text>`);
  });
  out.push('</svg>');
  return out.join('');
}
function rangeSVG() {
  if (!S.sections.length) return null;
  const F = S.fig, D = compositeData(colView('figure').axis), [from, to] = figAxisWin(D);
  const classes = S.classes.map((c, i) => ({ name: c.name, color: c.color, symbol: c.symbol || 'triDown', index: i, plain: !!c.plain })).filter((c) => S.classes[c.index].id !== 'artifact');
  const points = compositeTaxonPoints(D, F.taxaStyles?.range ? 'all' : F.rangeSrc || 'all');
  const res = buildRangeSVG({ title: curCore()?.name || S.name, subtitle: F.taxaStyles?.range ? t('taxa.subtitle') : t({ bars: 'fig.rangeSub', both: 'fig.rangeSubBoth', kite: 'fig.rangeSubKite', kiteSymbols: 'fig.rangeSubKiteSym' }[F.rangeDisplay] || 'fig.rangeSubPts'), colW: F.rangeColW || 30, axisLabel: logStyleOf(D, colView('figure')).label, style: logStyleOf(D, colView('figure')).style,
    from, to, height: F.rangeH, rows: D.rows, classes, points, taxa: F.taxaStyles?.range, markHollow: (F.taxaStyles?.range?.source || F.rangeSrc) === 'all' && F.rangeHollow !== false, display: F.rangeDisplay || 'symbols', total: F.rangeTotal, totalLabel: t('log.total') });
  setTimeout(() => syncColOrder('figure', res.columns), 0);   // V0.33 (after the form exists)
  return res.svg;
}

/** Adds title and caption around any figure SVG. */
function wrapFigure(svg, title, caption) {
  const INK = figInk();
  const w = +(/width="([\d.]+)"/.exec(svg)?.[1] || 800), h = +(/height="([\d.]+)"/.exec(svg)?.[1] || 600);
  const lines = []; let cur = '';
  for (const word of String(caption || '').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)) { if ((cur + ' ' + word).length * 6.2 > w - 20) { lines.push(cur); cur = word; } else cur = cur ? cur + ' ' + word : word; }
  if (cur) lines.push(cur);
  const top = title ? 34 : 0, H = top + h + (lines.length ? lines.length * 15 + 16 : 0);
  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${w}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${H}" font-family="IBM Plex Sans, Arial, sans-serif"><rect width="${w}" height="${H}" fill="${INK.bg}"/>${title ? `<text x="10" y="23" font-size="16" font-weight="600" fill="${INK.text}">${esc(title)}</text>` : ''}${svg.replace('<svg ', `<svg x="0" y="${top}" `)}${lines.map((l, i) => `<text x="10" y="${top + h + 18 + i * 15}" font-size="12" fill="${INK.text}">${esc(l)}</text>`).join('')}</svg>`;
}

/** V0.2: depth window fields shared by all figures (empty = whole core). */
function figWinHTML() {
  const F = S.fig, view = colView('figure'), base = [...S.sections].sort((a, b) => a.top * UNIT_TO_CM[a.units] - b.top * UNIT_TO_CM[b.units])[0]?.units || S.cores.find((c) => c.id === S.curCore)?.units || 'm';
  return `<div class="grid2"><label class="f"><span>${t('fig.from')} (${esc(base)})</span><input type="number" id="figSecFrom" step="any" value="${esc(view.depthFrom ?? '')}" placeholder="${t('fig.auto2')}"></label>
    <label class="f"><span>${t('fig.to')} (${esc(base)})</span><input type="number" id="figSecTo" step="any" value="${esc(view.depthTo ?? '')}" placeholder="${t('fig.auto2')}"></label></div>`;
}
/** Show in the core-figure form the size actually used (with «keep proportion» the height follows the image width). */
function syncCoreFigSize() {
  if (!$('figCoreImgW')) return;
  const F = S.fig, z = coreFigSize(figLightD());
  if (colView('figure').keepRatio && z.r) { F.coreImgW = z.W; F.coreH = z.H; /** @type {HTMLInputElement} */ ($('figCoreImgW')).value = String(z.W); /** @type {HTMLInputElement} */ ($('figCoreH')).value = String(z.H); $('figRatioNote').textContent = t('fig.ratioNote', { r: fmt(z.r, 3) }); }
  else $('figRatioNote').textContent = '';
  /** @type {HTMLInputElement} */ ($('figCoreH')).disabled = !!(colView('figure').keepRatio && z.r);
}
export function verticalStyleHTML(st = S.logView.style || {}, nd = true) {
  return `${nd ? ndFormHTML(st) : ''}<div class="grid2"><label class="f"><span>${t('st.yUnit')}</span><select data-st="yUnit">${['', 'mm', 'cm', 'm'].map((u) => `<option value="${u}" ${(st.yUnit || '') === u ? 'selected' : ''}>${u || t('st.native')}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>${tickFieldsHTML(st)}</div>`;
}
/** V0.72: orientation of each figure type (F.orients); projects saved before keep their single F.orient as the fallback. */
const orientOf = (F) => F.orients?.[F.type] ?? F.orient ?? 'v';
function figureAxisHTML(view, core = curCore()) {
  const ageAvailable = !!core?.ageModel?.model || core?.age?.some(Number.isFinite);
  return `<label class="f"><span>${t('fig.axisFig')}</span><select data-fview="axis"><option value="depth" ${view.axis !== 'age' ? 'selected' : ''}>${t('log.depth')}</option><option value="age" ${view.axis === 'age' ? 'selected' : ''} ${ageAvailable ? '' : 'disabled'}>${t('log.age')}</option></select></label>`;
}
