  const y0 = Math.max(roi.y0, Math.floor(ymin)), y1 = Math.min(roi.y1 - 1, Math.ceil(ymax));
  for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
    const yc = y + 0.5, xs = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < pts.length; k++) {
      const [ax, ay] = pts[k], [bx, by] = pts[(k + 1) % pts.length];
      if ((ay <= yc && by > yc) || (by <= yc && ay > yc)) xs.push(ax + ((yc - ay) / (by - ay)) * (bx - ax));
    }
    xs.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    for (let k = 0; k + 1 < xs.length; k += 2) {
      const xa = Math.max(roi.x0, Math.ceil(xs[k] - 0.5)), xb = Math.min(roi.x1 - 1, Math.floor(xs[k + 1] - 0.5));
      for (let x = xa; x <= xb; x++) out.push(y * w + x);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Accumulate per-object statistics over a region. ids: Set of ids to include, or null for all.
 * Colour statistics use the original (unfiltered) image so learning is independent of the display filters.
 *
 * @param {ArrayLike<number>} labels
 * @param {number} w
 * @param {number} h
 * @param {ArrayLike<number>} rgba
 * @param {ReadonlySet<number> | null} [ids]
 * @param {Roi | null} [region]
 * @param {StackTable | null} [stacks]
 * @returns {Map<number, TraceStats>}
 */
export function objectStats(labels, w, h, rgba, ids = null, region = null, stacks = null) {
  // V0.20: with `stacks`, a pixel in an overlap counts for every object of its stack.
  const R = region || { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: w, y1: h };
  /** @type {Map<number, TraceStats>} */
  const acc = new Map();
  /** @param {number} i */
  const lum = (i) => 0.299 * rgba[i * 4] + 0.587 * rgba[i * 4 + 1] + 0.114 * rgba[i * 4 + 2];
  /** @type {(k: number, id: number) => boolean} */
  const own = stacks ? (k, id) => hasId(labels[k], id, stacks) : (k, id) => labels[k] === id;
  /** @param {number} x @param {number} y @param {number} i @param {number} id */
  const add = (x, y, i, id) => {
    if (ids && !ids.has(id)) return;
    let a = acc.get(id);
    if (!a) {
      a = { n: 0, sx: 0, sy: 0, sxx: 0, syy: 0, sxy: 0, x0: x, x1: x, y0: y, y1: y, per: 0, sL: 0, sLL: 0, sA: 0, sB: 0, rL: 0, rN: 0 };
      acc.set(id, a);
    }
    const j = i * 4, r = rgba[j], g = rgba[j + 1], b = rgba[j + 2], L = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b;
    a.n++; a.sx += x; a.sy += y; a.sxx += x * x; a.syy += y * y; a.sxy += x * y;
    if (x < a.x0) a.x0 = x; if (x > a.x1) a.x1 = x; if (y < a.y0) a.y0 = y; if (y > a.y1) a.y1 = y;
    a.sL += L; a.sLL += L * L; a.sA += r - g; a.sB += (r + g) / 2 - b;
    const edge = x === 0 || !own(i - 1, id) || x === w - 1 || !own(i + 1, id) ||
      y === 0 || !own(i - w, id) || y === h - 1 || !own(i + w, id);
    if (edge) {
      a.per++;
      for (const [dx, dy] of [[-3, 0], [3, 0], [0, -3], [0, 3]]) {
        const xx = x + dx, yy = y + dy;
        if (xx < 0 || yy < 0 || xx >= w || yy >= h) continue;
        const k = yy * w + xx;
        if (!own(k, id)) { a.rL += lum(k); a.rN++; }
      }
    }
  };
  for (let y = R.y0; y < R.y1; y++) {
    for (let x = R.x0; x < R.x1; x++) {
      const i = y * w + x, v = labels[i];
      if (v > 0) add(x, y, i, v);
      else if (v < 0) for (const id of /** @type {number[]} */ ((/** @type {StackTable} */ (stacks))[-v])) add(x, y, i, id);
    }
  }
  return acc;
}

/** Shape and colour descriptors from accumulated statistics. ppu = pixels per cm.
 *
 * @param {TraceStats} a
 * @param {number} ppu
 */
export function features(a, ppu) {
  const n = a.n, mx = a.sx / n, my = a.sy / n;
  const cxx = a.sxx / n - mx * mx + 1 / 12, cyy = a.syy / n - my * my + 1 / 12, cxy = a.sxy / n - mx * my;
  const tr = cxx + cyy, det = cxx * cyy - cxy * cxy, disc = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, (tr * tr) / 4 - det));
  const l1 = tr / 2 + disc, l2 = Math.max(tr / 2 - disc, 1 / 12);
  const theta = 0.5 * Math.atan2(2 * cxy, cxx - cyy);
  const meanL = a.sL / n;
  const majorCm = (4 * Math.sqrt(l1)) / ppu, minorCm = (4 * Math.sqrt(l2)) / ppu, areaCm2 = n / (ppu * ppu);
  return {
    areaCm2, majorCm, minorCm,
    logArea: Math.log10(areaCm2 + 1e-6),
    logMajor: Math.log10(majorCm + 1e-6),
    logMinor: Math.log10(minorCm + 1e-6),
    elong: Math.sqrt(l1 / l2),
    vert: Math.abs(Math.sin(theta)),
    compact: Math.min(1, (4 * Math.PI * n) / Math.max(1, a.per * a.per)),
    fill: n / ((a.x1 - a.x0 + 1) * (a.y1 - a.y0 + 1)),
    meanL,
    stdL: Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, a.sLL / n - meanL * meanL)),
    meanA: a.sA / n,
    meanB: a.sB / n,
    contrast: meanL - (a.rN ? a.rL / a.rN : meanL),
    cx: mx, cy: my,
  };
}

/** Box-average downsampling of an RGBA raster by an integer factor k (remainder rows/columns dropped).
 *
 * @param {RgbaRaster} src
 * @param {number} w
 * @param {number} h
 * @param {number} k
 */
export function downsampleRGBA(src, w, h, k) {
  if (k <= 1) return { rgba: src, w, h };
  const W = Math.floor(w / k), H = Math.floor(h / k), n = k * k;
  const wide = src instanceof Uint16Array;
  const out = wide ? new Uint16Array(W * H * 4) : new Uint8ClampedArray(W * H * 4);
  for (let Y = 0; Y < H; Y++) {
    for (let X = 0; X < W; X++) {
      let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
      for (let dy = 0; dy < k; dy++) {
        let j = ((Y * k + dy) * w + X * k) * 4;
        for (let dx = 0; dx < k; dx++, j += 4) { r += src[j]; g += src[j + 1]; b += src[j + 2]; }
      }
      const o = (Y * W + X) * 4;
      out[o] = wide ? Math.round(r / n) : r / n;
      out[o + 1] = wide ? Math.round(g / n) : g / n;
      out[o + 2] = wide ? Math.round(b / n) : b / n;
      out[o + 3] = wide ? 65535 : 255;
    }
  }
  return { rgba: out, w: W, h: H };
}

/** Rotate a w×h raster with `ch` channels by 90° (dir = 1 clockwise, -1 counter-clockwise).
 *
 * @template {RasterArray} T
 * @param {T} src
 * @param {number} w
 * @param {number} h
 * @param {number} ch
 * @param {number} dir
 * @returns {T}
 */
export function rotate90(src, w, h, ch, dir) {
  const Type = /** @type {new (length: number) => T} */ (src.constructor);
  const out = new Type(src.length);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      const nx = dir > 0 ? h - 1 - y : y, ny = dir > 0 ? x : w - 1 - x;
      const s = (y * w + x) * ch, d = (ny * h + nx) * ch;
      for (let c = 0; c < ch; c++) out[d + c] = src[s + c];
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Separability of the luminance histogram inside the ROI: Otsu's between-class variance divided by the
 * total variance (η, 0–1; Otsu, 1979). High η = two well separated groups of grey levels (e.g. trace
 * infill vs host sediment). Used to rank enhancement settings on one image.
 *
 * @param {ArrayLike<number>} rgba
 * @param {number} w
 * @param {number} h
 * @param {Roi} [roi]
 */
export function separability(rgba, w, h, roi = { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: w, y1: h }) {
  const hist = new Float64Array(256); let n = 0;
  for (let y = roi.y0; y < roi.y1; y++) for (let x = roi.x0; x < roi.x1; x++) {
    const j = (y * w + x) * 4; hist[Math.min(255, Math.round(0.299 * rgba[j] + 0.587 * rgba[j + 1] + 0.114 * rgba[j + 2]))]++; n++;
  }
  if (!n) return { eta: 0, threshold: 0 };
  let mu = 0; for (let v = 0; v < 256; v++) mu += v * hist[v] / n;
  let varT = 0; for (let v = 0; v < 256; v++) varT += (v - mu) ** 2 * hist[v] / n;
  if (!varT) return { eta: 0, threshold: 0 };
  let w0 = 0, m0 = 0, best = 0, thr = 0;
  for (let v = 0; v < 255; v++) {
    w0 += hist[v] / n; m0 += v * hist[v] / n;
    if (w0 <= 0 || w0 >= 1) continue;
    const sb = (mu * w0 - m0) ** 2 / (w0 * (1 - w0));
    if (sb > best) { best = sb; thr = v; }
  }
  return { eta: best / varT, threshold: thr };
}

/**
 * V0.28: how well an enhanced image separates traces from matrix, for ranking enhancement settings.
 * cls[i] (same size as the image): 1 = trace, 2 = matrix, 0 = not used. The feature is the luminance (global
 * threshold) or the luminance minus its local mean over `radius` px (adaptive threshold), as the detection uses it.
 * Score = 2·AUC − 1 in the expected direction (polarity 'dark': traces darker; 'light'; 'both' = either), 0–1.
 *
 * @param {ArrayLike<number>} rgba
 * @param {number} w
 * @param {number} h
 * @param {Roi} roi
 * @param {ArrayLike<number>} cls
 * @param {{local?: boolean, radius?: number, polarity?: string}} [options]
 */
export function traceSeparation(rgba, w, h, roi, cls, { local = true, radius = 40, polarity = 'dark' } = {}) {
  const L = lumaOf(rgba, w * h), M = local ? boxBlur(L, w, h, Math.max(1, Math.round(radius))) : null;
  const ht = new Float64Array(511), hm = new Float64Array(511); let nt = 0, nm = 0;
  for (let y = roi.y0; y < roi.y1; y++) for (let x = roi.x0; x < roi.x1; x++) {
    const i = y * w + x, c = cls[i]; if (!c) continue;
    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(510, Math.round((M ? L[i] - M[i] : L[i]) + 255)));
    if (c === 1) { ht[v]++; nt++; } else { hm[v]++; nm++; }
  }
  if (!nt || !nm) return { score: 0, auc: 0.5, nTrace: nt, nMatrix: nm };
  // AUC = P(matrix value > trace value) + ½ P(equal): probability that a trace pixel is darker than a matrix pixel
  let below = 0, auc = 0;
  for (let v = 0; v < 511; v++) { auc += (ht[v] / nt) * ((nm - below - hm[v]) / nm + 0.5 * (hm[v] / nm)); below += hm[v]; }
  const dir = polarity === 'light' ? 1 - auc : polarity === 'both' ? Math.max(auc, 1 - auc) : auc;
  return { score: Math.max(0, 2 * dir - 1), auc, nTrace: nt, nMatrix: nm };
}

/**
 * V0.28: separability without reviewed traces, computed in tiles of `tile` px (so slow colour changes along the core
 * and large cracks weigh less than with one histogram of the whole area). In each tile not flat (std ≥ 4 grey levels)
 * the grey levels are split at Otsu's threshold and Fisher's ratio J = (μ₁ − μ₂)² / (σ₁² + σ₂²) is measured (invariant
 * only to linear stretches; equalisation or local contrast can raise it on pure noise).
 * V0.37: J/(J + 6) is multiplied by the spatial coherence of the split: κ = (A − E)/(1 − E), with A the share of
 * horizontal and vertical neighbour pairs on the same side of the threshold and E = p₀² + p₁² the share expected if
 * the pixels were scattered at random (κ ≤ 0 → 0). Noise gives κ ≈ 0 whatever the enhancement; compact traces give κ
 * near 1. Result: mean over the tiles (0–1).
 *
 * @param {ArrayLike<number>} rgba
 * @param {number} w
 * @param {number} h
 * @param {Roi} roi
 * @param {number} [tile]
 */
export function localSeparability(rgba, w, h, roi, tile = 48) {
  let sum = 0, n = 0;
  const hist = new Float64Array(256);
  for (let y0 = roi.y0; y0 < roi.y1; y0 += tile) for (let x0 = roi.x0; x0 < roi.x1; x0 += tile) {
    const x1 = Math.min(roi.x1, x0 + tile), y1 = Math.min(roi.y1, y0 + tile);
    if ((x1 - x0) * (y1 - y0) < tile * tile * 0.4) continue;
    hist.fill(0); let k = 0, s1 = 0, s2 = 0;
    for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) { const j = (y * w + x) * 4, v = Math.min(255, Math.round(0.299 * rgba[j] + 0.587 * rgba[j + 1] + 0.114 * rgba[j + 2])); hist[v]++; k++; s1 += v; s2 += v * v; }
    if (Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, s2 / k - (s1 / k) ** 2)) < 4) continue;
    const thr = separability(rgba, w, h, { x0, y0, x1, y1 }).threshold;
    let n0 = 0, a0 = 0, b0 = 0, n1 = 0, a1 = 0, b1 = 0;
    for (let v = 0; v < 256; v++) { const c = hist[v]; if (v <= thr) { n0 += c; a0 += c * v; b0 += c * v * v; } else { n1 += c; a1 += c * v; b1 += c * v * v; } }
    if (!n0 || !n1) continue;
    const m0 = a0 / n0, m1 = a1 / n1, v0 = b0 / n0 - m0 * m0, v1 = b1 / n1 - m1 * m1, J = (m1 - m0) ** 2 / Math.max(1e-6, v0 + v1);
    let agree = 0, pairs = 0;
    /** @param {number} x @param {number} y */
    const g = (x, y) => { const j = (y * w + x) * 4; return 0.299 * rgba[j] + 0.587 * rgba[j + 1] + 0.114 * rgba[j + 2] > thr + 0.5; };
    for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
      const c = g(x, y);
      if (x + 1 < x1) { pairs++; if (g(x + 1, y) === c) agree++; }
      if (y + 1 < y1) { pairs++; if (g(x, y + 1) === c) agree++; }
    }
    const p0 = n0 / k, E = p0 * p0 + (1 - p0) ** 2, kappa = E < 1 ? Math.max(0, (agree / pairs - E) / (1 - E)) : 0;
    sum += (J / (J + 6)) * kappa; n++;
  }
  return n ? sum / n : 0;
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/interp.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool web — gridding of scattered core values for maps (V20), in the spirit of Surfer.
// No DOM. Distances are in km on a Lambert azimuthal equal-area projection centred on the map (V22; before: degrees).
// Methods: inverse distance, nearest neighbour, triangulation with linear interpolation (Delaunay),
// radial basis functions (thin-plate spline = minimum curvature, multiquadric, gaussian), ordinary kriging
// (spherical / exponential / gaussian / linear variogram, fitted automatically or by hand) and moving average.
// Contours by marching squares; colour ramps for the grid and the colour bar.

import { makeProjection, validMapCoords } from './geo.js';

export const INTERP_METHODS = ['idw', 'nearest', 'tin', 'mincurv', 'rbf', 'kriging', 'movavg'];
export const INTERP_DEFAULT = {
  method: 'idw', power: 2, radius: 0, neighbours: 0, rbf: 'multiquadric', shape: 200, smooth: 0,
  variogram: 'spherical', autoVario: true, nugget: 0, sill: 1, range: 500, res: 90, hull: true, mask: 'sea',
  contours: false, levels: 8, labels: true, ramp: 'div', reverse: false, scale: 'sym2', vmin: -2, vmax: 2, steps: 0, opacity: 0.75, on: true,
};

/** Linear system A x = b (Gaussian elimination with partial pivoting); returns null if singular. */
export function solve(A, b) {
  const n = b.length, M = A.map((r, i) => [...r, b[i]]), tol = 1e-12 * Math.max(1, ...A.map((r) => Math.max(...r.map(Math.abs))));
  for (let c = 0; c < n; c++) {
    let p = c; for (let r = c + 1; r < n; r++) if (Math.abs(M[r][c]) > Math.abs(M[p][c])) p = r;
    if (Math.abs(M[p][c]) < tol) return null;
    [M[c], M[p]] = [M[p], M[c]];
    for (let r = c + 1; r < n; r++) { const f = M[r][c] / M[c][c]; if (f) for (let k = c; k <= n; k++) M[r][k] -= f * M[c][k]; }
  }
  const x = new Array(n).fill(0);
  for (let r = n - 1; r >= 0; r--) { let s = M[r][n]; for (let k = r + 1; k < n; k++) s -= M[r][k] * x[k]; x[r] = s / M[r][r]; }
  return x;
}

/** Delaunay triangulation (Bowyer–Watson). pts = [[x, y]]; returns triangles as index triples. */
export function delaunay(pts) {
  const n = pts.length; if (n < 3) return [];
  let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y] of pts) { x0 = Math.min(x0, x); y0 = Math.min(y0, y); x1 = Math.max(x1, x); y1 = Math.max(y1, y); }
  const d = Math.max(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) * 20 || 1, mx = (x0 + x1) / 2, my = (y0 + y1) / 2;
  const P = [...pts, [mx - d, my - d], [mx + d, my - d], [mx, my + d]];
  const circ = (a, b, c) => {
    const [ax, ay] = P[a], [bx, by] = P[b], [cx, cy] = P[c], D = 2 * (ax * (by - cy) + bx * (cy - ay) + cx * (ay - by));
    if (Math.abs(D) < 1e-14) return { x: 0, y: 0, r: Infinity };
    const ux = ((ax * ax + ay * ay) * (by - cy) + (bx * bx + by * by) * (cy - ay) + (cx * cx + cy * cy) * (ay - by)) / D;
    const uy = ((ax * ax + ay * ay) * (cx - bx) + (bx * bx + by * by) * (ax - cx) + (cx * cx + cy * cy) * (bx - ax)) / D;
    return { x: ux, y: uy, r: (ax - ux) ** 2 + (ay - uy) ** 2 };
  };
  let tris = [{ v: [n, n + 1, n + 2], c: circ(n, n + 1, n + 2) }];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const [px, py] = P[i], bad = [], keep = [];
    for (const t of tris) ((px - t.c.x) ** 2 + (py - t.c.y) ** 2 < t.c.r ? bad : keep).push(t);
    const edges = new Map();
    for (const t of bad) for (const [a, b] of [[t.v[0], t.v[1]], [t.v[1], t.v[2]], [t.v[2], t.v[0]]]) { const k = a < b ? `${a},${b}` : `${b},${a}`; edges.set(k, edges.has(k) ? null : [a, b]); }
    for (const e of edges.values()) if (e) keep.push({ v: [e[0], e[1], i], c: circ(e[0], e[1], i) });
    tris = keep;
  }
  return tris.filter((t) => t.v.every((k) => k < n)).map((t) => t.v);
}

const phi = (kind, r, c) => (kind === 'tps' ? (r > 0 ? r * r * Math.log(r) : 0) : kind === 'gaussian' ? Math.exp(-((r / c) ** 2)) : Math.sqrt(r * r + c * c));

/** Semivariogram model γ(h). */
export function variogram(model, h, nugget, sill, range) {
  if (h <= 0) return 0;
  const ps = Math.max(sill - nugget, 0), a = Math.max(range, 1e-9);
  if (model === 'linear') return nugget + ps * Math.min(h / a, 1);
  if (model === 'exponential') return nugget + ps * (1 - Math.exp((-3 * h) / a));
  if (model === 'gaussian') return nugget + ps * (1 - Math.exp(-3 * (h / a) ** 2));
  return h >= a ? sill : nugget + ps * (1.5 * (h / a) - 0.5 * (h / a) ** 3); // spherical
}

/** Empirical semivariogram (8 distance classes up to half the largest distance) and least-squares fit of range and nugget. */
export function fitVariogram(xy, v, model = 'spherical') {
  const n = v.length, pairs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) pairs.push([Math.hypot(xy[i][0] - xy[j][0], xy[i][1] - xy[j][1]), 0.5 * (v[i] - v[j]) ** 2]);
  const mean = v.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / (n || 1), sill = v.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / Math.max(n - 1, 1) || 1;
  if (pairs.length < 3) return { nugget: 0, sill, range: 1, bins: [] };
  const hmax = Math.max(...pairs.map((p) => p[0])) / 2 || 1, nb = 8, bins = [];
  for (let k = 0; k < nb; k++) {
    const a = (k * hmax) / nb, b = ((k + 1) * hmax) / nb, sel = pairs.filter((p) => p[0] > a && p[0] <= b);
    if (sel.length) bins.push({ h: sel.reduce((s, p) => s + p[0], 0) / sel.length, g: sel.reduce((s, p) => s + p[1], 0) / sel.length, n: sel.length });
  }
  let best = { err: Infinity, nugget: 0, range: hmax };
  for (let r = 1; r <= 30; r++) for (let q = 0; q <= 5; q++) {
    const range = (hmax * 2 * r) / 30, nugget = (sill * q) / 10;
    const err = bins.reduce((s, b) => s + (b.n / (b.h * b.h)) * (variogram(model, b.h, nugget, sill, range) - b.g) ** 2, 0); // weights n_h/h² (gstat fit.method 7, Pebesma 2004; Cressie 1985 uses n_h/γ(h)²)
    if (err < best.err) best = { err, nugget, range };
  }
  return { nugget: best.nugget, sill, range: best.range, bins };
}

/**
 * Grid of interpolated values. points = [{lat, lon, value}]; bbox = [x0, y0, x1, y1]; o = INTERP_DEFAULT-like options.
 * Returns {grid (row-major from the north, null = empty), nx, ny, info} with info.variogram for kriging.
 */
export function interpolate(points, bbox, nx, ny, opt = {}) {
  const o = { ...INTERP_DEFAULT, ...opt }, raw = points.filter((p) => Number.isFinite(p.value) && validMapCoords(p));
  const pr = makeProjection('laea', bbox), X = (lon, lat) => pr.fwd(lon, lat).map((q) => q * 6371); // km
  // co-located cores (< 10 m apart) are averaged: duplicates would make the kriging and RBF systems singular
  const merged = [];
  for (const p of raw) { const q = X(p.lon, p.lat), m = merged.find((r) => Math.hypot(r.xy[0] - q[0], r.xy[1] - q[1]) < 0.01); if (m) { m.s += p.value; m.k++; } else merged.push({ xy: q, s: p.value, k: 1 }); }
  const xy = merged.map((m) => m.xy), v = merged.map((m) => m.s / m.k), n = merged.length, grid = new Array(nx * ny).fill(null), info = { merged: raw.length - n };
  if (!n) return { grid, nx, ny, info };
  const dist = (a, b) => Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]);
  let at; // function of a cell centre [x, y] → value
  if (o.method === 'nearest') at = (c) => { let b = 0, d = Infinity; xy.forEach((q, i) => { const e = dist(q, c); if (e < d) { d = e; b = i; } }); return o.radius > 0 && d > o.radius ? null : v[b]; };
  else if (o.method === 'movavg') at = (c) => { let s = 0, k = 0; const r = o.radius > 0 ? o.radius : 500; xy.forEach((q, i) => { if (dist(q, c) <= r) { s += v[i]; k++; } }); return k ? s / k : null; };
  else if (o.method === 'tin') {
    const T = delaunay(xy);
    at = (c) => {
      for (const [a, b, d] of T) {
        const [x1, y1] = xy[a], [x2, y2] = xy[b], [x3, y3] = xy[d], D = (y2 - y3) * (x1 - x3) + (x3 - x2) * (y1 - y3);
        if (!D) continue;
        const l1 = ((y2 - y3) * (c[0] - x3) + (x3 - x2) * (c[1] - y3)) / D, l2 = ((y3 - y1) * (c[0] - x3) + (x1 - x3) * (c[1] - y3)) / D, l3 = 1 - l1 - l2;
        if (l1 >= -1e-9 && l2 >= -1e-9 && l3 >= -1e-9) return l1 * v[a] + l2 * v[b] + l3 * v[d];
      }
      return null;
    };
  } else if (o.method === 'rbf' || o.method === 'mincurv') {
    const kind = o.method === 'mincurv' ? 'tps' : o.rbf, c0 = o.shape || 1;
    const A = [], b = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { A.push([...xy.map((q, j) => phi(kind, dist(xy[i], q), c0) + (i === j ? o.smooth : 0)), 1, xy[i][0], xy[i][1]]); b.push(v[i]); }
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { A.push([...xy.map((q) => (k === 0 ? 1 : q[k - 1])), 0, 0, 0]); b.push(0); }
    const w = n >= 3 ? solve(A, b) : null;
    at = w ? (c) => { let s = w[n] + w[n + 1] * c[0] + w[n + 2] * c[1]; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += w[i] * phi(kind, dist(xy[i], c), c0); return s; } : null;
  } else if (o.method === 'kriging') {
    const vg = o.autoVario ? fitVariogram(xy, v, o.variogram) : { nugget: o.nugget, sill: o.sill, range: o.range, bins: fitVariogram(xy, v, o.variogram).bins };
    info.variogram = { model: o.variogram, ...vg }; info.few = n < 15;
    const g = (h) => variogram(o.variogram, h, vg.nugget, vg.sill, vg.range);
    const A = xy.map((p) => [...xy.map((q) => g(dist(p, q))), 1]).concat([[...xy.map(() => 1), 0]]);
    // V0.41: estimate = λᵀv with λ = A⁻¹b; A is symmetric, so λᵀv = bᵀ(A⁻¹[v; 0]) = bᵀu: one solve, then O(n) per cell
    // (before: n + 1 solves and O(n²) per cell; same values up to rounding)
    const u = solve(A, [...v, 0]);
    if (u) at = (c) => { let s = u[n]; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += u[i] * g(dist(xy[i], c)); return s; };
  }
  if (!at) { // inverse distance (also the fallback when a system cannot be solved)
    at = (c) => {
      let ds = xy.map((q, i) => [dist(q, c), i]);
      if (o.radius > 0) ds = ds.filter((q) => q[0] <= o.radius);
      if (o.neighbours > 0) ds = ds.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).slice(0, o.neighbours);
      let sw = 0, sv = 0;
      for (const [d, i] of ds) { if (d < 1e-9) return v[i]; const w = 1 / d ** o.power; sw += w; sv += w * v[i]; }
      return sw ? sv / sw : null;
    };
    if (o.method !== 'idw') info.fallback = 'idw';
  }
  const hull = o.hull && n >= 3 ? convexHull(xy) : null;
  for (let iy = 0; iy < ny; iy++) {
    const lat = bbox[3] - ((iy + 0.5) / ny) * (bbox[3] - bbox[1]);
    for (let ix = 0; ix < nx; ix++) {
      const lon = bbox[0] + ((ix + 0.5) / nx) * (bbox[2] - bbox[0]), c = X(lon, lat);
      if (hull && !inPoly(c, hull)) continue;
      const val = at(c);
      grid[iy * nx + ix] = val != null && Number.isFinite(val) ? val : null;
    }
  }
  return { grid, nx, ny, info };
}

/** Convex hull (monotone chain) of [[x, y]]. */
export function convexHull(p) {
  const s = [...p].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1] - b[1]), cr = (o, a, b) => (a[0] - o[0]) * (b[1] - o[1]) - (a[1] - o[1]) * (b[0] - o[0]);
  const lo = [], up = [];
  for (const q of s) { while (lo.length >= 2 && cr(lo[lo.length - 2], lo[lo.length - 1], q) <= 0) lo.pop(); lo.push(q); }
  for (const q of s.reverse()) { while (up.length >= 2 && cr(up[up.length - 2], up[up.length - 1], q) <= 0) up.pop(); up.push(q); }
  return lo.slice(0, -1).concat(up.slice(0, -1));
}
export function inPoly([x, y], poly) {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [xi, yi] = poly[i], [xj, yj] = poly[j];
    if ((yi > y) !== (yj > y) && x < ((xj - xi) * (y - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

/** keep = 'sea': cells whose centre is on land (Natural Earth rings, flat [lon, lat, …]) become null; 'land': cells at sea become null; 'both': unchanged. */
export function maskLand(grid, nx, ny, bbox, rings, keep = 'sea') {
  if (keep === 'both') return grid;
  const near = rings.filter((r) => { let a = Infinity, b = -Infinity, c = Infinity, d = -Infinity; for (let k = 0; k < r.length; k += 2) { a = Math.min(a, r[k]); b = Math.max(b, r[k]); c = Math.min(c, r[k + 1]); d = Math.max(d, r[k + 1]); } return b >= bbox[0] && a <= bbox[2] && d >= bbox[1] && c <= bbox[3]; })
    .map((r) => { const p = []; for (let k = 0; k < r.length; k += 2) p.push([r[k], r[k + 1]]); return p; });
  return grid.map((v, i) => {
    if (v == null) return v;
    const ix = i % nx, iy = Math.floor(i / nx), lon = bbox[0] + ((ix + 0.5) / nx) * (bbox[2] - bbox[0]), lat = bbox[3] - ((iy + 0.5) / ny) * (bbox[3] - bbox[1]);
    const onLand = near.some((p) => inPoly([lon, lat], p));
    return (keep === 'sea' && onLand) || (keep === 'land' && !onLand) ? null : v;
  });
}

/* ───────── colour ramps ───────── */
export const RAMPS = {
  div: [[38, 110, 150], [242, 236, 219], [196, 80, 46]],
  bluered: [[49, 54, 149], [116, 173, 209], [255, 255, 191], [244, 109, 67], [165, 0, 38]],
  viridis: [[68, 1, 84], [59, 82, 139], [33, 145, 140], [94, 201, 98], [253, 231, 37]],
  magma: [[0, 0, 4], [81, 18, 124], [183, 55, 121], [252, 137, 97], [252, 253, 191]],
  terrain: [[0, 104, 55], [102, 189, 99], [217, 239, 139], [254, 224, 139], [166, 97, 26], [255, 255, 255]],
  ocean: [[8, 29, 88], [37, 52, 148], [34, 94, 168], [65, 182, 196], [199, 233, 180]],
  grey: [[30, 30, 30], [235, 235, 235]],
  rainbow: [[110, 64, 170], [38, 150, 230], [40, 200, 120], [230, 220, 50], [235, 120, 40], [200, 30, 30]],
};
/** Colour of value v on a ramp between vmin and vmax (steps > 0: discrete classes). */
export function rampColor(ramp, v, vmin, vmax, steps = 0, reverse = false) {
  const R = RAMPS[ramp] || RAMPS.div;
  let t = vmax > vmin ? (v - vmin) / (vmax - vmin) : 0.5;
  t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t)); if (steps > 1) t = Math.min(steps - 1, Math.floor(t * steps)) / (steps - 1); if (reverse) t = 1 - t;
  const f = t * (R.length - 1), k = Math.min(R.length - 2, Math.floor(f)), u = f - k;
  return `rgb(${R[k].map((a, j) => Math.round(a + (R[k + 1][j] - a) * u)).join(',')})`;
}
/** Colour range of a grid: 'sym2' = −2…2 (z-scores), 'auto' = min…max, 'symauto' = ±max|v|, 'manual' = vmin…vmax. */
export function colorRange(values, o) {
  const v = values.filter((x) => x != null && Number.isFinite(x));
  if (o.scale === 'manual') return [o.vmin, o.vmax];
  if (o.scale === 'sym2' || !v.length) return [-2, 2];
  const lo = Math.min(...v), hi = Math.max(...v);
  if (o.scale === 'symauto') { const m = Math.max(Math.abs(lo), Math.abs(hi)) || 1; return [-m, m]; }
  return lo === hi ? [lo - 1, hi + 1] : [lo, hi];
}

/* ───────── contours (marching squares on cell centres) ───────── */
/** Levels evenly spaced inside [vmin, vmax] (n levels, round numbers when possible). */
export function contourLevels(vmin, vmax, n) {
  const raw = (vmax - vmin) / (n + 1), p = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(raw || 1)), st = [1, 2, 2.5, 5, 10].map((k) => k * p).find((k) => k >= raw) || raw;
  const out = []; for (let x = Math.ceil(vmin / st) * st; x < vmax - 1e-12; x += st) if (x > vmin + 1e-12) out.push(+x.toFixed(10));
  return out;
}
/** Segments [[x1, y1, x2, y2]] in grid-cell units (x = column centre index, y = row) for one level. */
export function contourSegments(grid, nx, ny, level) {
  const seg = [], g = (x, y) => grid[y * nx + x];
  for (let y = 0; y < ny - 1; y++) for (let x = 0; x < nx - 1; x++) {
    const a = g(x, y), b = g(x + 1, y), c = g(x + 1, y + 1), d = g(x, y + 1);
    if (a == null || b == null || c == null || d == null) continue;
    const k = (a > level ? 8 : 0) | (b > level ? 4 : 0) | (c > level ? 2 : 0) | (d > level ? 1 : 0);
    if (k === 0 || k === 15) continue;
    const L = (p, q) => (level - p) / (q - p || 1e-12);
    const T = [x + L(a, b), y], R = [x + 1, y + L(b, c)], B = [x + L(d, c), y + 1], Lf = [x, y + L(a, d)];
    const cases = { 1: [[Lf, B]], 2: [[B, R]], 3: [[Lf, R]], 4: [[T, R]], 5: [[Lf, T], [B, R]], 6: [[T, B]], 7: [[Lf, T]], 8: [[Lf, T]], 9: [[T, B]], 10: [[T, R], [Lf, B]], 11: [[T, R]], 12: [[Lf, R]], 13: [[B, R]], 14: [[Lf, B]] };
    for (const [p, q] of cases[k]) seg.push([p[0], p[1], q[0], q[1]]);
  }
  return seg;
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/learn.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool web — incremental learning of trace-fossil classes from labelled objects.
// Distance-weighted k-nearest neighbours on standardised shape/colour descriptors:
// it learns from the first few examples, needs no training step, and refuses to
// label objects that look unlike anything seen so far (novelty gate).

export const FEATURES = ['logArea', 'logMajor', 'logMinor', 'elong', 'vert', 'compact', 'fill', 'meanL', 'stdL', 'meanA', 'meanB', 'contrast'];

/** Leave-one-out uses at most this many examples (evenly spaced). */
export const LOO_MAX = 1500;

export const vec = (f) => FEATURES.map((k) => (Number.isFinite(f?.[k]) ? f[k] : 0));

/** Signature of a descriptor vector (3 decimals): identical traces give identical signatures. */
export const descriptorSig = (x) => x.map((v) => (+v).toFixed(3)).join(',');
/**
 * External examples (library, other projects) that may come from a reserved core are left out of training and model
 * selection: same core id in any project (copies keep the id), same core name, or a descriptor vector identical to a
 * reserved trace (catches copies whose origin is unknown). reserved = {ids, names (lower case), sigs} or null.
 */
export function independentExternalExamples(examples, reserved, xOf = (e) => e.x) {
  if (!reserved || !(reserved.ids.size + reserved.names.size + reserved.sigs.size)) return examples;
  return examples.filter((e) => {
    const parts = String(e.key || '').split('|');
    if (parts.length >= 3 && reserved.ids.has(parts[1])) return false;
    const name = String(e.core ?? String(e.from ?? e.where ?? '').split(' · ').pop()).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (name && reserved.names.has(name)) return false;
    const x = xOf(e);
    return !(Array.isArray(x) && reserved.sigs.has(descriptorSig(x)));
  });
}

export class Classifier {
  /** V0.17: `features` names the input vector (FEATURES, or a vision-model id); its length is taken from the examples. */
  constructor({ k = 5, novelty = 3, weights = 'distance', features = FEATURES } = {}) { this.k = k; this.novelty = novelty; this.weights = weights; this.features = features; this.n = 0; }

  fit(examples) {
    this.n = examples.length;
    this.X = examples.map((e) => e.x);
    this.g = examples.map((e) => e.g ?? null); // group (image section): leave-one-out leaves out the whole group
    this.y = examples.map((e) => e.y);
    this.counts = {};
    for (const y of this.y) this.counts[y] = (this.counts[y] || 0) + 1;
    if (this.n < 2) { this.accuracy = null; return this; }
    const d = examples[0].x.length;
    this.mu = new Array(d).fill(0); this.sd = new Array(d).fill(0);
    for (const e of examples) e.x.forEach((v, j) => (this.mu[j] += v / this.n));
    for (const e of examples) e.x.forEach((v, j) => (this.sd[j] += (v - this.mu[j]) ** 2 / this.n));
    this.sd = this.sd.map((s) => Math.sqrt(s) || 1);
    this.Z = examples.map((e) => this.z(e.x));
    // Leave-one-out: typical nearest-neighbour distance (novelty scale) and accuracy estimate.
    // (at most LOO_MAX evenly spaced examples, so that very large libraries stay fast)
    const nn = [], ok = [], step = Math.max(1, this.n / LOO_MAX), useG = new Set(this.g.filter((v) => v != null)).size >= 2;
    this.grouped = useG;
    for (let f = 0; f < this.n; f += step) {
      const i = Math.floor(f);
      const r = this.vote(this.Z[i], i);
      nn.push(r.dmin); // novelty scale: nearest other example, whatever its group
      // accuracy: leaving out the whole group (image section) when there are several groups, else the example alone
      const rg = useG ? this.vote(this.Z[i], i, this.g[i]) : r;
      if (Object.keys(this.counts).length > 1 && rg.y != null) ok.push(rg.y === this.y[i] ? 1 : 0);
    }
    nn.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    this.scale = nn[nn.length >> 1] || 1;
    this.accuracy = ok.length ? ok.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / ok.length : null;
    return this;
  }

  z(x) { return x.map((v, j) => (v - this.mu[j]) / this.sd[j]); }

  vote(z, skip = -1, group = null) {
    const best = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < this.n; i++) {
      if (i === skip || (group != null && this.g?.[i] === group)) continue;
      const zi = this.Z[i]; let s = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < zi.length; j++) { const t = zi[j] - z[j]; s += t * t; }
      const d = Math.sqrt(s);
      if (best.length < this.k) { best.push([d, i]); best.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]); }
      else if (d < best[best.length - 1][0]) { best[best.length - 1] = [d, i]; best.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]); }
    }
    const votes = {}; let tot = 0;
    for (const [d, i] of best) { const wgt = this.weights === 'uniform' ? 1 : 1 / (d + 0.05); votes[this.y[i]] = (votes[this.y[i]] || 0) + wgt; tot += wgt; }
    let y = null, v = -1;
    for (const [k, s] of Object.entries(votes)) if (s > v) { y = k; v = s; }
    const probs = {};
    for (const [k, s] of Object.entries(votes)) probs[k] = tot ? s / tot : 0;
    return { y, conf: tot ? v / tot : 0, probs, dmin: best.length ? best[0][0] : Infinity };
  }

  /**
   * Returns {y, conf, probs} (probs = share of the distance-weighted vote of each class among the k neighbours),
   * {y: null, novel: true, probs} for an object unlike any example, or null with fewer than 2 examples.
   */
  predict(x) {
    if (this.n < 2) return null;
    const r = this.vote(this.z(x));
    if (r.dmin > this.novelty * this.scale) return { y: null, conf: 0, novel: true, probs: r.probs };
    return r;
  }

  /** Stored model ("weights" of a k-NN model: standardisation, novelty scale and the examples). */
  toJSON() {
    const r5 = (v) => +v.toFixed(5);
    return { kind: 'knn', version: 1, k: this.k, novelty: this.novelty, weights: this.weights, n: this.n, features: this.features, mu: this.mu?.map(r5) || null, sd: this.sd?.map(r5) || null,
      scale: this.scale ?? null, accuracy: this.accuracy ?? null, counts: this.counts || {}, y: this.y || [], X: (this.X || []).map((x) => x.map(r5)) };
  }

  /** Model rebuilt from toJSON() without refitting (same standardisation, scale and examples). */
  static fromJSON(m, features = FEATURES) {
    const c = new Classifier({ k: m.k, novelty: m.novelty, weights: m.weights || 'distance', features });
    if (!m || !Array.isArray(m.X) || JSON.stringify(m.features) !== JSON.stringify(features)) return c;
    Object.assign(c, { n: m.n, y: m.y, X: m.X, counts: m.counts, scale: m.scale, accuracy: m.accuracy, mu: m.mu, sd: m.sd });
    if (c.n >= 2 && c.mu) c.Z = c.X.map((x) => c.z(x));
    return c;
  }
}

/* ───────── several methods for ichnotaxa (V18) ───────── */

/** Small seeded generator (mulberry32) so that evaluations can be repeated exactly. */
export function rng(seed = 42) { let a = seed >>> 0; return () => { a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0; let t = a; t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1); t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61); return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }

/**
 * Grouped folds: whole groups (image sections) go to one fold, so traces of the same section are never both trained
 * and tested. Groups are shuffled (seeded) and each one goes to the fold with fewest examples of its main class.
 * V0.37: with 2…k−1 groups, one fold per group (leave one group out); stratifiedFolds only with a single group.
 */
export function groupedFolds(y, groups, k = 5, seed = 42) {
  const ids = [...new Set(groups)];
  if (ids.length < 2) return stratifiedFolds(y, k, seed);
  if (ids.length < k) return ids.map((g) => groups.reduce((o, x, i) => (x === g && o.push(i), o), []));
  const r = rng(seed), by = new Map(ids.map((g) => [g, []]));
  groups.forEach((g, i) => by.get(g).push(i));
  for (let i = ids.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(r() * (i + 1)); [ids[i], ids[j]] = [ids[j], ids[i]]; }
  const folds = Array.from({ length: k }, () => []), cnt = folds.map(() => ({}));
  ids.sort((a, b) => by.get(b).length - by.get(a).length);
  for (const g of ids) {
    const m = by.get(g), main = Object.entries(m.reduce((o, i) => ((o[y[i]] = (o[y[i]] || 0) + 1), o), {})).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0][0];
    let best = 0; for (let f = 1; f < k; f++) if ((cnt[f][main] || 0) < (cnt[best][main] || 0) || ((cnt[f][main] || 0) === (cnt[best][main] || 0) && folds[f].length < folds[best].length)) best = f;
    folds[best].push(...m); for (const i of m) cnt[best][y[i]] = (cnt[best][y[i]] || 0) + 1;
  }
  return folds.filter((q) => q.length);
}

/** Stratified folds: indices of each class shuffled (seeded) and dealt round-robin into k folds. */
export function stratifiedFolds(y, k = 5, seed = 42) {
  const r = rng(seed), by = {}, folds = Array.from({ length: k }, () => []);
  y.forEach((c, i) => (by[c] ||= []).push(i));
  let f = 0;
  for (const c of Object.keys(by).sort()) {
    const ix = by[c];
    for (let i = ix.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(r() * (i + 1)); [ix[i], ix[j]] = [ix[j], ix[i]]; }
    for (const i of ix) { folds[f % k].push(i); f++; }
  }
  return folds.filter((q) => q.length);
}

/**
 * Model of ichnotaxa with a choice of method. A k-NN Classifier is always fitted: it gives the standardisation,
 * the novelty test and (for k-NN) the leave-one-out accuracy. With method 'rf' or 'nb' the class probabilities come
 * from a Random Forest or a Gaussian naive Bayes fitted on the standardised descriptors (fitFn/probaFn are injected:
 * geo.js fitModel/predictProba).
 * opt = {method: 'knn'|'rf'|'nb', k, weights, novelty, rf: {...}, nb: {...}, fitFn, probaFn, seed}
 */
export class TaxonModel {
  constructor(opt = {}) { this.opt = { method: 'knn', k: 5, weights: 'distance', novelty: 3, seed: 42, ...opt }; this.n = 0; this.knn = new Classifier(this.opt); }
  get method() { return this.opt.method; }
  fit(examples) {
    const o = this.opt;
    this.knn = new Classifier({ k: o.k, novelty: o.novelty, weights: o.weights, features: o.features || FEATURES }).fit(examples);
    this.n = this.knn.n; this.counts = this.knn.counts; this.ext = null;
    if (o.method !== 'knn' && this.n >= 2 && Object.keys(this.counts).length >= 2 && o.fitFn) {
      this.ext = o.fitFn(o.method, this.knn.Z, this.knn.y, o[o.method] || {}, o.seed);
    }
    this.accuracy = o.method === 'knn' ? this.knn.accuracy : (this.cv ?? null);
    return this;
  }
  set novelty(v) { this.opt.novelty = v; this.knn.novelty = v; }
  get scale() { return this.knn.scale; }
  /** {y, conf, probs, novel} like Classifier.predict; probabilities from the chosen method. */
  predict(x, { ignoreNovelty = false } = {}) {
    const base = this.knn.predict(x); if (!base) return null;
    let probs = base.probs;
    if (this.ext && this.opt.probaFn) probs = this.opt.probaFn(this.ext, this.knn.z(x));
    let y = null, v = -1; for (const [c, p] of Object.entries(probs)) if (p > v) { y = c; v = p; }
    const novel = !ignoreNovelty && !!base.novel;
    return { y: novel ? null : y, conf: novel ? 0 : v, probs, novel, dmin: base.dmin };
  }
  toJSON() { return { ...this.knn.toJSON(), method: this.opt.method, params: this.opt[this.opt.method] || {} }; }
}

/**
 * Compare methods and scopes on the same test examples (V18).
 * test = examples to predict ({key, y, x}); pools = {scope: examples available for training}; each fold removes its
 * test examples (by key) from every pool, so all scopes are judged on the same traces.
 * Returns {scope: {method: {accuracy, balanced, classes, cm, n}}}.
 */
export function evaluateModels({ test, pools, methods, opt, k = 5, seed = 42, group = (e) => e.g }) {
  // V0.37: scheme = 'groups' (k grouped folds), 'logo' (one fold per group) or 'examples' (single group: optimistic)
  const gs = test.map((e) => group(e) ?? null), nGroups = new Set(gs.filter((g) => g != null)).size;
  const kk = Math.min(k, test.length), folds = nGroups >= 2 ? groupedFolds(test.map((e) => e.y), gs.map((g, i) => g ?? test[i].key), kk, seed) : stratifiedFolds(test.map((e) => e.y), kk, seed), out = {};
  const scheme = nGroups >= kk && nGroups >= 2 ? 'groups' : nGroups >= 2 ? 'logo' : 'examples';
  // rows = true classes of the test traces; columns add every class the models can predict, so a prediction of a class
  // absent from the test set still counts as an error (column «other»)
  const classes = [...new Set(test.map((e) => e.y))].sort();
  for (const sc of Object.keys(pools)) {
    out[sc] = {};
    for (const m of methods) {
      const cm = classes.map(() => classes.map(() => 0)), other = classes.map(() => 0), none = classes.map(() => 0);
      for (const f of folds) {
        // the test traces and every other trace of their groups (same section or core) are left out of training
        const keys = new Set(f.map((i) => test[i].key)), held = new Set(f.map((i) => gs[i]).filter((g) => g != null));
        const train = pools[sc].filter((e) => !keys.has(e.key) && !(scheme !== 'examples' && held.has(group(e))));
        const trainable = new Set(train.map((e) => e.y)).size >= 2, model = trainable ? new TaxonModel({ ...opt, method: m }).fit(train) : null;
        for (const i of f) {
          const a = classes.indexOf(test[i].y), p = model?.predict(test[i].x, { ignoreNovelty: true }), b = classes.indexOf(p?.y);
          if (b >= 0) cm[a][b]++; else if (p?.y != null) other[a]++; else none[a]++;
        }
      }
      const n = test.length, okc = classes.reduce((s, _, i) => s + cm[i][i], 0);
      const rows = classes.map((_, i) => cm[i].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + other[i] + none[i]);
      const rec = classes.map((_, i) => (rows[i] ? cm[i][i] / rows[i] : null)).filter((v) => v != null);
      out[sc][m] = { accuracy: n ? okc / n : null, balanced: rec.length ? rec.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / rec.length : null, classes, cm, other, none, n, scheme, nGroups, folds: folds.length };
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * V0.49: metrics of a final test on reserved cores. pairs = [{y: true class, p: predicted class or null}].
 * accuracy = right / all (no prediction counts as wrong); balanced = mean recall over the true classes;
 * cm[a][b] counts true a predicted b ('—' = no prediction).
 */
export function finalTestMetrics(pairs) {
  const classes = [...new Set(pairs.map((q) => q.y))].sort(), cm = {}, rec = [];
  for (const q of pairs) { const p = q.p ?? '—'; (cm[q.y] ||= {})[p] = (cm[q.y][p] || 0) + 1; }
  for (const c of classes) { const row = cm[c] || {}, n = Object.values(row).reduce((a, v) => a + v, 0); rec.push(n ? (row[c] || 0) / n : 0); }
  const n = pairs.length, ok = pairs.filter((q) => q.p === q.y).length;
  return { n, right: ok, accuracy: n ? ok / n : null, balanced: rec.length ? rec.reduce((a, v) => a + v, 0) / rec.length : null, classes, cm, none: pairs.filter((q) => q.p == null).length };
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/locmap.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool web — V0.43: core location map (figure). DOM-free: coordinate reference systems (EPSG:4326, EPSG:3857,
// UTM/WGS 84 with zone and hemisphere), map frame, geographic graticule or metric grid, edge labels (DD, DMS or
// metres), scale bar, north arrow and the SVG. Names are prefixed (lm…) because the offline build joins all modules.

const LM_D = Math.PI / 180, LM_A = 6378137, LM_F = 1 / 298.257223563;

/** Default settings of the figure (S.fig.loc). bbox = [W, S, E, N] in degrees or null (fitted to the cores). */
export const LOCMAP_DEFAULTS = {
  crs: 'EPSG:4326', utmZone: null, utmSouth: null, bbox: null, margin: 0.25, width: 900, aspect: '4:3',
  base: 'vector', baseOpacity: 1, land: '#e6e6dc', sea: '#eef4f6', coast: true,
  grid: true, gridType: 'auto', gridStep: null, gridColor: '#8a9592', gridWidth: 0.5, gridDash: true,
  labels: { top: false, bottom: true, left: true, right: false }, coordFmt: 'dd', metricUnit: 'm', rotateSide: true,
  labelSize: 10, ticks: true, textColor: '#1c2422', subColor: '#5b6763', bgColor: '#ffffff',
  scaleBar: true, scalePos: 'bl', north: true, northPos: 'tr', northSize: 1,
  symbol: 'circle', pointSize: 6, pointColor: '#c0392b', pointStroke: '#1c2422', coreLabels: true, coreLabelSize: 11,
  fmt: 'svg', scale: 3, quality: 0.92,
};

/* ── coordinate reference systems ── */

/** UTM zone (1–60) of a longitude. */
export const lmUtmZone = (lon) => Math.min(60, Math.max(1, Math.floor((((lon + 180) % 360) + 360) % 360 / 6) + 1));

/** Transverse Mercator (Krüger series to n⁴, WGS 84; sub-millimetre within the zone). */
function lmUTM(zone, south) {
  const n = LM_F / (2 - LM_F), n2 = n * n, n3 = n2 * n, n4 = n3 * n, e = (2 * Math.sqrt(n)) / (1 + n);
  const A = (LM_A / (1 + n)) * (1 + n2 / 4 + n4 / 64), k0 = 0.9996, E0 = 500000, N0 = south ? 1e7 : 0, l0 = (zone * 6 - 183) * LM_D;
  const al = [n / 2 - (2 * n2) / 3 + (5 * n3) / 16 + (41 * n4) / 180, (13 * n2) / 48 - (3 * n3) / 5 + (557 * n4) / 1440, (61 * n3) / 240 - (103 * n4) / 140, (49561 * n4) / 161280];
  const be = [n / 2 - (2 * n2) / 3 + (37 * n3) / 96 - n4 / 360, n2 / 48 + n3 / 15 - (437 * n4) / 1440, (17 * n3) / 480 - (37 * n4) / 840, (4397 * n4) / 161280];
  const de = [2 * n - (2 * n2) / 3 - 2 * n3 + (116 * n4) / 45, (7 * n2) / 3 - (8 * n3) / 5 - (227 * n4) / 45, (56 * n3) / 15 - (136 * n4) / 35, (4279 * n4) / 630];
  return {
    fwd(lon, lat) {
      let dl = lon * LM_D - l0; dl = Math.atan2(Math.sin(dl), Math.cos(dl)); dl = Math.max(-1.3, Math.min(1.3, dl));   // ±75° from the meridian at most
      const p = Math.max(-89.9, Math.min(89.9, lat)) * LM_D, s = Math.sin(p), tt = Math.sinh(Math.atanh(s) - e * Math.atanh(e * s));
      const xi = Math.atan2(tt, Math.cos(dl)), eta = Math.atanh(Math.sin(dl) / Math.sqrt(1 + tt * tt));
      let x = eta, y = xi;
      for (let j = 1; j <= 4; j++) { x += al[j - 1] * Math.cos(2 * j * xi) * Math.sinh(2 * j * eta); y += al[j - 1] * Math.sin(2 * j * xi) * Math.cosh(2 * j * eta); }
      return [E0 + k0 * A * x, N0 + k0 * A * y];
    },
    inv(E, N) {
      const xi = (N - N0) / (k0 * A), eta = (E - E0) / (k0 * A);
      let x = xi, y = eta;
      for (let j = 1; j <= 4; j++) { x -= be[j - 1] * Math.sin(2 * j * xi) * Math.cosh(2 * j * eta); y -= be[j - 1] * Math.cos(2 * j * xi) * Math.sinh(2 * j * eta); }
      const chi = Math.asin(Math.sin(x) / Math.cosh(y));
      let p = chi; for (let j = 1; j <= 4; j++) p += de[j - 1] * Math.sin(2 * j * chi);
      return [(l0 + Math.atan2(Math.sinh(y), Math.cos(x))) / LM_D, p / LM_D];
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Coordinate reference system: {id, name, epsg, geographic, unit, fwd(lon, lat) → [X, Y], inv(X, Y) → [lon, lat]}.
 * id: 'EPSG:4326' (X = lon, Y = lat, degrees), 'EPSG:3857' (Web Mercator, m) or 'UTM' with o.zone and o.south.
 */
export function lmCRS(id, o = {}) {
  if (id === 'EPSG:3857') {
    return { id, name: 'WGS 84 / Pseudo-Mercator', epsg: 3857, geographic: false, unit: 'm',
      fwd: (lon, lat) => [LM_A * lon * LM_D, LM_A * Math.log(Math.tan(Math.PI / 4 + (Math.max(-85.0511, Math.min(85.0511, lat)) * LM_D) / 2))],
      inv: (x, y) => [x / LM_A / LM_D, (2 * Math.atan(Math.exp(y / LM_A)) - Math.PI / 2) / LM_D] };
  }
  if (id === 'UTM') {
    const zone = Math.min(60, Math.max(1, Math.round(o.zone || 30))), south = !!o.south, t = lmUTM(zone, south);
    return { id, name: `WGS 84 / UTM ${zone}${south ? 'S' : 'N'}`, epsg: (south ? 32700 : 32600) + zone, geographic: false, unit: 'm', zone, south, ...t };
  }
  return { id: 'EPSG:4326', name: 'WGS 84 (geographic)', epsg: 4326, geographic: true, unit: '°', fwd: (lon, lat) => [lon, lat], inv: (x, y) => [x, y] };
}

/** Extent around the cores: [W, S, E, N] with a margin (fraction of the span, at least 0.2°). */
export function lmFitBBox(points, margin = 0.25) {
  const P = points.filter((p) => Number.isFinite(p.lat) && Number.isFinite(p.lon));
  if (!P.length) return [-12, 34, 6, 45];
  let w = Math.min(...P.map((p) => p.lon)), e = Math.max(...P.map((p) => p.lon)), s = Math.min(...P.map((p) => p.lat)), n = Math.max(...P.map((p) => p.lat));
  const mx = Math.max(0.2, (e - w) * margin), my = Math.max(0.2, (n - s) * margin);
  w -= mx; e += mx; s = Math.max(-85, s - my); n = Math.min(85, n + my);
  return [w, s, e, n].map((v) => +v.toFixed(4));
}
/** Zoom (f > 1 in) and pan (fractions of the span) an extent. */
export function lmZoom(b, f = 1, dx = 0, dy = 0) {
  const cx = (b[0] + b[2]) / 2 + dx * (b[2] - b[0]), cy = (b[1] + b[3]) / 2 + dy * (b[3] - b[1]), hw = (b[2] - b[0]) / 2 / f, hh = (b[3] - b[1]) / 2 / f;
  return [cx - hw, Math.max(-85, cy - hh), cx + hw, Math.min(85, cy + hh)].map((v) => +v.toFixed(5));
}

/**
 * Frame: projected envelope of the bbox (its edges sampled, so curved UTM edges fit), size in px and transforms.
 * aspect 'auto' keeps the projected proportion; a number (height/width) widens the envelope to that ratio.
 */
export function lmFrame(crs, bbox, width = 900, aspect = 'auto') {
  const [w, s, e, n] = bbox, xs = [], ys = [];
  for (let k = 0; k <= 32; k++) {
    const lo = w + ((e - w) * k) / 32, la = s + ((n - s) * k) / 32;
    for (const [a, b] of [[lo, s], [lo, n], [w, la], [e, la]]) { const [x, y] = crs.fwd(a, b); xs.push(x); ys.push(y); }
  }
  let X0 = Math.min(...xs), X1 = Math.max(...xs), Y0 = Math.min(...ys), Y1 = Math.max(...ys);
  const r = +aspect;
  if (r > 0) { const cur = (Y1 - Y0) / (X1 - X0 || 1); if (cur < r) { const c = (Y0 + Y1) / 2, h = ((X1 - X0) * r) / 2; Y0 = c - h; Y1 = c + h; } else { const c = (X0 + X1) / 2, h = (Y1 - Y0) / r / 2; X0 = c - h; X1 = c + h; } }
  const W = Math.round(width), H = Math.max(40, Math.round((W * (Y1 - Y0)) / (X1 - X0 || 1)));
  const toPx = (X, Y) => [((X - X0) / (X1 - X0)) * W, ((Y1 - Y) / (Y1 - Y0)) * H];
  const fromPx = (px, py) => [X0 + (px / W) * (X1 - X0), Y1 - (py / H) * (Y1 - Y0)];
  return { X0, X1, Y0, Y1, W, H, toPx, fromPx, P: (lon, lat) => toPx(...crs.fwd(lon, lat)), Pinv: (px, py) => crs.inv(...fromPx(px, py)) };
}

/* ── grid steps and label formats ── */

const LM_DEG_STEPS = [1 / 3600, 2 / 3600, 5 / 3600, 10 / 3600, 15 / 3600, 30 / 3600, 1 / 60, 2 / 60, 5 / 60, 10 / 60, 15 / 60, 20 / 60, 0.5, 1, 2, 5, 10, 15, 20, 30, 45];
/** Step of a geographic graticule: sexagesimal-friendly values (minutes and seconds) giving about n lines. */
export function lmDegStep(span, n = 5) { const raw = span / n; return LM_DEG_STEPS.find((v) => v >= raw * 0.999) || 45; }
/** Step of a metric grid: 1, 2 or 5 × 10ᵏ m giving about n lines. */
export function lmMetricStep(span, n = 5) { const raw = span / n, p = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(raw)); return [1, 2, 5, 10].map((k) => k * p).find((v) => v >= raw * 0.999); }
const lmMultiples = (a, b, st) => { const out = []; for (let v = Math.ceil(a / st - 1e-9) * st; v <= b + st * 1e-9; v += st) out.push(+v.toFixed(9)); return out; };

/** Degrees as text: fmt 'dd' (decimal, digits from the step) or 'dms'; axis 'lat' | 'lon' adds the hemisphere. */
export function lmFormatDeg(v, axis, fmt = 'dd', step = 1) {
  const hemi = axis === 'lat' ? (v > 1e-9 ? 'N' : v < -1e-9 ? 'S' : '') : (v > 1e-9 && v < 180 - 1e-9 ? 'E' : v < -1e-9 ? 'W' : ''), a = Math.abs(v);
  if (fmt === 'dms') {
    let tot = Math.round(a * 3600), d = Math.floor(tot / 3600), m = Math.floor((tot % 3600) / 60), s = tot % 60;
    if (s === 60) { s = 0; m++; } if (m === 60) { m = 0; d++; }
    const secs = step < 1 / 60 - 1e-12, mins = secs || step < 1 - 1e-12 || m || s;
    return `${d}°${mins ? String(m).padStart(2, '0') + '′' : ''}${secs ? String(s).padStart(2, '0') + '″' : ''}${hemi}`;
  }
  let dec = 0; while (dec < 6 && Math.abs(Math.round(step * 10 ** dec) - step * 10 ** dec) > 1e-6) dec++;
  return `${a.toFixed(dec)}°${hemi}`;
}
/** Metres as text with thin-space thousands (unit 'm' or 'km'). */
export function lmFormatMetric(v, unit = 'm', step = 1000) {
  if (unit === 'km') { const dec = step >= 1000 ? 0 : Math.min(3, Math.ceil(-Math.log10(step / 1000))); return (v / 1000).toFixed(dec).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ' ') + ' km'; }
  return Math.round(v).toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ' ');
}

/* ── scale bar and north arrow ── */

const lmHav = (lo1, la1, lo2, la2) => { const p1 = la1 * LM_D, p2 = la2 * LM_D, dp = p2 - p1, dl = (lo2 - lo1) * LM_D, h = Math.sin(dp / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(p1) * Math.cos(p2) * Math.sin(dl / 2) ** 2; return 2 * 6371008.8 * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h))); };
/** Ground metres per pixel at a pixel of the frame (great-circle distance across ±10 px horizontally). */
export function lmMetresPerPx(fr, px, py) { const [a, b] = fr.Pinv(px - 10, py), [c, d] = fr.Pinv(px + 10, py); return lmHav(a, b, c, d) / 20; }
/** "Nice" scale-bar length (1, 2, 5 × 10ᵏ m) of at most maxPx pixels: {m, px, label}. */
export function lmScaleBar(mpp, maxPx) {
  const raw = mpp * maxPx, p = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(raw)), m = [5, 2, 1].map((k) => k * p).find((k) => k <= raw) || p;
  return { m, px: m / mpp, label: m >= 1000 ? `${+(m / 1000).toFixed(3)} km` : `${m} m` };
}
/** Angle (degrees, clockwise from the top of the frame) of true north at a pixel; 0 in EPSG:4326/3857, the grid convergence in UTM. */
export function lmNorthAngle(fr, px, py) {
  const [lon, lat] = fr.Pinv(px, py), a = fr.P(lon, lat), b = fr.P(lon, Math.min(89.9, lat + 0.01));
  return (Math.atan2(b[0] - a[0], a[1] - b[1]) * 180) / Math.PI;
}

/* ── SVG ── */

const lmEsc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"]/g, (m) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' })[m]);
function lmSymbol(sym, x, y, r, attrs) {
  const f = (v) => v.toFixed(1);
  if (sym === 'square') return `<rect x="${f(x - r * 0.89)}" y="${f(y - r * 0.89)}" width="${f(r * 1.78)}" height="${f(r * 1.78)}" ${attrs}/>`;
  if (sym === 'triangle') return `<polygon points="${f(x)},${f(y - r * 1.15)} ${f(x + r)},${f(y + r * 0.6)} ${f(x - r)},${f(y + r * 0.6)}" ${attrs}/>`;
  if (sym === 'diamond') return `<polygon points="${f(x)},${f(y - r * 1.2)} ${f(x + r)},${f(y)} ${f(x)},${f(y + r * 1.2)} ${f(x - r)},${f(y)}" ${attrs}/>`;
  if (sym === 'star') { const p = []; for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (k * Math.PI) / 5, q = k % 2 ? r * 0.5 : r * 1.25; p.push(`${f(x + q * Math.cos(a))},${f(y + q * Math.sin(a))}`); } return `<polygon points="${p.join(' ')}" ${attrs}/>`; }
  return `<circle cx="${f(x)}" cy="${f(y)}" r="${f(r)}" ${attrs}/>`;
}
/** Where the polyline values v(t) along an edge cross level `level` (t in [0, 1]), by sampling. */
function lmCross(fn, level, n = 240) {
  const out = []; let a = fn(0) - level;
  for (let k = 1; k <= n; k++) { const b = fn(k / n) - level; if ((a <= 0 && b > 0) || (a >= 0 && b < 0)) out.push((k - 1 + Math.abs(a) / (Math.abs(a) + Math.abs(b) || 1)) / n); a = b; }
  return out;
}

/**
 * SVG of the location map. o = {settings (LOCMAP_DEFAULTS), points [{name, lat, lon}], land (rings of [lon, lat, …]),
 * bg {href, attribution} (an image of exactly the frame in the map CRS), title, labelsText {crs}}.
 * Returns {svg, frame, crs, bbox}.
 */
export function buildLocMapSVG(o) {
  const st = { ...LOCMAP_DEFAULTS, ...(o.settings || {}), labels: { ...LOCMAP_DEFAULTS.labels, ...(o.settings?.labels || {}) } };
  const pts = (o.points || []).filter((p) => Number.isFinite(p.lat) && Number.isFinite(p.lon));
  const bbox = st.bbox || lmFitBBox(pts, st.margin), cLon = (bbox[0] + bbox[2]) / 2, cLat = (bbox[1] + bbox[3]) / 2;
  const crs = lmCRS(st.crs, { zone: st.utmZone || lmUtmZone(cLon), south: st.utmSouth ?? cLat < 0 });
  const aspect = { auto: 'auto', '1:1': 1, '4:3': 0.75, '3:2': 2 / 3, '16:9': 0.5625, A4: 210 / 297, A4v: 297 / 210 }[st.aspect] ?? 'auto';
  const fr = lmFrame(crs, bbox, st.width, aspect), { W, H } = fr, fs = st.labelSize, L = st.labels;
  const geoGrid = st.gridType === 'geo' || crs.geographic;   // 'auto' and 'metric': metric grid in projected CRSs
  // margins: room for the labels on each chosen side
  const sideW = st.rotateSide ? fs + 10 : fs * (geoGrid ? 6.2 : 4.8) + 8;
  const m = { t: (L.top ? fs + 12 : 8) + (o.title ? 24 : 0), b: (L.bottom ? fs + 14 : 8) + 12, l: L.left ? sideW : 8, r: L.right ? sideW : 8 };
  const TW = W + m.l + m.r, TH = H + m.t + m.b, ox = m.l, oy = m.t, f1 = (v) => v.toFixed(1);
  const s = [`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" width="${TW}" height="${TH}" viewBox="0 0 ${TW} ${TH}" font-family="IBM Plex Sans, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs}">`,
    `<rect width="${TW}" height="${TH}" fill="${lmEsc(st.bgColor)}"/>`];
  if (o.title) s.push(`<text x="${ox}" y="18" font-size="14" font-weight="600" fill="${lmEsc(st.textColor)}">${lmEsc(o.title)}</text>`);
  s.push(`<defs><clipPath id="lmclip"><rect x="${ox}" y="${oy}" width="${W}" height="${H}"/></clipPath></defs><g clip-path="url(#lmclip)">`);
  const P = (lon, lat) => { const [x, y] = fr.P(lon, lat); return [x + ox, y + oy]; };
  const raster = !!o.bg?.href && st.base !== 'vector' && st.base !== 'blank';
  s.push(`<rect x="${ox}" y="${oy}" width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="${st.base === 'blank' ? '#ffffff' : lmEsc(st.sea)}"/>`);
  if (raster) s.push(`<image x="${ox}" y="${oy}" width="${W}" height="${H}" preserveAspectRatio="none" href="${o.bg.href}" xlink:href="${o.bg.href}" opacity="${st.baseOpacity}"/>`);
  // land (vector base) or coastline over a raster
  if ((st.base === 'vector' || (raster && st.coast)) && o.land?.length) {
    const [w, sb, e, n] = bbox, mx = (e - w) * 0.5 + 1, my = (n - sb) * 0.5 + 1;
    for (const r of o.land) {
      let inBox = false; for (let k = 0; k < r.length; k += 2) if (r[k] >= w - mx && r[k] <= e + mx && r[k + 1] >= sb - my && r[k + 1] <= n + my) { inBox = true; break; }
      if (!inBox) continue;
      let d = ''; for (let k = 0; k < r.length; k += 2) { const [x, y] = P(r[k], r[k + 1]); d += `${k ? 'L' : 'M'}${f1(x)} ${f1(y)}`; }
      s.push(st.base === 'vector' ? `<path d="${d}Z" fill="${lmEsc(st.land)}" stroke="#9a9a8a" stroke-width="0.6"/>` : `<path d="${d}Z" fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.75" stroke-width="0.7"/>`);
    }
  }
  // grid lines and where they cross each side of the frame
  const ticks = { top: [], bottom: [], left: [], right: [] };
  const gAttr = `fill="none" stroke="${lmEsc(st.gridColor)}" stroke-width="${st.gridWidth}" stroke-opacity="0.85"${st.gridDash ? ' stroke-dasharray="3 3"' : ''}`;
  if (geoGrid) {
    // geographic graticule: meridians and parallels over the whole frame (inverse-projected corners give the range)
    const cs = [[0, 0], [W, 0], [0, H], [W, H], [W / 2, 0], [W / 2, H], [0, H / 2], [W, H / 2]].map(([x, y]) => fr.Pinv(x, y));
    const lo0 = Math.min(...cs.map((c) => c[0])), lo1 = Math.max(...cs.map((c) => c[0])), la0 = Math.max(-89, Math.min(...cs.map((c) => c[1]))), la1 = Math.min(89, Math.max(...cs.map((c) => c[1])));
    const span = Math.min(lo1 - lo0, (la1 - la0) * 1.4), step = st.gridStep > 0 ? st.gridStep : st.coordFmt === 'dms' ? lmDegStep(span, 5) : lmMetricStep(span, 5);   // DD: 1, 2, 5 × 10ᵏ °
    const mer = lmMultiples(lo0, lo1, step), par = lmMultiples(la0, la1, step), N = 60;
    if (st.grid) {
      for (const v of mer) s.push(`<path d="${Array.from({ length: N + 1 }, (_, k) => { const [x, y] = P(v, la0 - 1 + ((la1 - la0 + 2) * k) / N); return `${k ? 'L' : 'M'}${f1(x)} ${f1(y)}`; }).join('')}" ${gAttr}/>`);
      for (const v of par) s.push(`<path d="${Array.from({ length: N + 1 }, (_, k) => { const [x, y] = P(lo0 - 1 + ((lo1 - lo0 + 2) * k) / N, v); return `${k ? 'L' : 'M'}${f1(x)} ${f1(y)}`; }).join('')}" ${gAttr}/>`);
    }
    for (const v of mer) {   // meridians are labelled on the top and bottom sides
      for (const t of lmCross((u) => fr.Pinv(u * W, H)[0], v)) ticks.bottom.push([t * W, lmFormatDeg(v, 'lon', st.coordFmt, step)]);
      for (const t of lmCross((u) => fr.Pinv(u * W, 0)[0], v)) ticks.top.push([t * W, lmFormatDeg(v, 'lon', st.coordFmt, step)]);
    }
    for (const v of par) {   // parallels on the left and right sides
      for (const t of lmCross((u) => fr.Pinv(0, u * H)[1], v)) ticks.left.push([t * H, lmFormatDeg(v, 'lat', st.coordFmt, step)]);
      for (const t of lmCross((u) => fr.Pinv(W, u * H)[1], v)) ticks.right.push([t * H, lmFormatDeg(v, 'lat', st.coordFmt, step)]);
    }
  } else {
    // metric grid of the projected CRS: straight lines X = const and Y = const
    const step = st.gridStep > 0 ? st.gridStep : lmMetricStep(Math.min(fr.X1 - fr.X0, (fr.Y1 - fr.Y0) * 1.4), 5);
    for (const X of lmMultiples(fr.X0, fr.X1, step)) {
      const [x] = fr.toPx(X, fr.Y0); if (st.grid) s.push(`<path d="M${f1(x + ox)} ${oy}V${oy + H}" ${gAttr}/>`);
      const txt = lmFormatMetric(X, st.metricUnit, step); ticks.top.push([x, txt]); ticks.bottom.push([x, txt]);
    }
    for (const Y of lmMultiples(fr.Y0, fr.Y1, step)) {
      const [, y] = fr.toPx(fr.X0, Y); if (st.grid) s.push(`<path d="M${ox} ${f1(y + oy)}H${ox + W}" ${gAttr}/>`);
      const txt = lmFormatMetric(Y, st.metricUnit, step); ticks.left.push([y, txt]); ticks.right.push([y, txt]);
    }
  }
  // cores
  for (const p of pts) {
    const [x, y] = P(p.lon, p.lat); if (x < ox - 20 || x > ox + W + 20 || y < oy - 20 || y > oy + H + 20) continue;
    s.push(lmSymbol(st.symbol, x, y, st.pointSize, `fill="${lmEsc(st.pointColor)}" stroke="${lmEsc(st.pointStroke)}" stroke-width="1"`));
    if (st.coreLabels) s.push(`<text x="${f1(x + st.pointSize + 3)}" y="${f1(y + st.coreLabelSize * 0.36)}" font-size="${st.coreLabelSize}" fill="${raster ? '#ffffff' : '#1c2422'}" stroke="${raster ? '#000000' : '#ffffff'}" stroke-opacity="0.65" stroke-width="3" paint-order="stroke" stroke-linejoin="round">${lmEsc(p.name)}</text>`);
  }
  const ink = raster ? '#ffffff' : '#1c2422', halo = raster ? '#000000' : '#ffffff';
  const corner = (pos, w, h, pad = 12) => [pos[1] === 'l' ? ox + pad : ox + W - w - pad, pos[0] === 't' ? oy + pad : oy + H - h - pad];
  if (st.scaleBar) {
    const sb = lmScaleBar(lmMetresPerPx(fr, W / 2, H / 2), Math.min(220, W * 0.28)), bw = sb.px, [bx, by] = corner(st.scalePos, bw, 22);
    s.push(`<g font-size="9" fill="${ink}"><rect x="${f1(bx - 4)}" y="${f1(by - 2)}" width="${f1(bw + 8)}" height="26" fill="${halo}" fill-opacity="${raster ? 0.35 : 0.7}" rx="2"/>`);
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) s.push(`<rect x="${f1(bx + (k * bw) / 4)}" y="${f1(by + 14)}" width="${f1(bw / 4)}" height="5" fill="${k % 2 ? 'none' : ink}" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="0.8"/>`);
    s.push(`<text x="${f1(bx)}" y="${f1(by + 10)}">0</text><text x="${f1(bx + bw)}" y="${f1(by + 10)}" text-anchor="end">${sb.label}</text></g>`);
  }
  if (st.north) {
    const z = 22 * st.northSize, [nx0, ny0] = corner(st.northPos, z, z * 1.6), cx = nx0 + z / 2, cy = ny0 + z * 0.9, ang = lmNorthAngle(fr, cx - ox, cy - oy);
    s.push(`<g transform="rotate(${ang.toFixed(2)} ${f1(cx)} ${f1(cy)})"><polygon points="${f1(cx)},${f1(cy - z * 0.9)} ${f1(cx - z * 0.32)},${f1(cy + z * 0.35)} ${f1(cx)},${f1(cy + z * 0.12)}" fill="${ink}"/><polygon points="${f1(cx)},${f1(cy - z * 0.9)} ${f1(cx + z * 0.32)},${f1(cy + z * 0.35)} ${f1(cx)},${f1(cy + z * 0.12)}" fill="${halo}" stroke="${ink}" stroke-width="0.8"/><text x="${f1(cx)}" y="${f1(cy - z * 0.98)}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${f1(10 * st.northSize)}" font-weight="600" fill="${ink}" stroke="${halo}" stroke-width="2.5" paint-order="stroke">N</text></g>`);
  }
  s.push('</g>');
  // frame, ticks and edge labels
  s.push(`<rect x="${ox}" y="${oy}" width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="none" stroke="#1c2422" stroke-width="1"/>`);
  const tl = st.ticks ? 5 : 0;
  for (const side of ['top', 'bottom', 'left', 'right']) {
    const show = L[side];
    for (const [p, txt] of ticks[side]) {
      if (p < 0.5 || (side === 'top' || side === 'bottom' ? p > W - 0.5 : p > H - 0.5)) continue;
      if (side === 'top' || side === 'bottom') {
        const x = ox + p, y = side === 'top' ? oy : oy + H, d = side === 'top' ? -1 : 1;
        if (st.ticks && show) s.push(`<line x1="${f1(x)}" y1="${y}" x2="${f1(x)}" y2="${y + d * tl}" stroke="#1c2422" stroke-width="0.8"/>`);
        if (show) s.push(`<text x="${f1(x)}" y="${f1(side === 'top' ? y - tl - 3 : y + tl + fs + 1)}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${lmEsc(st.textColor)}">${lmEsc(txt)}</text>`);
      } else {
        const y = oy + p, x = side === 'left' ? ox : ox + W, d = side === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
        if (st.ticks && show) s.push(`<line x1="${x}" y1="${f1(y)}" x2="${x + d * tl}" y2="${f1(y)}" stroke="#1c2422" stroke-width="0.8"/>`);
        if (!show) continue;
        const tx = x + d * (tl + 3);
        s.push(st.rotateSide
          ? `<text transform="translate(${f1(tx + (side === 'left' ? -2 : fs - 1))} ${f1(y)}) rotate(-90)" text-anchor="middle" fill="${lmEsc(st.textColor)}">${lmEsc(txt)}</text>`
          : `<text x="${f1(tx)}" y="${f1(y + fs * 0.35)}" text-anchor="${side === 'left' ? 'end' : 'start'}" fill="${lmEsc(st.textColor)}">${lmEsc(txt)}</text>`);
      }
    }
  }
  const foot = [o.labelsText?.crs ? `${o.labelsText.crs}: ${crs.name} (EPSG:${crs.epsg})` : `${crs.name} (EPSG:${crs.epsg})`, raster ? o.bg.attribution : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  s.push(`<text x="${ox + W}" y="${TH - 2}" text-anchor="end" font-size="8" fill="${lmEsc(st.subColor)}">${lmEsc(foot)}</text>`);
  s.push('</svg>');
  return { svg: s.join(''), frame: fr, crs, bbox, margins: m };
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/log.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool web — composite core log (no DOM): table parsing, axis transforms, SVG and CSV of a window.
import { interpAge, toCSV } from './core.js';

/** Parse CSV / TXT tables: delimiter , ; tab | or spaces; optional header; decimal comma; '#' comments. */
export function parseTable(text) {
  const lines = String(text || '').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').split(/\r?\n/).filter((l) => l.trim() && !/^\s*#/.test(l));
  if (!lines.length) return { headers: [], rows: [], text: [], dropped: 0, droppedCols: [] };
  const head = lines.slice(0, 6);
  // V0.38: fields between double quotes may contain the delimiter ("a, b") and "" for a quote
  const splitQ = (l, d) => {
    if (!l.includes('"')) return l.split(d);
    const out = []; let cur = '', q = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < l.length; i++) {
      const ch = l[i];
      if (q) { if (ch === '"') { if (l[i + 1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; } else q = false; } else cur += ch; }
      else if (ch === '"' && !cur.trim()) { q = true; cur = ''; }
      else if (l.startsWith(d, i)) { out.push(cur); cur = ''; i += d.length - 1; }
      else cur += ch;
    }
    out.push(cur); return out;
  };
  // the delimiter splits the first line and all but at most one of the next lines (a stray last line is tolerated)
  const delim = ['\t', ';', ',', '|'].find((d) => splitQ(head[0], d).length > 1 && head.filter((l) => splitQ(l, d).length > 1).length >= head.length - (head.length > 2 ? 1 : 0)) || /\s+/;
  const split = (l) => (delim instanceof RegExp ? l.trim().split(delim) : splitQ(l, delim)).map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"(.*)"$/, '$1'));
  const sample = lines.slice(0, 30).flatMap(split);   // V0.38: "1,5" means decimal comma; "1,234.5" means thousands
  const decimalComma = delim !== ',' && sample.some((c) => /^[-+]?\d+,\d+$/.test(c) || /^[-+]?\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+,\d+$/.test(c)) && !sample.some((c) => /^[-+]?\d{1,3}(,\d{3})+\.\d+$/.test(c));
  const num = (c) => {
    if (c == null || c === '') return null;
    let x = c.replace(/^\+/, '');
    // V0.38: thousands separators ("1.234,5" with decimal comma, "1,234.5" otherwise) and spaces inside numbers
    if (decimalComma && /^-?\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+(,\d+)?$/.test(x)) x = x.replace(/\./g, '');
    else if (!decimalComma && delim !== ',' && /^-?\d{1,3}(,\d{3})+(\.\d+)?$/.test(x)) x = x.replace(/,/g, '');
    if (/^-?\d{1,3}( \d{3})+([.,]\d+)?$/.test(x)) x = x.replace(/ /g, '');
    const v = Number(decimalComma ? x.replace(',', '.') : x);
    return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null;
  };
  const first = split(lines[0]);
  const hasHeader = first.some((c) => c !== '' && num(c) === null);
  const headers = hasHeader ? first : first.map((_, i) => `col${i + 1}`);
  const body = (hasHeader ? lines.slice(1) : lines).map(split);
  const rows = body.map((c) => headers.map((_, i) => num(c[i])));
  const cells = body.map((c) => headers.map((_, i) => c[i] ?? ''));
  // V0.38: cells with text in mostly numeric columns are read as empty: counted so the interface can warn
  let dropped = 0; const droppedCols = [];
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    const filled = cells.filter((r) => r[i] !== ''), ok = rows.filter((r) => r[i] != null).length, bad = filled.length - ok;
    if (bad > 0 && ok >= bad) { dropped += bad; droppedCols.push(h); }
  });
  const short = body.filter((c) => c.length < headers.length).length;
  return { headers, rows, text: cells, dropped, droppedCols, short };
}

/** V0.38: text of a file, as UTF-8 or, if it is not valid UTF-8 (e.g. Excel CSV on Windows), as Windows-1252. */
export function decodeText(buf) {
  const b = buf instanceof Uint8Array ? buf : new Uint8Array(buf);
  try { return new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(b); } catch { return new TextDecoder('windows-1252').decode(b); }
}

/** Guess which column holds depth/age and which axis it is. */
export function guessAxisColumn(headers) {
  const i = headers.findIndex((h) => /age|edad|\bka\b|\bma\b|yr|años|cal/i.test(h));
  const j = headers.findIndex((h) => /depth|prof|mbsf|csf|ccsf|mcd|\bm\b|cm/i.test(h));
  if (j >= 0) return { col: j, axis: 'depth' };
  if (i >= 0) return { col: i, axis: 'age' };
  return { col: 0, axis: 'depth' };
}

export function niceTicks(a, b, n = 5) {
  if (!(b > a)) return [a];
  const raw = (b - a) / n, mag = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(raw));
  const st = [1, 2, 2.5, 5, 10].map((m) => m * mag).find((s) => s >= raw);
  const out = [];
  for (let v = Math.ceil(a / st - 1e-9) * st; v <= b + st * 1e-9; v += st) out.push(+v.toFixed(10));
  return out;
}

/** Vertical ticks in displayed units, shared by column and publication figures. */
export function verticalTicks(from, to, height, style = {}) {
  const f = +style.yFactor || 1, step = +style.yStep;
  if (step > 0) {
    const out = [], a = Math.ceil(from * f / step - 1e-9), b = Math.floor(to * f / step + 1e-9);
    for (let k = a; k <= b && out.length < 400; k++) out.push(k * step / f || 0);
    return out;
  }
  return niceTicks(from * f, to * f, style.yDiv > 0 ? style.yDiv : Math.max(3, Math.round(height / 70))).map((v) => v / f);
}

/**
 * V0.22: extra ticks of the vertical axis without labels. Steps are in displayed units (× yFactor):
 * yMajor = long ticks, yMedium = medium ticks (V0.69), yMinor = short ticks (none by default). Where several coincide the
 * highest level wins: labelled > long > medium > short. Lengths: yMajorLen, yMediumLen, yMinorLen. Returns [{ v, len }] in axis units,
 * skipping positions that already carry a labelled tick (or a long tick, for the short ones).
 */
export function extraTicks(from, to, height, style = {}) {
  const f = +style.yFactor || 1, out = [], lab = verticalTicks(from, to, height, style), eps = Math.abs(to - from) * 1e-7 + 1e-12;
  const near = (list, v) => list.some((u) => Math.abs(u - v) < eps);
  const steps = (step) => { const r = []; if (!(step > 0)) return r; const a = Math.ceil(Math.min(from, to) * f / step - 1e-9), b = Math.floor(Math.max(from, to) * f / step + 1e-9); if (b - a > 4000) return r; for (let k = a; k <= b; k++) r.push(k * step / f || 0); return r; };
  const major = steps(+style.yMajor).filter((v) => !near(lab, v));
  const medium = steps(+style.yMedium).filter((v) => !near(lab, v) && !near(major, v));
  const minor = steps(+style.yMinor).filter((v) => !near(lab, v) && !near(major, v) && !near(medium, v));
  const len = (k, d) => (+style[k] > 0 ? +style[k] : d);
  for (const v of major) out.push({ v, len: len('yMajorLen', 6) });
  for (const v of medium) out.push({ v, len: len('yMediumLen', 4.5) });
  for (const v of minor) out.push({ v, len: len('yMinorLen', 3) });
  return out;
}
const labelLen = (st) => (+st?.yLabelLen > 0 ? +st.yLabelLen : 6);

/** V0.33: reorder (st.colOrder) and hide (st.colHide) columns in place, keeping the first `keep` columns fixed. */
export function orderCols(cols, st = {}, keep = 0) {
  const ord = st.colOrder || [], hide = new Set(st.colHide || []), rank = (c) => { const k = ord.indexOf(c.id); return k < 0 ? 1e6 : k; };
  const body = cols.slice(keep).map((c, i) => ({ c, i })).filter(({ c }) => !hide.has(c.id)).sort((a, b) => rank(a.c) - rank(b.c) || a.i - b.i).map(({ c }) => c);
  cols.splice(keep, cols.length - keep, ...body);
  return cols;
}

export function niceRange(min, max) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(min) || !Number.isFinite(max)) return [0, 1];
  if (min === max) { min -= Math.abs(min) * 0.1 || 1; max += Math.abs(max) * 0.1 || 1; }
  const t = niceTicks(min, max, 4), st = t.length > 1 ? t[1] - t[0] : 1;
  return [Math.floor(min / st + 1e-9) * st, Math.ceil(max / st - 1e-9) * st];
}

/** Age model swapped to age → depth (assumes ages increase monotonically with depth). */
export function invertModel(m) {
  if (!m) return null;
  const p = m.depth.map((d, i) => [m.age[i], d]).sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
  return { depth: p.map((x) => x[0]), age: p.map((x) => x[1]) };
}

/** Convert a value from one axis (depth/age) to another with the age model; null if impossible. */
export function toAxis(v, from, to, model, inverse = invertModel(model)) {
  if (v == null) return null;
  if (from === to) return v;
  if (!model) return null;
  return from === 'depth' ? interpAge(model, v) : interpAge(inverse, v);
}

/**
 * Interval rows of all sections on the chosen axis.
 * sections: [{name, bins}] with bins in common depth units (dTop, dBottom, depth, pct, classPct, BI, ii, BIm, iim).
 */
export function compositeRows(sections, { axis, model }) {
  const rows = [];
  for (const s of sections) {
    for (const b of s.bins) {
      const age = interpAge(model, b.depth);
      let a0 = b.dTop, a1 = b.dBottom, c = b.depth;
      if (axis === 'age') {
        a0 = interpAge(model, b.dTop); a1 = interpAge(model, b.dBottom); c = age;
        if (a0 == null || a1 == null) continue;
      }
      rows.push({ ...b, section: s.name, a0: Math.min(a0, a1), a1: Math.max(a0, a1), c, age });
    }
  }
  return rows.sort((p, q) => p.a0 - q.a0);
}

export function rowsRange(rows) {
  if (!rows.length) return [0, 1];
  return [Math.min(...rows.map((r) => r.a0)), Math.max(...rows.map((r) => r.a1))];
}

/** Identification marker: its reference point remains at the trace depth. */
export function traceSymbolPath(kind = 'triDown') {
  if (kind === 'circle') return 'M4.5 -4a4.5 4.5 0 1 0 -9 0a4.5 4.5 0 1 0 9 0';
  if (kind === 'square') return 'M-4 -8h8v8h-8z';
  if (kind === 'diamond') return 'M0 -9L5 -4L0 1L-5 -4z';
  if (kind === 'tri') return 'M0 -8L4.5 0h-9z';
  if (kind === 'cross') return 'M-5 -7L-3 -9L0 -6L3 -9L5 -7L2 -4L5 -1L3 1L0 -2L-3 1L-5 -1L-2 -4z';
  if (kind === 'star') return Array.from({length:10}, (_, i) => { const a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * Math.PI / 5, r = i % 2 ? 2.2 : 5.5; return `${i ? 'L' : 'M'}${f2(r * Math.cos(a))} ${f2(-4 + r * Math.sin(a))}`; }).join('') + 'z';
  return 'M0 0l-4.5 -8h9z';
}
let SYM = { k: 1, stroke: '#ffffff', sw: 0.8 };   // V0.23: marker size and border of the current figure
const traceSymbol = (x, y, color, kind) => `<path transform="translate(${f2(x)} ${f2(y)})${SYM.k !== 1 ? ` scale(${f2(SYM.k)})` : ''}" d="${traceSymbolPath(kind)}" fill="${color}" stroke="${SYM.stroke}" stroke-width="${SYM.sw}"/>`;
/** V0.23: style of NO DATA intervals: show, fill (hatch / solid / none), colours and label. */
export const ND_STYLE = { ndShow: true, ndFill: 'hatch', ndColor: '#f2f2f0', ndLine: '#b9bdbb', ndLabel: true, ndText: '', ndImage: false };
export function noDataDefs(st) {
  const q = { ...ND_STYLE, ...(st || {}) };
  return `<pattern id="nodata" width="6" height="6" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" patternTransform="rotate(45)"><rect width="6" height="6" fill="${q.ndColor}"/>${q.ndFill === 'hatch' ? `<line x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="6" stroke="${q.ndLine}" stroke-width="1.6"/>` : ''}</pattern>`;
}
/** NO DATA rectangle (and label) for one column; '' when hidden. */
export function noDataRect(st, x, w, ya, h, text) {
  const q = { ...ND_STYLE, ...(st || {}) };
  if (!q.ndShow || q.ndFill === 'none' || h <= 0) return '';
  let r = `<rect x="${f2(x)}" y="${f2(ya)}" width="${f2(w)}" height="${f2(Math.max(0.4, h))}" fill="url(#nodata)"/>`;
  if (q.ndLabel && h >= 12 && w >= 44) r += `<text x="${f2(x + w / 2)}" y="${f2(ya + h / 2 + 3)}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="8.5" font-weight="600" fill="#6b7471">${escXml(q.ndText || text || 'NO DATA')}</text>`;
  return r;
}
/** Intervals without data among rows (merged when contiguous). */
export function noDataGaps(rows) {
  const gaps = [];
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.pct != null) continue;
    const g = gaps[gaps.length - 1];
    if (g && Math.abs(r.a0 - g.a1) < 1e-9) g.a1 = r.a1; else gaps.push({ a0: r.a0, a1: r.a1 });
  }
  return gaps;
}
const escXml = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (m) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' })[m]);
const f2 = (v) => +(+v).toFixed(2);
const lbl = (v) => String(+(+v).toPrecision(6));

/** Split a track name into at most two lines of about w px (10 px font); the second line ends in … if cut. */
export function wrapTitle(text, w) {
  // V0.27: long names also break after "_", "-" or "/" (magnetic_ / susceptibility_IU)
  const max = Math.max(4, Math.floor((w - 4) / 5.6)), parts = String(text ?? '').match(/\s*[^\s_\-/]+[_\-/]*|\s*[_\-/]+/g) || [], lines = [''];
  for (const raw of parts) {
    const sp = /^\s/.test(raw), wd = raw.trim(), cur = lines[lines.length - 1], next = cur ? cur + (sp ? ' ' : '') + wd : wd;
    if (next.length <= max || !cur) lines[lines.length - 1] = next; else lines.push(wd);
  }
  const out = lines.slice(0, 2).map((l) => (l.length > max ? l.slice(0, max - 1) + '…' : l));
  if (lines.length > 2) out[1] = (out[1].length >= max ? out[1].slice(0, max - 1) : out[1]) + '…';
  return out;
}

/**
 * Build the composite log as an SVG string.
 * o = { title, subtitle, axisLabel, from, to, height, rows, classes:[{name,color,index}],
 *       tracks:{image,total,species,BI,ii,manual}, image:{href}|null, imageWidth,
 *       records:[{name, color, reverse, pts:[[axisValue, y], ...]}], boundaries:[{a,label}], labels:{...},
 *       bands:[{title, full, key, w, segs:[{a0,a1,color,opacity}]}] (key: stable id for widths and clicks),
 *       marks:[{a, xf, color, abbr}] (identified traces: triangle on the image at relative x xf),
 *       idList:[{a0, a1, items:[{name, abbr, color, plain}]}] (identified ichnotaxa per interval, column at the right),
 *       respectTaxonColumnVisibility: boolean (keep style.colHide separate from taxa.hidden; hide columns and legend together),
 *       legendClasses:[{name, color}] (ichnotaxa legend, always drawn when given), bandLegend, shade:[{a0,a1,color}] (class colours behind the record curves) }
 */
/**
 * V0.5: style of the column and its figure. yFactor converts axis values to the displayed unit (e.g. m → cm: 100);
 * yStep (displayed units) or yDiv (approximate number of divisions) set the vertical ticks; pctDiv the divisions of
 * the 0–100 % axes; totalStyle: 'barsline' | 'bars' | 'line' | 'points'; frame/grid/boundaries on or off; custom
 * titles for the bioturbation and ichnotaxa columns; fontSize and whether title/subtitle are drawn.
 */
/**
 * Resolve new presentation options without changing the defaults of existing figures.
 * @param {Partial<{layout:string, abundance:string, occurrence:string, source:string, opacity:number, scale:string, values:boolean, hidden:string[]}>} [value]
 * @param {{range?:boolean, style?:{speciesMode?:string, speciesHide?:string[], colHide?:string[]}, display?:string, source?:string}} [context]
 */
export function taxaOptions(value = {}, { range = false, style = {}, display = 'symbols', source = 'all' } = {}) {
  const kite = display === 'kite' || display === 'kiteSymbols';
  const defaults = { layout: range ? 'separate' : style.speciesMode || 'stack',
    abundance: range ? (kite ? 'kite' : ['bars', 'both'].includes(display) ? 'bars' : 'none') : 'bars',
