// SedCoreTool web — scientific plots of the Data tab (V21), in the spirit of Golden Software Grapher. No DOM.
// XY plots (line, scatter, line + symbols, step, area, bars; secondary Y axis; log or inverted axes; curve fits with
// equation and R²) and stratigraphic plots (one panel per variable sharing a vertical depth/age axis).

const esc3 = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"]/g, (m) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' })[m]);
export const PLOT_TYPES = ['line', 'scatter', 'linesym', 'step', 'area', 'bar'];
export const FIT_KINDS = ['none', 'linear', 'poly', 'exp', 'power', 'log', 'movavg'];
export const PLOT_COLORS = ['#1f6fb2', '#c0392b', '#2e7d4f', '#8e44ad', '#d68910', '#16a085', '#7f8c8d', '#c2185b', '#5d4037', '#283593'];

/* ───────── statistics and fits ───────── */
const okv = (v) => v != null && Number.isFinite(v);
export function xyPairs(x, y) { const out = []; for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(x.length, y.length); i++) if (okv(x[i]) && okv(y[i])) out.push([x[i], y[i]]); return out; }
export function plotStats(v) {
  const a = v.filter(okv), n = a.length; if (!n) return { n: 0 };
  const m = a.reduce((s, q) => s + q, 0) / n, sd = n > 1 ? Math.sqrt(a.reduce((s, q) => s + (q - m) ** 2, 0) / (n - 1)) : 0;
  const so = [...a].sort((p, q) => p - q), med = n % 2 ? so[(n - 1) / 2] : (so[n / 2 - 1] + so[n / 2]) / 2;
  return { n, mean: m, sd, min: so[0], max: so[n - 1], median: med };
}
export function pearson(x, y) {
  const p = xyPairs(x, y); if (p.length < 3) return null;
  const mx = p.reduce((s, q) => s + q[0], 0) / p.length, my = p.reduce((s, q) => s + q[1], 0) / p.length;
  let sxy = 0, sxx = 0, syy = 0; for (const [a, b] of p) { sxy += (a - mx) * (b - my); sxx += (a - mx) ** 2; syy += (b - my) ** 2; }
  return sxx && syy ? sxy / Math.sqrt(sxx * syy) : null;
}
/** Least squares polynomial of degree d (normal equations, Gaussian elimination). */
function polyfit(p, d) {
  const m = d + 1, A = Array.from({ length: m }, () => new Array(m + 1).fill(0));
  for (const [x, y] of p) for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) { for (let j = 0; j < m; j++) A[i][j] += x ** (i + j); A[i][m] += y * x ** i; }
  for (let c = 0; c < m; c++) {
    let r = c; for (let k = c + 1; k < m; k++) if (Math.abs(A[k][c]) > Math.abs(A[r][c])) r = k;
    [A[c], A[r]] = [A[r], A[c]]; if (Math.abs(A[c][c]) < 1e-14) return null;
    for (let k = 0; k < m; k++) if (k !== c) { const f = A[k][c] / A[c][c]; for (let j = c; j <= m; j++) A[k][j] -= f * A[c][j]; }
  }
  return A.map((r, i) => r[m] / r[i]);
}
const r2of = (p, f) => { const my = p.reduce((s, q) => s + q[1], 0) / p.length; let ss = 0, sr = 0; for (const [x, y] of p) { ss += (y - my) ** 2; sr += (y - f(x)) ** 2; } return ss ? 1 - sr / ss : null; };
const fmtN = (v) => (Math.abs(v) >= 1e4 || (Math.abs(v) < 1e-3 && v !== 0) ? v.toExponential(3) : +v.toPrecision(4));
/**
 * Curve fit. kind: linear | poly (deg) | exp (y = a·e^(b·x), fitted on ln y) | power (y = a·x^b, on ln x, ln y) |
 * log (y = a + b·ln x) | movavg (centred moving average of `window` points, along x). Returns {fn, eq, r2, n} or null.
 */
export function fitCurve(x, y, kind, { deg = 2, window = 5 } = {}) {
  let p = xyPairs(x, y).sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]); if (p.length < 3) return null;
  if (kind === 'linear' || kind === 'poly') {
    const d = kind === 'linear' ? 1 : Math.max(2, Math.min(6, deg)), c = polyfit(p, d); if (!c) return null;
    const fn = (t) => c.reduce((s, k, i) => s + k * t ** i, 0);
    const eq = 'y = ' + c.map((k, i) => (i === 0 ? fmtN(k) : `${k < 0 ? '−' : '+'} ${fmtN(Math.abs(k))}${i === 1 ? 'x' : `x^${i}`}`)).join(' ');
    return { fn, eq, r2: r2of(p, fn), n: p.length, coef: c };
  }
  if (kind === 'exp') { const q = p.filter((r) => r[1] > 0); const c = polyfit(q.map(([a, b]) => [a, Math.log(b)]), 1); if (!c) return null; const A = Math.exp(c[0]), B = c[1], fn = (t) => A * Math.exp(B * t); return { fn, eq: `y = ${fmtN(A)}·e^(${fmtN(B)}x)`, r2: r2of(q, fn), n: q.length, coef: [A, B] }; }
  if (kind === 'power') { const q = p.filter((r) => r[0] > 0 && r[1] > 0); const c = polyfit(q.map(([a, b]) => [Math.log(a), Math.log(b)]), 1); if (!c) return null; const A = Math.exp(c[0]), B = c[1], fn = (t) => A * t ** B; return { fn, eq: `y = ${fmtN(A)}·x^${fmtN(B)}`, r2: r2of(q, fn), n: q.length, coef: [A, B] }; }
  if (kind === 'log') { const q = p.filter((r) => r[0] > 0); const c = polyfit(q.map(([a, b]) => [Math.log(a), b]), 1); if (!c) return null; const fn = (t) => c[0] + c[1] * Math.log(t); return { fn, eq: `y = ${fmtN(c[0])} ${c[1] < 0 ? '−' : '+'} ${fmtN(Math.abs(c[1]))}·ln x`, r2: r2of(q, fn), n: q.length, coef: c }; }
  if (kind === 'movavg') {
    const h = Math.floor(Math.max(2, window) / 2), sm = p.map((_, i) => { const w = p.slice(Math.max(0, i - h), i + h + 1); return [p[i][0], w.reduce((s, r) => s + r[1], 0) / w.length]; });
    return { pts: sm, eq: `movavg(${2 * h + 1})`, r2: null, n: p.length };
  }
  return null;
}

/* ───────── axes ───────── */
export function plotStep(span, n = 6) { const raw = span / n || 1, p = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(raw)); return [1, 2, 2.5, 5, 10].map((k) => k * p).find((k) => k >= raw) || raw; }
function ticksOf(lo, hi, log, n = 6) {
  if (log) { const out = []; for (let e = Math.floor(Math.log10(lo)); e <= Math.ceil(Math.log10(hi)); e++) for (const m of [1, 2, 5]) { const v = m * 10 ** e; if (v >= lo * 0.999 && v <= hi * 1.001) out.push(v); } return out; }
  const st = plotStep(hi - lo, n), out = []; for (let v = Math.ceil(lo / st) * st; v <= hi + st * 1e-9; v += st) out.push(+v.toPrecision(12)); return out;
}
/** Axis range from data (with 4 % padding) unless min/max are given. */
export function axisRange(vals, ax = {}) {
  let v = vals.filter(okv); if (ax.log) v = v.filter((q) => q > 0);
  let lo = okv(ax.min) ? ax.min : Math.min(...v), hi = okv(ax.max) ? ax.max : Math.max(...v);
  if (!v.length && !(okv(ax.min) && okv(ax.max))) return [0, 1];
  if (lo === hi) { lo -= 1; hi += 1; }
  if (!ax.log) { const pad = (hi - lo) * 0.04; if (!okv(ax.min)) lo -= pad; if (!okv(ax.max)) hi += pad; } else if (lo <= 0) lo = hi / 1000;
  return [lo, hi];
}
const scaler = (lo, hi, a, b, log, inv) => { const f = log ? (v) => (Math.log10(v) - Math.log10(lo)) / (Math.log10(hi) - Math.log10(lo)) : (v) => (v - lo) / (hi - lo); return (v) => (inv ? b - f(v) * (b - a) : a + f(v) * (b - a)); };
const fmtT = (v) => (Math.abs(v) >= 1e5 || (Math.abs(v) < 1e-3 && v !== 0) ? v.toExponential(1) : +v.toPrecision(6));

function plotSymbol(kind, x, y, r, col) {
  if (kind === 'square') return `<rect x="${(x - r).toFixed(1)}" y="${(y - r).toFixed(1)}" width="${2 * r}" height="${2 * r}" fill="${col}"/>`;
  if (kind === 'triangle') return `<polygon points="${x},${(y - r * 1.2).toFixed(1)} ${(x - r).toFixed(1)},${(y + r * 0.8).toFixed(1)} ${(x + r).toFixed(1)},${(y + r * 0.8).toFixed(1)}" fill="${col}"/>`;
  if (kind === 'diamond') return `<polygon points="${x},${(y - r * 1.3).toFixed(1)} ${(x + r).toFixed(1)},${y} ${x},${(y + r * 1.3).toFixed(1)} ${(x - r).toFixed(1)},${y}" fill="${col}"/>`;
  return `<circle cx="${x.toFixed(1)}" cy="${y.toFixed(1)}" r="${r}" fill="${col}"/>`;
}
const SYMS = ['circle', 'square', 'triangle', 'diamond'];

/** One series drawn with scale functions sx, sy (data → px). Series {x, y, type, color, width, size, symbol, dash, base}. */
function drawSeries(se, sx, sy, horizontal = false) {
  const P = xyPairs(se.x, se.y), out = [], col = se.color, w = se.width ?? 1.4, r = se.size ?? 2.6;
  if (!P.length) return '';
  const pt = ([a, b]) => (horizontal ? [sx(b), sy(a)] : [sx(a), sy(b)]); // stratigraphic panels: x = value, y = depth
  const sorted = [...P].sort((p, q) => p[0] - q[0]), dash = se.dash ? ` stroke-dasharray="${se.dash}"` : '';
  const path = (pts) => pts.map((p, k) => `${k ? 'L' : 'M'}${p[0].toFixed(1)} ${p[1].toFixed(1)}`).join('');
  if (se.type === 'area') {
    const base = horizontal ? sx(se.base ?? 0) : sy(se.base ?? 0), pts = sorted.map(pt);
    const poly = horizontal ? [[base, pts[0][1]], ...pts, [base, pts[pts.length - 1][1]]] : [[pts[0][0], base], ...pts, [pts[pts.length - 1][0], base]];
    out.push(`<path d="${path(poly)}Z" fill="${col}" fill-opacity="0.35" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${w}"/>`);
  } else if (se.type === 'bar') {
    const n = sorted.length, span = sorted.length > 1 ? (sorted[n - 1][0] - sorted[0][0]) / (n - 1) : 1;
    for (const [a, b] of sorted) {
      if (horizontal) { const y1 = sy(a - span * 0.4), y2 = sy(a + span * 0.4), x0 = sx(se.base ?? 0), x1 = sx(b); out.push(`<rect x="${Math.min(x0, x1).toFixed(1)}" y="${Math.min(y1, y2).toFixed(1)}" width="${Math.abs(x1 - x0).toFixed(1)}" height="${Math.max(0.5, Math.abs(y2 - y1)).toFixed(1)}" fill="${col}" fill-opacity="0.8"/>`); }
      else { const x1 = sx(a - span * 0.4), x2 = sx(a + span * 0.4), y0 = sy(se.base ?? 0), y1 = sy(b); out.push(`<rect x="${Math.min(x1, x2).toFixed(1)}" y="${Math.min(y0, y1).toFixed(1)}" width="${Math.max(0.5, Math.abs(x2 - x1)).toFixed(1)}" height="${Math.abs(y1 - y0).toFixed(1)}" fill="${col}" fill-opacity="0.8"/>`); }
    }
  } else if (se.type === 'step') {
    const pts = []; sorted.forEach(([a, b], k) => { if (k) pts.push(pt([a, sorted[k - 1][1]])); pts.push(pt([a, b])); });
    out.push(`<path d="${path(pts)}" fill="none" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${w}"${dash}/>`);
  } else if (se.type !== 'scatter') {
    // a gap larger than 5× the median spacing breaks the line (no line across missing intervals)
    const dx = sorted.slice(1).map((p, k) => p[0] - sorted[k][0]).sort((a, b) => a - b), med = dx[dx.length >> 1] || 0;
    let seg = [];
    sorted.forEach((p, k) => { if (k && med && p[0] - sorted[k - 1][0] > 5 * med) { if (seg.length > 1) out.push(`<path d="${path(seg)}" fill="none" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${w}"${dash}/>`); seg = []; } seg.push(pt(p)); });
    if (seg.length > 1) out.push(`<path d="${path(seg)}" fill="none" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${w}"${dash}/>`);
  }
  if (se.type === 'scatter' || se.type === 'linesym') for (const p of P) { const [a, b] = pt(p); out.push(plotSymbol(se.symbol || 'circle', a, b, r, col)); }
  return out.join('');
}

function drawFit(se, fit, sx, sy, lo, hi, horizontal = false) {
  if (!fit) return '';
  let pts;
  if (fit.pts) pts = fit.pts; else { pts = []; for (let k = 0; k <= 120; k++) { const t = lo + ((hi - lo) * k) / 120, v = fit.fn(t); if (okv(v)) pts.push([t, v]); } }
  const d = pts.map((p, k) => { const [a, b] = horizontal ? [sx(p[1]), sy(p[0])] : [sx(p[0]), sy(p[1])]; return `${k ? 'L' : 'M'}${a.toFixed(1)} ${b.toFixed(1)}`; }).join('');
  return `<path d="${d}" fill="none" stroke="${se.color}" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-dasharray="6 3" opacity="0.9"/>`;
}

/**
 * XY plot. spec = {title, width, height, x: axis, y: axis, y2: axis|null, series: [{name, x, y, type, color, symbol, y2, fit, deg, window}],
 * legend, grid, font}. axis = {label, min, max, log, invert}. Returns {svg, fits: [{name, eq, r2, n}], stats}.
 */
export function buildXYPlot(spec) {
  const W = spec.width || 760, H = spec.height || 480, fs = spec.font || 11;
  const hasY2 = spec.series.some((s) => s.y2), L = 64, R = hasY2 ? 66 : 20, T = spec.title ? 34 : 14, B = 48 + (spec.legend !== false ? 18 * Math.ceil(spec.series.length / 4) : 0);
  const pw = W - L - R, ph = H - T - B;
  const xr = axisRange(spec.series.flatMap((s) => s.x), spec.x), yr = axisRange(spec.series.filter((s) => !s.y2).flatMap((s) => s.y), spec.y), y2r = hasY2 ? axisRange(spec.series.filter((s) => s.y2).flatMap((s) => s.y), spec.y2 || {}) : null;
  const sx = scaler(xr[0], xr[1], L, L + pw, spec.x?.log, spec.x?.invert), sy = scaler(yr[0], yr[1], T + ph, T, spec.y?.log, spec.y?.invert);
  const sy2 = hasY2 ? scaler(y2r[0], y2r[1], T + ph, T, spec.y2?.log, spec.y2?.invert) : null;
  const s = [`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" font-family="IBM Plex Sans, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs}"><rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="#fff"/>`];
  if (spec.title) s.push(`<text x="${W / 2}" y="20" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${fs + 3}" font-weight="600">${esc3(spec.title)}</text>`);
  const xt = ticksOf(xr[0], xr[1], spec.x?.log), yt = ticksOf(yr[0], yr[1], spec.y?.log);
  if (spec.grid !== false) { for (const v of xt) s.push(`<line x1="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y1="${T}" x2="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y2="${T + ph}" stroke="#e3e7e6"/>`); for (const v of yt) s.push(`<line x1="${L}" y1="${sy(v).toFixed(1)}" x2="${L + pw}" y2="${sy(v).toFixed(1)}" stroke="#e3e7e6"/>`); }
  s.push(`<defs><clipPath id="pc"><rect x="${L}" y="${T}" width="${pw}" height="${ph}"/></clipPath></defs><g clip-path="url(#pc)">`);
  const fits = [], st = [];
  spec.series.forEach((se, i) => {
    const color = se.color || PLOT_COLORS[i % PLOT_COLORS.length], ser = { ...se, color, symbol: se.symbol || SYMS[i % SYMS.length] }, ys = se.y2 ? sy2 : sy;
    s.push(drawSeries(ser, sx, ys));
    if (se.fit && se.fit !== 'none') { const f = fitCurve(se.x, se.y, se.fit, { deg: se.deg, window: se.window }); if (f) { s.push(drawFit(ser, f, sx, ys, xr[0], xr[1])); fits.push({ name: se.name, eq: f.eq, r2: f.r2, n: f.n }); } }
    st.push({ name: se.name, ...plotStats(se.y), r: pearson(se.x, se.y) });
  });
  s.push('</g>');
  s.push(`<rect x="${L}" y="${T}" width="${pw}" height="${ph}" fill="none" stroke="#1c2422" stroke-width="0.8"/>`);
  for (const v of xt) s.push(`<line x1="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y1="${T + ph}" x2="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y2="${T + ph + 4}" stroke="#1c2422"/><text x="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y="${T + ph + 16}" text-anchor="middle">${fmtT(v)}</text>`);
  for (const v of yt) s.push(`<line x1="${L - 4}" y1="${sy(v).toFixed(1)}" x2="${L}" y2="${sy(v).toFixed(1)}" stroke="#1c2422"/><text x="${L - 6}" y="${(sy(v) + 3.5).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="end">${fmtT(v)}</text>`);
  if (hasY2) for (const v of ticksOf(y2r[0], y2r[1], spec.y2?.log)) s.push(`<line x1="${L + pw}" y1="${sy2(v).toFixed(1)}" x2="${L + pw + 4}" y2="${sy2(v).toFixed(1)}" stroke="#1c2422"/><text x="${L + pw + 6}" y="${(sy2(v) + 3.5).toFixed(1)}">${fmtT(v)}</text>`);
  s.push(`<text x="${L + pw / 2}" y="${T + ph + 34}" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600">${esc3(spec.x?.label)}</text>`);
  s.push(`<text transform="translate(16 ${T + ph / 2}) rotate(-90)" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600">${esc3(spec.y?.label)}</text>`);
  if (hasY2) s.push(`<text transform="translate(${W - 12} ${T + ph / 2}) rotate(90)" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600">${esc3(spec.y2?.label)}</text>`);
  if (spec.legend !== false) spec.series.forEach((se, i) => {
    const col = se.color || PLOT_COLORS[i % PLOT_COLORS.length], x = L + (i % 4) * (pw / 4), y = T + ph + 50 + 18 * Math.floor(i / 4);
    s.push(`<line x1="${x}" y1="${y - 4}" x2="${x + 18}" y2="${y - 4}" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="2"/>${se.type === 'scatter' || se.type === 'linesym' ? plotSymbol(se.symbol || SYMS[i % SYMS.length], x + 9, y - 4, 3, col) : ''}<text x="${x + 24}" y="${y}">${esc3(se.name)}${se.y2 ? ' (Y2)' : ''}</text>`);
  });
  s.push('</svg>');
  return { svg: s.join(''), fits, stats: st };
}

/**
 * Stratigraphic plot: one panel per variable, all sharing the vertical axis (depth or age, downwards).
 * spec = {title, width, height, v: axis (vertical), panels: [{label, series: [{name, x: depth/age, y: value, type, color}], log, min, max, fit}]}.
 */
export function buildStratPlot(spec) {
  const np = Math.max(1, spec.panels.length), H = spec.height || 640, fs = spec.font || 11, L = 64, T = (spec.title ? 34 : 14) + 34, B = 26 + (spec.legend !== false ? 18 : 0);
  const pw = spec.panelWidth || 150, gap = 14, W = Math.max(spec.width || 0, L + np * (pw + gap) + 10), ph = H - T - B;
  const all = spec.panels.flatMap((p) => p.series.flatMap((se) => se.x));
  const vr = axisRange(all, spec.v), inv = spec.v?.invert !== false;
  const sv = scaler(vr[0], vr[1], T, T + ph, false, !inv); // depth grows downwards
  const s = [`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" font-family="IBM Plex Sans, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs}"><rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="#fff"/>`];
  if (spec.title) s.push(`<text x="${W / 2}" y="20" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${fs + 3}" font-weight="600">${esc3(spec.title)}</text>`);
  const vt = ticksOf(vr[0], vr[1], false, 10);
  for (const v of vt) s.push(`<line x1="${L - 4}" y1="${sv(v).toFixed(1)}" x2="${L}" y2="${sv(v).toFixed(1)}" stroke="#1c2422"/><text x="${L - 6}" y="${(sv(v) + 3.5).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="end">${fmtT(v)}</text>`);
  s.push(`<text transform="translate(16 ${T + ph / 2}) rotate(-90)" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600">${esc3(spec.v?.label)}</text>`);
  const fits = [], st = [], names = new Map();
  spec.panels.forEach((p, k) => {
    const x0 = L + k * (pw + gap), xr = axisRange(p.series.flatMap((se) => se.y), p), sx = scaler(xr[0], xr[1], x0, x0 + pw, p.log, p.invert);
    if (spec.grid !== false) { for (const v of vt) s.push(`<line x1="${x0}" y1="${sv(v).toFixed(1)}" x2="${x0 + pw}" y2="${sv(v).toFixed(1)}" stroke="#eef1f0"/>`); }
    s.push(`<clipPath id="sp${k}"><rect x="${x0}" y="${T}" width="${pw}" height="${ph}"/></clipPath><g clip-path="url(#sp${k})">`);
    p.series.forEach((se, i) => {
      const color = se.color || PLOT_COLORS[i % PLOT_COLORS.length]; names.set(se.core || se.name, color);
      const ser = { ...se, color, symbol: se.symbol || SYMS[i % SYMS.length] };
      s.push(drawSeries(ser, sx, sv, true));
      if (p.fit && p.fit !== 'none') { const f = fitCurve(se.x, se.y, p.fit === 'movavg' ? 'movavg' : p.fit, { deg: p.deg, window: p.window }); if (f) { s.push(drawFit(ser, f, sx, sv, vr[0], vr[1], true)); fits.push({ name: `${p.label} · ${se.name}`, eq: f.eq, r2: f.r2, n: f.n }); } }
      st.push({ name: `${p.label} · ${se.name}`, ...plotStats(se.y) });
    });
    s.push('</g>');
    s.push(`<rect x="${x0}" y="${T}" width="${pw}" height="${ph}" fill="none" stroke="#1c2422" stroke-width="0.8"/>`);
    const xt = ticksOf(xr[0], xr[1], p.log, 3);
    for (const v of xt) s.push(`<line x1="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y1="${T - 4}" x2="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y2="${T}" stroke="#1c2422"/><text x="${sx(v).toFixed(1)}" y="${T - 7}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${fs - 1.5}">${fmtT(v)}</text>`);
    s.push(`<text x="${x0 + pw / 2}" y="${T - 22}" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600">${esc3(p.label)}</text>`);
  });
  if (spec.legend !== false) { let x = L; for (const [n, c] of names) { s.push(`<line x1="${x}" y1="${H - 12}" x2="${x + 18}" y2="${H - 12}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="2"/><text x="${x + 24}" y="${H - 8}">${esc3(n)}</text>`); x += 40 + String(n).length * 6.5; } }
  s.push('</svg>');
  return { svg: s.join(''), fits, stats: st };
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/sheet.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool web — formulas of the Data tab (no DOM, no eval).
// Column formulas work on whole columns of one core: [XRF Ba/Ti] / TOC_pct, log10(x), zscore(x), movavg(x, 5)…
// Cell formulas work like a small spreadsheet: =A1*2, =SUM(A1:A5), =MEAN(COL("TOC_pct")), =CORR(COL("a"), COL("b")).

/** Formula error carrying a key + args; the UI translates it with fxText() (i18n.js). `message` is the key. */
const ferr = (key, args = {}) => Object.assign(new Error(key), { key, args });

/* ───────── tokenizer and parser (recursive descent) ───────── */

function tokenize(src) {
  const out = []; let i = 0;
  while (i < src.length) {
    const ch = src[i];
    if (/\s/.test(ch)) { i++; continue; }
    if (/[0-9.]/.test(ch)) { const m = /^(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)(e[+-]?\d+)?/i.exec(src.slice(i)); out.push({ t: 'num', v: +m[0] }); i += m[0].length; continue; }
    if (ch === '[') { const j = src.indexOf(']', i); if (j < 0) throw ferr('noBracket'); out.push({ t: 'name', v: src.slice(i + 1, j) }); i = j + 1; continue; }
    if (ch === '"' || ch === "'") { const j = src.indexOf(ch, i + 1); if (j < 0) throw ferr('noQuote'); out.push({ t: 'str', v: src.slice(i + 1, j) }); i = j + 1; continue; }
    const id = /^[A-Za-z_$][\w.$]*(:[A-Za-z]+\d+)?/.exec(src.slice(i));
    if (id) { out.push({ t: 'id', v: id[0] }); i += id[0].length; continue; }
    const op = /^(<=|>=|==|!=|[-+*/^(),<>])/.exec(src.slice(i));
    if (op) { out.push({ t: 'op', v: op[0] }); i += op[0].length; continue; }
    throw ferr('badChar', { v: ch });
  }
  return out;
}

/** Parse to an AST: {k:'num'|'str'|'ref'|'call'|'bin'|'neg', ...}. */
export function parseFormula(src) {
  const tk = tokenize(String(src).replace(/^\s*=/, '')); let p = 0;
  const peek = () => tk[p], eat = (v) => { if (tk[p]?.v !== v) throw ferr('expected', { v }); p++; };
  const binary = (next, ops) => () => { let a = next(); while (tk[p]?.t === 'op' && ops.includes(tk[p].v)) { const op = tk[p++].v; a = { k: 'bin', op, a, b: next() }; } return a; };
  const primary = () => {
    const x = peek(); if (!x) throw ferr('incomplete');
    if (x.t === 'num') { p++; return { k: 'num', v: x.v }; }
    if (x.t === 'str') { p++; return { k: 'str', v: x.v }; }
    if (x.t === 'name') { p++; return { k: 'ref', v: x.v }; }
    if (x.t === 'op' && x.v === '(') { p++; const e = cmp(); eat(')'); return e; }
    if (x.t === 'op' && x.v === '-') { p++; return { k: 'neg', a: power() }; }
    if (x.t === 'id') {
      p++;
      if (tk[p]?.v === '(') { p++; const args = []; if (tk[p]?.v !== ')') { args.push(cmp()); while (tk[p]?.v === ',') { p++; args.push(cmp()); } } eat(')'); return { k: 'call', f: x.v.toLowerCase(), args }; }
      return { k: 'ref', v: x.v };
    }
    throw ferr('unexpected', { v: x.v });
  };
  const power = () => { const a = primary(); if (tk[p]?.v === '^') { p++; return { k: 'bin', op: '^', a, b: power() }; } return a; };
  const unary = () => (tk[p]?.v === '-' ? (p++, { k: 'neg', a: unary() }) : power());
  const mul = binary(unary, ['*', '/']);
  const add = binary(mul, ['+', '-']);
  const cmp = binary(add, ['<', '>', '<=', '>=', '==', '!=']);
  const e = cmp(); if (p < tk.length) throw ferr('extra', { v: tk[p].v });
  return e;
}

/* ───────── helpers on arrays with missing values (null) ───────── */
const ok = (v) => v != null && Number.isFinite(v);
const vals = (a) => (Array.isArray(a) ? a.flat(Infinity) : [a]).filter(ok);
const meanOf = (a) => { const v = vals(a); return v.length ? v.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / v.length : null; };
const stdOf = (a) => { const v = vals(a), m = meanOf(v); return v.length > 1 ? Math.sqrt(v.reduce((s, x) => s + (x - m) ** 2, 0) / (v.length - 1)) : null; };
function corrOf(a, b) {
  const pr = a.map((x, i) => [x, b[i]]).filter(([x, y]) => ok(x) && ok(y)); if (pr.length < 3) return null;
  const ma = meanOf(pr.map((q) => q[0])), mb = meanOf(pr.map((q) => q[1]));
  let sab = 0, saa = 0, sbb = 0; for (const [x, y] of pr) { sab += (x - ma) * (y - mb); saa += (x - ma) ** 2; sbb += (y - mb) ** 2; }
  return saa && sbb ? sab / Math.sqrt(saa * sbb) : null;
}
/** Element-wise operation; scalars broadcast; null propagates. */
function zip(a, b, f) {
  if (!Array.isArray(a) && !Array.isArray(b)) return ok(a) && ok(b) ? f(a, b) : null;
  const n = Array.isArray(a) ? a.length : b.length, out = new Array(n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = Array.isArray(a) ? a[i] : a, y = Array.isArray(b) ? b[i] : b; const r = ok(x) && ok(y) ? f(x, y) : null; out[i] = ok(r) ? r : null; }
  return out;
}
const map1 = (a, f) => (Array.isArray(a) ? a.map((x) => { const r = ok(x) ? f(x) : null; return ok(r) ? r : null; }) : ok(a) ? f(a) : null);

const FUN = {
  log10: (x) => map1(x, Math.log10), ln: (x) => map1(x, Math.log), log: (x) => map1(x, Math.log), exp: (x) => map1(x, Math.exp),
  sqrt: (x) => map1(x, Math.sqrt), abs: (x) => map1(x, Math.abs), round: (x, d = 0) => map1(x, (v) => +v.toFixed(d)),
  pow: (x, y) => zip(x, y, Math.pow), min2: (x, y) => zip(x, y, Math.min), max2: (x, y) => zip(x, y, Math.max),
  mean: meanOf, average: meanOf, std: stdOf, stdev: stdOf, median: (a) => { const v = vals(a).sort((p, q) => p - q); return v.length ? (v.length % 2 ? v[(v.length - 1) / 2] : (v[v.length / 2 - 1] + v[v.length / 2]) / 2) : null; },
  sum: (...a) => { const v = vals(a); return v.length ? v.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) : 0; }, count: (...a) => vals(a).length,
  min: (...a) => { if (a.length === 2 && Array.isArray(a[0]) && Array.isArray(a[1])) return zip(a[0], a[1], Math.min); const v = vals(a); return v.length ? Math.min(...v) : null; },
  max: (...a) => { if (a.length === 2 && Array.isArray(a[0]) && Array.isArray(a[1])) return zip(a[0], a[1], Math.max); const v = vals(a); return v.length ? Math.max(...v) : null; },
  zscore: (x) => { const m = meanOf(x), s = stdOf(x); return map1(x, (v) => (s ? (v - m) / s : 0)); },
  norm01: (x) => { const v = vals(x), lo = Math.min(...v), hi = Math.max(...v); return map1(x, (q) => (hi > lo ? (q - lo) / (hi - lo) : 0)); },
  movavg: (x, n = 3) => { const h = Math.floor(n / 2); return x.map((_, i) => meanOf(x.slice(Math.max(0, i - h), i + h + 1))); },
  diff: (x) => x.map((v, i) => (i && ok(v) && ok(x[i - 1]) ? v - x[i - 1] : null)),
  cumsum: (x) => { let s = 0; return x.map((v) => (ok(v) ? (s += v) : null)); },
  if: (c, a, b) => (Array.isArray(c) ? c.map((v, i) => (v == null ? null : v ? (Array.isArray(a) ? a[i] : a) : (Array.isArray(b) ? b[i] : b))) : c ? a : b),
  corr: corrOf,
};
export const FUNCTIONS = Object.keys(FUN);

function evalAst(e, resolve) {
  switch (e.k) {
    case 'num': case 'str': return e.v;
    case 'ref': return resolve(e.v);
    case 'neg': return map1(evalAst(e.a, resolve), (v) => -v);
    case 'bin': {
      const a = evalAst(e.a, resolve), b = evalAst(e.b, resolve);
      const f = { '+': (x, y) => x + y, '-': (x, y) => x - y, '*': (x, y) => x * y, '/': (x, y) => (y === 0 ? null : x / y), '^': Math.pow,
        '<': (x, y) => +(x < y), '>': (x, y) => +(x > y), '<=': (x, y) => +(x <= y), '>=': (x, y) => +(x >= y), '==': (x, y) => +(x === y), '!=': (x, y) => +(x !== y) }[e.op];
      return zip(a, b, f);
    }
    case 'call': {
      if (e.f === 'col') return resolve({ col: evalAst(e.args[0], resolve), core: e.args[1] ? evalAst(e.args[1], resolve) : null });
      const fn = FUN[e.f]; if (!fn) throw ferr('unkFn', { v: e.f });
      return fn(...e.args.map((a) => evalAst(a, resolve)));
    }
    default: throw ferr('invalid');
  }
}

/** Evaluate a column formula over the columns of one core. Returns an array of length n. */
export function evalColumn(formula, columns, n) {
  const ast = parseFormula(formula);
  const r = evalAst(ast, (name) => {
    if (typeof name !== 'string') throw ferr('colOnly');
    if (!(name in columns)) throw ferr('noCol', { v: name });
    return columns[name];
  });
  return Array.isArray(r) ? r : new Array(n).fill(ok(r) ? r : null);
}

/* ───────── free cells (A1 notation) ───────── */
const colIdx = (letters) => [...letters.toUpperCase()].reduce((s, ch) => s * 26 + ch.charCodeAt(0) - 64, 0) - 1;
export const cellName = (c, r) => { let s = '', x = c + 1; while (x) { s = String.fromCharCode(65 + ((x - 1) % 26)) + s; x = Math.floor((x - 1) / 26); } return s + (r + 1); };

/**
 * Evaluate all cells. cells = {A1: '12' | '=A1*2' | 'texto'}; getCol(name, core) gives a core column.
 * Returns {A1: value | {error}}. Cycles give an error.
 */
export function evalCells(cells, getCol) {
  const out = {}, state = {};
  const value = (ref) => {
    ref = ref.toUpperCase();
    if (state[ref] === 2) return out[ref];
    if (state[ref] === 1) throw ferr('circular');
    state[ref] = 1;
    const raw = cells[ref];
    let v;
    try {
      if (raw == null || raw === '') v = null;
      else if (String(raw).startsWith('=')) v = evalAst(parseFormula(raw), resolve);
      else v = Number.isFinite(+raw) && String(raw).trim() !== '' ? +raw : raw;
      if (Array.isArray(v)) v = meanOf(v); // a cell shows one value
    } catch (e) { v = { error: e.message, err: e }; }
    out[ref] = v; state[ref] = 2;
    return v;
  };
  const resolve = (name) => {
    if (typeof name === 'object') return getCol(name.col, name.core);
    const m = /^([A-Za-z]+)(\d+)(?::([A-Za-z]+)(\d+))?$/.exec(name);
    if (!m) throw ferr('badRef', { v: name });
    if (!m[3]) { const v = value(m[1] + m[2]); if (v && v.error) throw v.err; return v; }
    const c0 = colIdx(m[1]), r0 = +m[2] - 1, c1 = colIdx(m[3]), r1 = +m[4] - 1, arr = [];
    for (let r = Math.min(r0, r1); r <= Math.max(r0, r1); r++) for (let c = Math.min(c0, c1); c <= Math.max(c0, c1); c++) { const v = value(cellName(c, r)); arr.push(typeof v === 'number' ? v : null); }
    return arr;
  };
  for (const ref of Object.keys(cells)) value(ref);
  return out;
}

/** V0.35: names of the columns a formula refers to. */
export function formulaRefs(src) {
  const out = new Set(), walk = (n) => { if (!n) return; if (n.k === 'ref') out.add(n.v); for (const k of ['a', 'b']) walk(n[k]); (n.args || []).forEach(walk); };
  walk(parseFormula(src));
  return [...out];
}

/**
 * V0.35: values of a column at every row, linearly interpolated along x (depth or age, ascending) between the nearest
 * rows that have a value; no extrapolation beyond the first and last values, and no filling across gaps wider than
 * maxGap (in x units). Rows that already have a value keep it.
 */
export function fillLinear(x, v, maxGap = Infinity) {
  const n = v.length, out = v.slice();
  let prev = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (v[i] == null || x[i] == null) continue;
    if (prev >= 0 && i - prev > 1 && x[i] - x[prev] <= maxGap) {
      for (let k = prev + 1; k < i; k++) if (x[k] != null && x[i] !== x[prev]) out[k] = v[prev] + ((v[i] - v[prev]) * (x[k] - x[prev])) / (x[i] - x[prev]);
    }
    prev = i;
  }
  return out;
}

const ASSET_BASES = ['', '../examples/'];

/** Bundled files (example images, test core). The standalone build injects them as data URIs.
 *
 * @param {string} path
 * @returns {Promise<Response>}
 */
export async function fetchAsset(path) {
  const inline = window.ICHANTOOL_ASSETS?.[path];
  for (const u of inline ? [inline] : ASSET_BASES.map((b) => b + path)) {
    try { const r = await fetch(u); if (r.ok) return r; } catch { /* next */ }
  }
  throw new Error('file not found: ' + path);
}

/** V0.50: whether an example file is available (the downloads carry no sample data; the repository does, for tests).
 *
 * @param {string} path
 * @returns {Promise<boolean>}
 */
async function assetExists(path) {
  if (window.ICHANTOOL_ASSETS?.[path]) return true;
  for (const b of ASSET_BASES) { try { const r = await fetch(b + path, { method: 'HEAD' }); if (r.ok) return true; } catch { /* next */ } }
  return false;
}
/** V0.50: example and demo buttons only where their files exist. */
export async function hideMissingExamples() {
  for (const [id, path] of [['loadExample', 'data/idealized_core_0-500cm.jpg'], ['openIdeal', 'data/idealized_core_0-500cm.jpg'], ['openTest', 'U1385I-1H/records/397-U1385I-1H_MS.csv'], ['geoDemo', 'geochem_demo/intervals.csv'], ['openTutorial', 'tutorial/cores.csv']]) {
    const el = $(id); if (el && !(await assetExists(path))) el.hidden = true;
  }
}

// SedCoreTool web — project library in the browser (IndexedDB). Each project is a "folder" holding its
// images, depths, age model, labels and external records. Data stay on this computer and browser.

const DB_NAME = 'ichantool', DB_VERSION = 6;   // Add bounded image chunk storage
let dbPromise = null;

function openDB() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
    req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const db = req.result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('meta')) db.createObjectStore('meta', { keyPath: 'id' });
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('data')) db.createObjectStore('data', { keyPath: 'id' });
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('prefs')) db.createObjectStore('prefs', { keyPath: 'key' });
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('images')) db.createObjectStore('images', { keyPath: 'key' }); // V0.1: original image files, written once
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('lite')) db.createObjectStore('lite', { keyPath: 'id' }); // numeric data only, for the all-projects view
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('secs')) db.createObjectStore('secs', { keyPath: 'key' }); // V0.44: sections of a project, saved one by one
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('imageChunks')) db.createObjectStore('imageChunks');
    };
    req.onsuccess = () => { const d = req.result; d.onversionchange = () => { d.close(); dbPromise = null; }; resolve(d); };
    req.onblocked = () => console.warn('IndexedDB upgrade blocked by another tab');
    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
  });
}
const db = () => (dbPromise ||= openDB());

function run(stores, mode, fn) {
  return db().then((d) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const tx = d.transaction(stores, mode);
    let req;
    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(req && 'result' in req ? req.result : undefined);
    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
    tx.onabort = () => reject(tx.error);
    try { req = fn(tx); }
    catch (error) {
      // A synchronous clone/queue error must also roll back requests already queued by fn.
      tx.abort(); reject(error);
    }
  }));
}

export async function storageAvailable() {
  try { await db(); return true; } catch { return false; }
}
export const listProjects = () => run(['meta'], 'readonly', (tx) => tx.objectStore('meta').getAll());
/*
 * V0.44: saving by sections. The project record keeps a stub per section ({$sec, id, name, file}) and each section
 * lives in 'secs' under "<project id>|<section id>". A section is written only when it changed since it was last
 * written or read in this session (same masks and same remaining fields), or when its record is missing.
 * getProject() puts the sections back, so callers always receive the complete project. Records written before
 * V0.44 (sections inside the project) are read as they are.
 */
const lastSec = new Map();
const secSig = (q) => ({ lab: q.labels, ex: q.exclude, rest: JSON.stringify({ ...q, labels: 0, exclude: 0 }) });
const arrEq = (a, b) => { if (a === b) return true; if (!a || !b || a.length !== b.length) return false; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) return false; return true; };
const sameSec = (p, c) => p.rest === c.rest && arrEq(p.lab, c.lab) && arrEq(p.ex, c.ex);
const secLists = (d) => (d?.cores ? d.cores.map((c) => c.sections || []) : d?.sections ? [d.sections] : []);
export const getProject = (id) => run(['data', 'secs'], 'readonly', (tx) => {
  const out = { result: null }, r = tx.objectStore('data').get(id);
  r.onsuccess = () => {
    const d = r.result?.data || null; out.result = d; if (!d) return;
    const st = tx.objectStore('secs');
    for (const list of secLists(d)) list.forEach((q, i) => {
      if (!q?.$sec) return;
      const g = st.get(q.$sec);
      g.onsuccess = () => { if (g.result) { list[i] = g.result.q; lastSec.set(q.$sec, secSig(g.result.q)); } else list[i] = { ...q, $missing: true }; };
    });
  };
  return out;
});
export function putProject(meta, data) {
  const id = meta.id, writes = [], checks = [], keep = new Set(), stub = { ...data };
  const conv = (list, ci) => (list || []).map((q, i) => {
    if (!q || q.$sec) { if (q?.$sec) keep.add(q.$sec); return q; }
    const key = `${id}|${q.id ?? `${ci}:${i}`}`, sig = secSig(q), prev = lastSec.get(key); keep.add(key);
    (prev && sameSec(prev, sig) ? checks : writes).push({ key, q, sig });
    return { $sec: key, id: q.id, name: q.name, file: q.file };
  });
  if (data.cores) stub.cores = data.cores.map((c, ci) => ({ ...c, sections: conv(c.sections, ci) }));
  else if (data.sections) stub.sections = conv(data.sections, 0);
  return run(['meta', 'data', 'secs'], 'readwrite', (tx) => {
    const st = tx.objectStore('secs');
    tx.objectStore('meta').put(meta);
    tx.objectStore('data').put({ id, data: stub });
    for (const w of writes) st.put({ key: w.key, q: w.q });
    for (const c of checks) { const k = st.getKey(c.key); k.onsuccess = () => { if (k.result === undefined) st.put({ key: c.key, q: c.q }); }; }   // never trust the cache alone
    const old = st.getAllKeys(IDBKeyRange.bound(id + '|', id + '|\uffff'));
    old.onsuccess = () => { for (const k of old.result) if (!keep.has(k)) st.delete(k); };
  }).then(() => { for (const w of [...writes, ...checks]) lastSec.set(w.key, w.sig); return { written: writes.length, kept: checks.length }; });
}
export const putMeta = (meta) => run(['meta'], 'readwrite', (tx) => tx.objectStore('meta').put(meta));
/** Image keys referenced by a stored project (format v4: file.key). */
export function imageKeys(data) {
  const out = [];
  for (const c of data?.cores || [{ sections: data?.sections }]) for (const q of c.sections || []) if (q.file?.key) out.push(q.file.key);
  return out;
}
const IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
const imageWrites = new Set();

async function imageDirectory() {
  try {
    const root = await globalThis.navigator?.storage?.getDirectory?.();
    const dir = await root?.getDirectoryHandle('sedcoretool-images', { create: true });
    return typeof globalThis.FileSystemFileHandle?.prototype.createWritable === 'function' ? dir : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

const imageChunkRange = (generation) => IDBKeyRange.bound([generation, 0], [generation, Infinity]);

function removeImageRecord(tx, record, files) {
  if (record.chunks) tx.objectStore('imageChunks').delete(imageChunkRange(record.generation));
  if (record.disk) files.push(record.generation);
  tx.objectStore('images').delete(record.key);
}

async function removeImageFiles(files) {
  if (!files.length) return;
  const dir = await imageDirectory();
  if (dir) for (const file of files) await dir.removeEntry(file).catch(() => {});
}

export async function deleteProject(id) {
  const files = [];
  await run(['meta', 'data', 'lite', 'images', 'secs', 'imageChunks'], 'readwrite', (tx) => {
    const projects = tx.objectStore('data'), r = projects.getAll();
    r.onsuccess = () => {
      const owned = imageKeys(r.result.find((p) => p.id === id)?.data);
      const used = new Set(r.result.filter((p) => p.id !== id).flatMap((p) => imageKeys(p.data)));
      const store = tx.objectStore('images');
      for (const key of owned) {
        if (used.has(key)) continue;
        const req = store.get(key);
        req.onsuccess = () => { if (req.result) removeImageRecord(tx, req.result, files); };
      }
      projects.delete(id);
    };
    tx.objectStore('meta').delete(id);
    tx.objectStore('lite').delete(id);
    tx.objectStore('secs').delete(IDBKeyRange.bound(id + '|', id + '|\uffff'));
  });
  for (const k of [...lastSec.keys()]) if (k.startsWith(id + '|')) lastSec.delete(k);
  await removeImageFiles(files);
}

/** Publish the image reference only after its disk file or IndexedDB chunks are complete. */
export async function putImage(key, blob) {
  const generation = 'image-' + Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(4)), (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')).join('');
  const dir = await imageDirectory(), type = blob.type, size = blob.size;
  const chunks = Math.ceil(size / IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES);
  const staged = { key: generation, generation, size, type, t: Date.now(), disk: Boolean(dir), chunks: dir ? 0 : chunks, pending: true };
  const files = [];
  imageWrites.add(generation);
  try {
    await run(['images'], 'readwrite', (tx) => tx.objectStore('images').put(staged));
    if (dir) {
      const handle = await dir.getFileHandle(generation, { create: true });
      await blob.stream().pipeTo(await handle.createWritable());
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < chunks; i++) {
        const offset = i * IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES;
        const bytes = await blob.slice(offset, offset + IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES).arrayBuffer();
        await run(['imageChunks'], 'readwrite', (tx) => tx.objectStore('imageChunks').put(bytes, [generation, i]));
      }
    }
    await run(['images', 'imageChunks'], 'readwrite', (tx) => {
      const store = tx.objectStore('images'), previous = store.get(key);
      previous.onsuccess = () => {
        if (previous.result) removeImageRecord(tx, previous.result, files);
        store.put({ ...staged, key, pending: false });
        store.delete(generation);
      };
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const abandoned = [];
    await run(['images', 'imageChunks'], 'readwrite', (tx) => removeImageRecord(tx, staged, abandoned)).catch(() => {});
    await removeImageFiles(abandoned);
    throw err;
  } finally {
    imageWrites.delete(generation);
  }
  await removeImageFiles(files);
}

function chunkedImage(record) {
  async function readPart(index) {
    const bytes = await run(['imageChunks'], 'readonly', (tx) => tx.objectStore('imageChunks').get([record.generation, index]));
    const length = Math.min(IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES, record.size - index * IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES);
    if (!bytes || bytes.byteLength !== length) throw new Error('Missing stored image chunk');
    return bytes;
  }
  return {
    size: record.size,
    type: record.type,
    slice(start, end) {
      return {
        async arrayBuffer() {
          const out = new Uint8Array(end - start);
          for (let offset = start; offset < end;) {
            const part = await readPart(Math.floor(offset / IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES)), from = offset % IMAGE_CHUNK_BYTES;
            const count = Math.min(part.byteLength - from, end - offset);
            out.set(new Uint8Array(part, from, count), offset - start);
            offset += count;
          }
          return out.buffer;
        },
      };
    },
    stream() {
      let index = 0;
      return new ReadableStream({
        async pull(controller) {
          if (index === record.chunks) { controller.close(); return; }
          controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(await readPart(index++)));
        },
      });
    },
  };
}

/** Read legacy records as before. A ranged caller can consume the fallback chunks lazily. */
export async function getImage(key, ranged = false) {
  const record = await run(['images'], 'readonly', (tx) => tx.objectStore('images').get(key));
  if (!record || record.pending) return null;
  if (record.disk) {
    const dir = await imageDirectory();
    if (!dir) throw new Error('Stored image directory unavailable');
    const file = await (await dir.getFileHandle(record.generation)).getFile();
    if (file.size !== record.size) throw new Error('Incomplete stored image');
    return file.slice(0, file.size, record.type);
  }
  if (!record.generation) return record.blob || new Blob([record.bytes], { type: record.type || 'image/jpeg' });
  const source = chunkedImage(record);
  return ranged ? source : new Response(source.stream(), { headers: { 'Content-Type': record.type } }).blob();
}

/** Reclaim unreferenced images and abandoned staged writes after a grace period. */
export async function gcImages() {
  const files = [], now = Date.now(), hour = 3600e3;
  await run(['images', 'imageChunks', 'data'], 'readwrite', (tx) => {
    const projects = tx.objectStore('data').getAll(), store = tx.objectStore('images');
    projects.onsuccess = () => {
      const used = new Set(projects.result.flatMap((p) => imageKeys(p.data)));
      const keys = store.getAllKeys();
      keys.onsuccess = () => {
        for (const key of keys.result) {
          if (used.has(key) || imageWrites.has(key)) continue;
          const req = store.get(key);
          req.onsuccess = () => {
            const record = req.result;
            if (record && record.t < now - (record.pending ? 24 : 1) * hour) removeImageRecord(tx, record, files);
          };
        }
      };
    };
  });
  await removeImageFiles(files);
}
export const putLite = (id, lite) => run(['lite'], 'readwrite', (tx) => tx.objectStore('lite').put({ ...lite, id }));
export const listLite = () => run(['lite'], 'readonly', (tx) => tx.objectStore('lite').getAll());
export const getMeta = (id) => run(['meta'], 'readonly', (tx) => tx.objectStore('meta').get(id));

/** Preferences shared by all projects (user presets, backup folder handle, last backup time). */
export const getPref = (key) => run(['prefs'], 'readonly', (tx) => tx.objectStore('prefs').get(key)).then((r) => r?.value);
export const setPref = (key, value) => run(['prefs'], 'readwrite', (tx) => tx.objectStore('prefs').put({ key, value }));

// @ts-check
/** Visible-row geometry and DOM lifecycle for the experimental Data table. */

/**
 * @param {{rowCount: number, scrollTop: number, height: number, rowHeight: number, headerHeight?: number, overscan?: number}} options
 */
export function visibleTableRows({ rowCount, scrollTop, height, rowHeight, headerHeight = 0, overscan = 12 }) {
  if (!Number.isInteger(rowCount) || rowCount < 0 || !Number.isFinite(rowHeight) || rowHeight <= 0
    || !Number.isFinite(scrollTop) || !Number.isFinite(height) || !Number.isFinite(headerHeight)
    || !Number.isInteger(overscan) || overscan < 0) throw new RangeError('Invalid table viewport geometry');
  const first = Math.min(Math.max(0, rowCount - 1), Math.floor(Math.max(0, scrollTop - headerHeight) / rowHeight));
  const count = Math.ceil(Math.max(0, height) / rowHeight);
  return { start: Math.max(0, first - overscan), end: Math.min(rowCount, first + count + overscan + 1) };
}

/**
 * @param {{grid: HTMLElement, body: HTMLTableSectionElement, rowCount: number, columnCount: number,
 *   renderRows: (start: number, end: number) => string, scrollTop?: number}} options
 */
export function mountTableViewport({ grid, body, rowCount, columnCount, renderRows, scrollTop = 0 }) {
  const rowHeight = 28;
  let frame = 0, disposed = false, start = -1, end = -1;
  /** @type {Map<number, HTMLTableRowElement>} */
  const mounted = new Map();
  const top = createSpacer(), bottom = createSpacer();
  body.replaceChildren(top, bottom);

  function createSpacer() {
    const row = document.createElement('tr'), cell = document.createElement('td');
    row.dataset.spacer = ''; row.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    cell.colSpan = columnCount; row.append(cell);
    return row;
  }

  /** @param {Node | null} node */
  function rowAt(node) {
    const element = node instanceof Element ? node : node?.parentElement;
    const row = element?.closest('tr[data-row]');
    return row && body.contains(row) ? Number(/** @type {HTMLElement} */ (row).dataset.row) : null;
  }

  function selectedRows() {
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (!selection || selection.isCollapsed || !selection.rangeCount) return null;
    const anchor = rowAt(selection.anchorNode), focus = rowAt(selection.focusNode);
    if (anchor != null && focus != null) return { start: Math.min(anchor, focus), end: Math.max(anchor, focus) + 1 };
    if (selection.getRangeAt(0).intersectsNode(body)) return { start: 0, end: rowCount };
    return null;
  }

  /** @param {number} from @param {number} to @param {Node} before */
  function insertRows(from, to, before) {
    if (to <= from) return;
    const staging = document.createElement('tbody');
    staging.innerHTML = renderRows(from, to);
    const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (const row of [...staging.rows]) { mounted.set(Number(row.dataset.row), row); fragment.append(row); }
    body.insertBefore(fragment, before);
  }

  /** @param {HTMLTableRowElement} spacer @param {number} height */
  function sizeSpacer(spacer, height) {
    spacer.hidden = height === 0;
    spacer.cells[0].style.height = `${height}px`;
  }

  function update() {
    cancelAnimationFrame(frame); frame = 0;
    if (disposed || !body.isConnected) return;
    const range = visibleTableRows({ rowCount, scrollTop: grid.scrollTop, height: grid.clientHeight,
      headerHeight: grid.querySelector('thead')?.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0, rowHeight });
    const selected = selectedRows();
    if (selected) { range.start = Math.min(range.start, selected.start); range.end = Math.max(range.end, selected.end); }
    const active = document.activeElement;
    const activeRow = rowAt(active);
    if (activeRow != null && (activeRow < range.start || activeRow >= range.end) && active instanceof HTMLElement) {
      active.blur();
      if (disposed) return;
    }
    if (range.start === start && range.end === end) return;
    let first = Infinity, last = -1;
    for (const [index, row] of mounted) {
      if (index < range.start || index >= range.end) { row.remove(); mounted.delete(index); }
      else { first = Math.min(first, index); last = Math.max(last, index); }
    }
    if (last < 0) insertRows(range.start, range.end, bottom);
    else {
      insertRows(range.start, first, /** @type {HTMLTableRowElement} */ (mounted.get(first)));
      insertRows(last + 1, range.end, bottom);
    }
    start = range.start; end = range.end;
    sizeSpacer(top, start * rowHeight); sizeSpacer(bottom, (rowCount - end) * rowHeight);
    grid.dataset.start = String(start); grid.dataset.end = String(end); grid.dataset.available = String(rowCount);
  }

  function schedule() { if (!disposed && !frame) frame = requestAnimationFrame(update); }
  grid.addEventListener('scroll', schedule, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener('selectionchange', schedule);
  const resize = new ResizeObserver(schedule); resize.observe(grid);
  // Establish the full scroll extent before restoring a previously scrolled window.
  sizeSpacer(bottom, rowCount * rowHeight);
  grid.scrollTop = scrollTop;
  update();

  /** @param {number} index @param {number} column */
  function focusCell(index, column) {
    if (index < 0 || index >= rowCount) return false;
    const selection = window.getSelection(); selection?.removeAllRanges();
    if (document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement && body.contains(document.activeElement)) document.activeElement.blur();
    if (disposed) return false;
    const headerHeight = grid.querySelector('thead')?.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0;
    const rowTop = headerHeight + index * rowHeight;
    if (rowTop < grid.scrollTop + headerHeight) grid.scrollTop = index * rowHeight;
    else if (rowTop + rowHeight > grid.scrollTop + grid.clientHeight) grid.scrollTop = rowTop + rowHeight - grid.clientHeight;
    update();
    const cell = mounted.get(index)?.cells[column];
    if (!cell?.isContentEditable) return false;
    cell.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    const bounds = grid.getBoundingClientRect(), rect = cell.getBoundingClientRect();
    const left = bounds.left + (grid.querySelector('td.rn')?.getBoundingClientRect().width || 0);
    if (rect.left < left) grid.scrollLeft -= left - rect.left;
    else if (rect.right > bounds.right) grid.scrollLeft += rect.right - bounds.right;
    return true;
  }

  return {
    focusCell,
    pageRows: () => Math.max(1, Math.floor(grid.clientHeight / rowHeight) - 1),
    destroy() {
      disposed = true; cancelAnimationFrame(frame); resize.disconnect();
      grid.removeEventListener('scroll', schedule); document.removeEventListener('selectionchange', schedule);
      mounted.clear();
    },
  };
}

ARCHIVO: app/js/tiff.js
