/** Range decoder for the first image in a classic TIFF or BigTIFF. */
const TIFF_BLOCK_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
const TIFF_READ_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
const TIFF_UINT_BYTES = { 1: 1, 3: 2, 4: 4, 16: 8 };

class TiffDirectory {
  constructor(source, little, big) {
    this.source = source;
    this.little = little;
    this.big = big;
    this.tags = new Map();
  }

  async read(offset, length) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(offset) || !Number.isSafeInteger(length) ||
        offset < 0 || length < 0 || offset > this.source.size - length) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF byte range');
    const bytes = await this.source.slice(offset, offset + length).arrayBuffer();
    if (bytes.byteLength !== length) throw new Error('Truncated TIFF');
    return new DataView(bytes);
  }

  uint(view, offset, size) {
    if (size === 1) return view.getUint8(offset);
    if (size === 2) return view.getUint16(offset, this.little);
    if (size === 4) return view.getUint32(offset, this.little);
    const value = Number(view.getBigUint64(offset, this.little));
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value)) throw new Error('TIFF integer exceeds the supported range');
    return value;
  }

  async open() {
    const word = this.big ? 8 : 4, countBytes = this.big ? 8 : 2;
    const head = await this.read(0, this.big ? 16 : 8);
    if (this.big && (this.uint(head, 4, 2) !== 8 || this.uint(head, 6, 2) !== 0)) throw new Error('Invalid BigTIFF header');
    const offset = this.uint(head, this.big ? 8 : 4, word);
    const count = this.uint(await this.read(offset, countBytes), 0, countBytes);
    if (count > 65535) throw new Error('Too many TIFF tags');
    const entryBytes = this.big ? 20 : 12;
    const entries = await this.read(offset + countBytes, count * entryBytes);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const pos = i * entryBytes, tag = this.uint(entries, pos, 2);
      const width = TIFF_UINT_BYTES[this.uint(entries, pos + 2, 2)];
      if (!width) continue;
      const n = this.uint(entries, pos + 4, word);
      const valuePos = pos + 4 + word;
      this.tags.set(tag, { n, width, entries, valuePos, word });
    }
  }

  async values(tag, start, count) {
    const entry = this.tags.get(tag);
    if (!entry || start + count > entry.n) throw new Error('Incomplete TIFF tag ' + tag);
    const { n, width, entries, valuePos, word } = entry;
    const inline = n * width <= word;
    const view = inline ? entries : await this.read(this.uint(entries, valuePos, word) + start * width, count * width);
    const base = inline ? valuePos + start * width : 0;
    return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => this.uint(view, base + i * width, width));
  }

  async number(tag, fallback) {
    return this.tags.has(tag) ? (await this.values(tag, 0, 1))[0] : fallback;
  }
}

/** TIFF LZW uses MSB-first codes and increases the code size one entry early. */
function tiffLzw(input, output) {
  const prefix = new Uint16Array(4096), suffix = new Uint8Array(4096), stack = new Uint8Array(4096);
  let bit = 0, pos = 0, next = 258, width = 9, previous = -1, first = 0;
  while (bit + width <= input.length * 8) {
    let code = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < width; i++, bit++) code = (code << 1) | ((input[bit >>> 3] >>> (7 - (bit & 7))) & 1);
    if (code === 256) { next = 258; width = 9; previous = -1; continue; }
    if (code === 257) break;
    if (code > next || (code === next && previous < 0)) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF LZW code');
    let size = 0, entry = code;
    if (code === next) { stack[size++] = first; entry = previous; }
    while (entry >= 258) { stack[size++] = suffix[entry]; entry = prefix[entry]; }
    first = entry;
    stack[size++] = first;
    if (pos + size > output.length) throw new Error('Oversized TIFF LZW block');
    while (size) output[pos++] = stack[--size];
    if (previous >= 0 && next < 4096) {
      prefix[next] = previous;
      suffix[next++] = first;
      if (width < 12 && next === (1 << width) - 1) width++;
    }
    previous = code;
  }
  return pos;
}

function tiffPackBits(input, output) {
  let pos = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < input.length;) {
    const code = input[i++];
    if (code === 128) continue;
    const length = code < 128 ? code + 1 : 257 - code;
    const consumed = code < 128 ? length : 1;
    if (i + consumed > input.length || pos + length > output.length) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF PackBits block');
    if (code < 128) output.set(input.subarray(i, i + length), pos);
    else output.fill(input[i], pos, pos + length);
    i += consumed;
    pos += length;
  }
  return pos;
}

async function tiffInflate(input, output) {
  const reader = new Blob([input]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate')).getReader();
  let pos = 0;
  try {
    for (;;) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) return pos;
      if (pos + value.length > output.length) throw new Error('Oversized TIFF Deflate block');
      output.set(value, pos);
      pos += value.length;
    }
  } finally {
    await reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
  }
}

async function tiffDecodeBlock(compression, input, length) {
  const out = new Uint8Array(length);
  const size = compression === 5 ? tiffLzw(input, out) :
    compression === 32773 ? tiffPackBits(input, out) : await tiffInflate(input, out);
  if (size !== length) throw new Error('Incomplete TIFF block');
  return new DataView(out.buffer);
}

/**
 * Decode only intersecting strips or tiles. Unsupported classic TIFF layouts
 * return metadata so the caller can bound its legacy decoder.
 */
export async function decodeTiffBlocks(source, chooseArea, chooseFactor) {
  const header = new DataView(await source.slice(0, 4).arrayBuffer());
  if (header.byteLength !== 4) throw new Error('Truncated TIFF header');
  const order = header.getUint16(0);
  if (order !== 0x4949 && order !== 0x4d4d) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF byte order');
  const little = order === 0x4949, version = header.getUint16(2, little), big = version === 43;
  if (version !== 42 && !big) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF version');
  const directory = new TiffDirectory(source, little, big);
  await directory.open();
  const fw = await directory.number(256, 0), fh = await directory.number(257, 0);
  if (!fw || !fh || !Number.isSafeInteger(fw * fh)) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF dimensions');
  const channels = await directory.number(277, 1);
  const bits = await directory.number(258, 1), compression = await directory.number(259, 1);
  const photometric = await directory.number(262, 1), predictor = await directory.number(317, 1);
  const tiled = directory.tags.has(322);
  const gray = channels === 1 && (photometric === 0 || photometric === 1);
  const signed = gray && bits === 16 && (await directory.number(339, 1)) === 2;
  const rgb = (channels === 3 || channels === 4) && photometric === 2;
  const matches = async (tag, value) => {
    const count = directory.tags.get(tag)?.n ?? 0;
    return !count || (count <= channels && (await directory.values(tag, 0, count)).every((v) => v === value));
  };
  if (!(gray || rgb) || ![8, 16].includes(bits) || !(await matches(258, bits)) || !(await matches(339, signed ? 2 : 1)) ||
      (await directory.number(284, 1)) !== 1 || (await directory.number(266, 1)) !== 1 ||
      ![1, 2].includes(predictor) || ![1, 5, 8, 32946, 32773].includes(compression) ||
      ((compression === 8 || compression === 32946) && typeof DecompressionStream !== 'function')) {
    if (big) throw new Error('Unsupported BigTIFF pixel layout or compression');
    const bitCount = directory.tags.get(258)?.n ?? 0;
    const declaredBits = bitCount > 64 ? Infinity : bitCount ?
      Math.max(bits * channels, (await directory.values(258, 0, bitCount)).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)) : channels;
    return { fw, fh, legacyBytes: fh * Math.ceil(fw * declaredBits / 8) + fw * fh * 4 };
  }

  const area = chooseArea(fw, fh), k = chooseFactor(area.w, area.h);
  if (!Number.isInteger(k) || k < 1) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF reduction factor');
  const w = Math.floor(area.w / k), h = Math.floor(area.h / k), pixels = w * h;
  if (!w || !h || !Number.isSafeInteger(pixels)) throw new Error('Empty TIFF working area');
  const wide = bits === 16, max = wide ? 65535 : 255, bytes = bits / 8;
  const output = wide ? new Uint16Array(pixels * 4) : new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels * 4);
  const colorChannels = gray ? 1 : 3, divisor = k * k;
  const Sums = max * divisor <= 0xffffffff ? Uint32Array : Float64Array;
  const sums = k > 1 ? new Sums(pixels * colorChannels) : null;
  const bw = tiled ? await directory.number(322, 0) : fw;
  const bh = tiled ? await directory.number(323, 0) : Math.min(fh, await directory.number(278, fh));
  if (!bw || !bh || !Number.isSafeInteger(bw * bh * channels * bytes)) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF block dimensions');
  const across = Math.ceil(fw / bw), right = area.x + w * k, bottom = area.y + h * k;
  const offsetTag = tiled ? 324 : 273, lengthTag = tiled ? 325 : 279;
  const rowBytes = bw * channels * bytes;
  const value = (view, i) => wide ? view.getUint16(i * 2, little) : view.getUint8(i);

  function copyRows(view, x, y, rows) {
    const firstX = Math.max(x, area.x), lastX = Math.min(x + bw, right);
    for (let sy = Math.max(y, area.y); sy < Math.min(y + rows, bottom); sy++) {
      const base = (sy - y) * bw * channels;
      const row = Math.floor((sy - area.y) / k) * w;
      let red = 0, green = 0, blue = 0, alpha = 0;
      for (let sx = predictor === 2 ? x : firstX; sx < lastX; sx++) {
        const index = base + (sx - x) * channels;
        red = (value(view, index) + (predictor === 2 ? red : 0)) & max;
        if (rgb) {
          green = (value(view, index + 1) + (predictor === 2 ? green : 0)) & max;
          blue = (value(view, index + 2) + (predictor === 2 ? blue : 0)) & max;
          if (channels === 4) alpha = (value(view, index + 3) + (predictor === 2 ? alpha : 0)) & max;
        }
        if (sx < firstX) continue;
        const target = row + Math.floor((sx - area.x) / k);
        // Offset signed grayscale into the unsigned working domain without discarding any bits.
        const sample = signed ? red ^ 0x8000 : red;
        const r = photometric === 0 ? max - sample : sample;
        if (sums) {
          const o = target * colorChannels;
          sums[o] += r;
          if (rgb) { sums[o + 1] += green; sums[o + 2] += blue; }
        } else {
          const o = target * 4;
          output[o] = r; output[o + 1] = gray ? r : green; output[o + 2] = gray ? r : blue;
          output[o + 3] = channels === 4 ? alpha : max;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const firstBlock = Math.floor(area.x / bw), lastBlock = Math.ceil(right / bw);
  for (let by = Math.floor(area.y / bh); by < Math.ceil(bottom / bh); by++) {
    for (let bx = firstBlock; bx < lastBlock;) {
      const n = Math.min(256, lastBlock - bx), index = by * across + bx;
      const offsets = await directory.values(offsetTag, index, n);
      const lengths = await directory.values(lengthTag, index, n);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++, bx++) {
        const x = bx * bw, y = by * bh, rows = tiled ? bh : Math.min(bh, fh - y);
        const offset = offsets[i], size = lengths[i], decodedSize = rows * rowBytes;
        if (size === 0 || offset > source.size - size) throw new Error('Invalid TIFF block range');
        if (compression === 1) {
          if (size < decodedSize || rowBytes > TIFF_BLOCK_BYTES) throw new Error('Invalid or oversized TIFF row');
          const batch = Math.max(1, Math.floor(TIFF_READ_BYTES / rowBytes));
          const end = Math.min(rows, bottom - y);
          for (let start = Math.max(0, area.y - y); start < end; start += batch) {
            const count = Math.min(batch, end - start);
            const view = await directory.read(offset + start * rowBytes, count * rowBytes);
            copyRows(view, x, y + start, count);
          }
        } else {
          if (size > TIFF_BLOCK_BYTES || decodedSize > TIFF_BLOCK_BYTES) throw new Error('TIFF block exceeds 64 MiB');
          const data = await directory.read(offset, size);
          const view = await tiffDecodeBlock(compression, new Uint8Array(data.buffer), decodedSize);
          copyRows(view, x, y, rows);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (sums) for (let i = 0; i < pixels; i++) {
    const o = i * 4, s = i * colorChannels;
    const r = sums[s] / divisor, g = gray ? r : sums[s + 1] / divisor, b = gray ? r : sums[s + 2] / divisor;
    output[o] = wide ? Math.round(r) : r;
    output[o + 1] = wide ? Math.round(g) : g;
    output[o + 2] = wide ? Math.round(b) : b;
    output[o + 3] = max;
  }
  const rgba = wide ? Uint8ClampedArray.from(output, (sample) => sample >>> 8) : output;
  return { w, h, rgba, rgba16: wide ? output : null, k, fw, fh };
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/unet.js
##########################################

import { topAt } from './core.js';
// V0.15: learned segmentation (U-Net) in the app — DOM-free and independent of the inference engine.
// The same tiling, normalisation, blending, resampling and training-mask rules are implemented in
// src/sedcoretool/unet/tiling.py; tests in both languages check identical values.

/** Default model description (overridden by the model's JSON written by `sedcoretool unet train`). */
export const UNET_DEFAULTS = { tile: 512, overlap: 64, mean: [0.485, 0.456, 0.406], std: [0.229, 0.224, 0.225], pxPerCm: null, classes: ['bioturbation'], output: 'logits' };
/** Label value for pixels that must not be used for training or scoring (excluded zones, unreviewed candidates). */
export const IGNORE = 255;

/** Tile start positions along one axis: step tile − overlap, last tile flush with the end; one tile if len ≤ tile. */
export function tileStarts(len, tile, overlap) {
  if (len <= tile) return [0];
  const step = Math.max(1, tile - overlap), out = [];
  for (let s = 0; s + tile < len; s += step) out.push(s);
  if (out[out.length - 1] !== len - tile) out.push(len - tile);
  return out;
}

/** 1-D blending weight: linear ramp over `overlap` pixels at both ends, 1 in the centre. */
export function blendWindow(tile, overlap) {
  const w = new Float32Array(tile);
  for (let i = 0; i < tile; i++) w[i] = overlap > 0 ? Math.min(1, (i + 0.5) / overlap, (tile - i - 0.5) / overlap) : 1;
  return w;
}

/** Copy of the ROI of an RGBA image. */
export function cropRGBA(rgba, w, roi) {
  const cw = roi.x1 - roi.x0, ch = roi.y1 - roi.y0, out = new Uint8ClampedArray(cw * ch * 4);
  for (let y = 0; y < ch; y++) out.set(rgba.subarray(((roi.y0 + y) * w + roi.x0) * 4, ((roi.y0 + y) * w + roi.x1) * 4), y * cw * 4);
  return out;
}

/** Bilinear resampling of `c` interleaved channels (pixel centres aligned: xs = (x + 0.5)·w/W − 0.5, clamped). */
export function resampleBilinear(src, w, h, c, W, H) {
  const Out = src instanceof Float32Array ? Float32Array : Uint8ClampedArray, out = new Out(W * H * c);
  const sx = w / W, sy = h / H;
  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {
    const fy = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, (y + 0.5) * sy - 0.5)), y0 = Math.floor(fy), y1 = Math.min(h - 1, y0 + 1), ty = fy - y0;
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
      const fx = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, (x + 0.5) * sx - 0.5)), x0 = Math.floor(fx), x1 = Math.min(w - 1, x0 + 1), tx = fx - x0;
      for (let k = 0; k < c; k++) {
        const a = src[(y0 * w + x0) * c + k], b = src[(y0 * w + x1) * c + k], d = src[(y1 * w + x0) * c + k], e = src[(y1 * w + x1) * c + k];
        out[(y * W + x) * c + k] = (a * (1 - tx) + b * tx) * (1 - ty) + (d * (1 - tx) + e * tx) * ty;
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Network input (NCHW float32, 3 channels) for the tile at (x0, y0); coordinates outside the image are clamped (edge padding). */
export function tileInput(rgba, w, h, x0, y0, tile, mean, std) {
  const n = tile * tile, out = new Float32Array(3 * n);
  for (let y = 0; y < tile; y++) {
    const yy = Math.min(h - 1, y0 + y);
    for (let x = 0; x < tile; x++) {
      const j = (yy * w + Math.min(w - 1, x0 + x)) * 4, i = y * tile + x;
      for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) out[c * n + i] = (rgba[j + c] / 255 - mean[c]) / std[c];
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Network output of one tile (C × tile × tile) to per-pixel probabilities: sigmoid (C = 1) or softmax (C > 1) of logits. */
export function toProbs(out, C, n, logits = true) {
  const p = new Float32Array(C * n);
  if (!logits) { p.set(out.subarray(0, C * n)); return p; }
  if (C === 1) { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) p[i] = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-out[i])); return p; }
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    let m = -Infinity; for (let c = 0; c < C; c++) m = Math.max(m, out[c * n + i]);
    let s = 0; for (let c = 0; c < C; c++) { const e = Math.exp(out[c * n + i] - m); p[c * n + i] = e; s += e; }
    for (let c = 0; c < C; c++) p[c * n + i] /= s;
  }
  return p;
}

/**
 * Probability of bioturbation over the ROI. `run(input, tile)` returns { data, channels } for one tile (logits or
 * probabilities as cfg.output says). `scale` = model px/cm ÷ image px/cm (1 = no resampling).
 * Returns { prob: Float32Array(w·h) (0 outside the ROI), cls: Uint8Array(w·h) | null (argmax class 1..C−1 for C > 1) }.
 */
export async function predictProb(run, rgba, w, h, roi, cfg = {}, scale = 1, onTile = null) {
  const c = { ...UNET_DEFAULTS, ...cfg }, T = c.tile;
  const cw = roi.x1 - roi.x0, chh = roi.y1 - roi.y0;
  let img = cropRGBA(rgba, w, roi), W = cw, H = chh;
  if (Math.abs(scale - 1) > 0.02) { W = Math.max(1, Math.round(cw * scale)); H = Math.max(1, Math.round(chh * scale)); img = resampleBilinear(img, cw, chh, 4, W, H); }
  const xs = tileStarts(W, T, c.overlap), ys = tileStarts(H, T, c.overlap), win = blendWindow(T, c.overlap), n = T * T;
  let C = 0, acc = null; const wsum = new Float32Array(W * H);
  let done = 0;
  for (const y0 of ys) for (const x0 of xs) {
    const r = await run(tileInput(img, W, H, x0, y0, T, c.mean, c.std), T);
    if (!C) { C = r.channels; acc = new Float32Array(W * H * C); }
    const p = toProbs(r.data, C, n, c.output !== 'probs');
    for (let y = 0; y < T && y0 + y < H; y++) for (let x = 0; x < T && x0 + x < W; x++) {
      const q = (y0 + y) * W + x0 + x, wt = win[y] * win[x], i = y * T + x;
      wsum[q] += wt; for (let k = 0; k < C; k++) acc[q * C + k] += wt * p[k * n + i];
    }
    if (onTile) onTile(++done, xs.length * ys.length);
  }
  for (let q = 0; q < W * H; q++) for (let k = 0; k < C; k++) acc[q * C + k] /= wsum[q] || 1;
  const planes = W === cw && H === chh ? acc : resampleBilinear(acc, W, H, C, cw, chh);
  const prob = new Float32Array(w * h), cls = C > 1 ? new Uint8Array(w * h) : null;
  for (let y = 0; y < chh; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cw; x++) {
    const q = (y * cw + x) * C, i = (roi.y0 + y) * w + roi.x0 + x;
    if (C === 1) { prob[i] = planes[q]; continue; }
    prob[i] = 1 - planes[q];
    let best = 1; for (let k = 2; k < C; k++) if (planes[q + k] > planes[q + best]) best = k;
    cls[i] = best;
  }
  return { prob, cls };
}

/** Pixels whose probability is within `band` of 0.5 (the model's doubtful zone). */
export const doubtful = (p, band = 0.2) => Math.abs(p - 0.5) < band;

/**
 * Training mask of the ROI: object class index + 1 for reviewed traces, 0 for matrix and artefacts,
 * IGNORE for excluded pixels and for objects without a confirmed class. objLabel[id] = value for object id
 * (k ≥ 1 class, 0 matrix/artefact, −1 ignore).
 */
export function trainingMask(labels, w, roi, exclude, objLabel, stacks = null) {   // V0.20: an overlap takes its top object
  const cw = roi.x1 - roi.x0, ch = roi.y1 - roi.y0, out = new Uint8Array(cw * ch);
  for (let y = 0; y < ch; y++) for (let x = 0; x < cw; x++) {
    const i = (roi.y0 + y) * w + roi.x0 + x, id = stacks ? topAt(labels[i], stacks) : labels[i];
    if (exclude && exclude[i]) { out[y * cw + x] = IGNORE; continue; }
    const v = id ? (objLabel[id] ?? -1) : 0;
    out[y * cw + x] = v < 0 ? IGNORE : v;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Most frequent U-Net class among the pixels of each object: Map id → { k, frac }. */
export function objectClasses(labels, cls, ids) {
  const cnt = new Map();
  for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
    const id = labels[i]; if (!id || !ids.has(id) || !cls[i]) continue;
    let m = cnt.get(id); if (!m) cnt.set(id, (m = new Map()));
    m.set(cls[i], (m.get(cls[i]) || 0) + 1);
  }
  const out = new Map();
  for (const [id, m] of cnt) {
    let k = 0, best = 0, tot = 0; for (const [c, v] of m) { tot += v; if (v > best) { best = v; k = c; } }
    out.set(id, { k, frac: best / tot });
  }
  return out;
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/vision.js
##########################################

// V0.17: descriptors of each trace from a pre-trained vision model (DINOv2), for the ichnotaxon classifier.
// DOM-free and independent of the inference engine. The model is not trained here: it turns the image of a
// trace into a vector of numbers ("embedding"); the existing k-NN / Random Forest / Bayes learn from those vectors.

/** Default model: DINOv2 ViT-S/14 (Oquab et al., 2024) exported to ONNX by the Hugging Face onnx-community. */
// V0.45: the official download is pinned to a Hugging Face commit and its verified model.onnx SHA-256.
// The hash of the file actually used is stored in the project; a mismatch raises the existing warning.
const VISION_REV = '8b1f705a3a7f6f062f6bdd21986c1583d3ef105d';
export const VISION_MODEL = {
  id: 'dinov2-small', name: 'DINOv2 ViT-S/14', size: 224, dim: 384, mb: 88, rev: VISION_REV,
  sha256: 'f22797eabf810a75e41de68d378541ebea372122b25c4ce3ef25ff618250c20a',
  url: `https://huggingface.co/onnx-community/dinov2-small/resolve/${VISION_REV}/onnx/model.onnx`,
  mean: [0.485, 0.456, 0.406], std: [0.229, 0.224, 0.225], pad: 0.25,
};
/** Name of the feature set stored with models and examples (vectors of different sets are never mixed). */
export const visionFeatureId = (m = VISION_MODEL) => [`vision:${m.id}:${m.dim}`];

/**
 * Square window around a trace: its bounding box [x0, y0, x1, y1] (inclusive) enlarged by `pad` of its larger
 * side on every side (context of the host sediment), at least `minSide` px; may extend past the image (edge padding).
 */
export function traceWindow(bbox, pad = 0.25, minSide = 16) {
  const [x0, y0, x1, y1] = bbox, bw = x1 - x0 + 1, bh = y1 - y0 + 1;
  const side = Math.max(minSide, Math.round(Math.max(bw, bh) * (1 + 2 * pad)));
  const cx = (x0 + x1 + 1) / 2, cy = (y0 + y1 + 1) / 2;
  return { x: Math.round(cx - side / 2), y: Math.round(cy - side / 2), side };
}

/** Network input (1 × 3 × size × size, float32): bilinear resampling of the window, ImageNet normalisation. */
export function traceInput(rgba, w, h, win, m = VISION_MODEL) {
  const S = m.size, n = S * S, out = new Float32Array(3 * n), k = win.side / S;
  for (let y = 0; y < S; y++) {
    const fy = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, win.y + (y + 0.5) * k - 0.5)), y0 = Math.floor(fy), y1 = Math.min(h - 1, y0 + 1), ty = fy - y0;
    for (let x = 0; x < S; x++) {
      const fx = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, win.x + (x + 0.5) * k - 0.5)), x0 = Math.floor(fx), x1 = Math.min(w - 1, x0 + 1), tx = fx - x0;
      for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
        const a = rgba[(y0 * w + x0) * 4 + c], b = rgba[(y0 * w + x1) * 4 + c], d = rgba[(y1 * w + x0) * 4 + c], e = rgba[(y1 * w + x1) * 4 + c];
        const v = ((a * (1 - tx) + b * tx) * (1 - ty) + (d * (1 - tx) + e * tx) * ty) / 255;
        out[c * n + y * S + x] = (v - m.mean[c]) / m.std[c];
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Embedding from the model outputs ({name: {data, dims}}): `pooler_output` if present, else the first output
 * (first token of a [1, tokens, dim] output = class token). Unit length (L2), rounded to 4 decimals for storage.
 */
export function pickEmbedding(outputs, names) {
  const name = names.includes('pooler_output') ? 'pooler_output' : names[0], o = outputs[name];
  const dim = o.dims[o.dims.length - 1], v = Array.from(o.data.subarray ? o.data.subarray(0, dim) : o.data.slice(0, dim));
  const norm = Math.sqrt(v.reduce((s, x) => s + x * x, 0)) || 1;
  return v.map((x) => +(x / norm).toFixed(4));
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/worker.js
##########################################

// V0.41: SedCoreTool background worker — enhancement and detection off the main thread (the interface no longer freezes
// for 1–3 s on large images). Uses the same pure functions as the main thread, so results are identical.
import { filterImageChunks, detectMask, components } from './imageproc.js';

const filters = new Map();

self.onmessage = (e) => {
  const { id, op, a } = e.data;
  try {
    if (op === 'filters') {
      filters.set(id, filterImageChunks(a.w, a.h, a.f, a.wide));
      self.postMessage({ id, more: true });
    } else if (op === 'filterChunk') {
      const work = filters.get(id);
      if (work.append(a.rgba)) {
        filters.delete(id);
        const r = work.finish();
        self.postMessage({ id, r }, [r.buffer]);
      } else self.postMessage({ id, more: true });
    } else if (op === 'cancelFilter') {
      filters.delete(id);
    } else if (op === 'detect') {
      const m = detectMask(a.enh, a.w, a.h, a.roi, a.exclude, a.p, a.prob);
      const r = components(m, a.w, a.h, a.p.minArea);
      self.postMessage({ id, r }, [r.labels.buffer]);
    } else throw new Error('unknown op ' + op);
  } catch (err) {
    filters.delete(id);
    self.postMessage({ id, err: String(err?.message || err) });
  }
};


##########################################
ARCHIVO: app/js/zip.js
##########################################

// SedCoreTool — minimal ZIP writer and reader (V0.8), no external libraries.
// Used for the shareable project package (.sedcoretool; before V0.52 .corantool): project.json + images/… at full resolution.
// Writer: method 0 (stored, images are already compressed). Reader: stored and deflate (DecompressionStream).
// Works on Blobs, so large images are not copied into one big string. Limit: 4 GB per package (no ZIP64).

const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const t = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) { let c = n; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1; t[n] = c >>> 0; }
  return t;
})();
export function crc32(bytes, crc = 0) {
  let c = ~crc >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) c = CRC_TABLE[(c ^ bytes[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);
  return ~c >>> 0;
}
const enc = new TextEncoder();
const ZIP_CRC_BLOCK_SIZE = 4 * 1024 ** 2;

async function storedZipCRC(blob) {
  let crc = 0;
  for (let start = 0; start < blob.size; start += ZIP_CRC_BLOCK_SIZE) {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(await blob.slice(start, start + ZIP_CRC_BLOCK_SIZE).arrayBuffer());
    crc = crc32(bytes, crc);
  }
  return crc;
}

/** files: [{ name, data: Blob | Uint8Array | string }] → Blob (application/zip). */
export async function zipFiles(files) {
  const parts = [], central = []; let offset = 0;
  for (const f of files) {
    const data = typeof f.data === 'string' ? new Blob([enc.encode(f.data)]) : f.data instanceof Blob ? f.data : new Blob([f.data]);
    const name = enc.encode(f.name), size = data.size;
    if (offset + size + 30 + name.length > 0xffffffff) throw new Error('ZIP > 4 GB');
    const crc = await storedZipCRC(data);
    const h = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(30));
    h.setUint32(0, 0x04034b50, true); h.setUint16(4, 20, true); h.setUint16(6, 0x0800, true); h.setUint16(8, 0, true);
    h.setUint32(14, crc, true); h.setUint32(18, size, true); h.setUint32(22, size, true); h.setUint16(26, name.length, true);
    parts.push(h.buffer, name, data);
    const c = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(46));
    c.setUint32(0, 0x02014b50, true); c.setUint16(4, 20, true); c.setUint16(6, 20, true); c.setUint16(8, 0x0800, true);
    c.setUint32(16, crc, true); c.setUint32(20, size, true); c.setUint32(24, size, true); c.setUint16(28, name.length, true); c.setUint32(42, offset, true);
    central.push(c.buffer, name);
    offset += 30 + name.length + size;
  }
  const cdSize = central.reduce((a, p) => a + (p.byteLength ?? p.length), 0), e = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(22));
  if (files.length > 65535 || offset + cdSize + 22 > 0xffffffff) throw new Error('ZIP > 4 GB or too many entries');
  e.setUint32(0, 0x06054b50, true); e.setUint16(8, files.length, true); e.setUint16(10, files.length, true);
  e.setUint32(12, cdSize, true); e.setUint32(16, offset, true);
  return new Blob([...parts, ...central, e.buffer], { type: 'application/zip' });
}


const ZIP_DIRECTORY_LIMIT = 16 * 1024**2;
const ZIP_JSON_LIMIT = 128 * 1024**2;
const ZIP_INFLATE_ENTRY_LIMIT = 256 * 1024**2;
const ZIP_INFLATE_TOTAL_LIMIT = 1024**3;

function inflateZipMember(raw, size, expectedCRC) {
  let offset = 0, produced = 0, crc = 0;
  const input = new ReadableStream({
    async pull(controller) {
      if (offset === raw.size) { controller.close(); return; }
      const end = Math.min(raw.size, offset + 16 * 1024);
      controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(await raw.slice(offset, end).arrayBuffer()));
      offset = end;
    },
  });
  const verify = new TransformStream({
    transform(chunk, controller) {
      produced += chunk.byteLength;
      if (produced > size) throw new Error('ZIP inflated size exceeds declared size');
      crc = crc32(chunk, crc);
      controller.enqueue(chunk);
    },
    flush() {
      if (produced !== size || crc !== expectedCRC) throw new Error('ZIP size or CRC mismatch');
    },
  });
  return new Response(input.pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw')).pipeThrough(verify)).blob();
}

/** Validate a classic ZIP before exposing lazy, bounded readers for its members. */
export async function unzipBlob(blob) {
  if (blob.size > 0xffffffff) throw new Error('ZIP exceeds the supported size');
  const tailLen = Math.min(blob.size, 65557), tail = new DataView(await blob.slice(blob.size - tailLen).arrayBuffer());
  let eocd = -1;
  for (let i = tailLen - 22; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (tail.getUint32(i, true) === 0x06054b50 && i + 22 + tail.getUint16(i + 20, true) === tailLen) { eocd = i; break; }
  }
  if (eocd < 0) throw new Error('not a ZIP file');
  const n = tail.getUint16(eocd + 10, true), cdSize = tail.getUint32(eocd + 12, true), cdOff = tail.getUint32(eocd + 16, true);
  if (tail.getUint16(eocd + 4, true) || tail.getUint16(eocd + 6, true) || tail.getUint16(eocd + 8, true) !== n ||
      cdSize > ZIP_DIRECTORY_LIMIT || cdSize < 46 * n || cdOff + cdSize !== blob.size - tailLen + eocd) {
    throw new Error('Invalid or oversized ZIP directory');
  }
  const cd = new DataView(await blob.slice(cdOff, cdOff + cdSize).arrayBuffer());
  const dec = new TextDecoder(), entries = [], out = new Map();
  let p = 0, expanded = 0, inflated = 0, bytesRead = 0, bytesInflated = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (p + 46 > cdSize || cd.getUint32(p, true) !== 0x02014b50) throw new Error('Invalid ZIP directory entry');
    const flags = cd.getUint16(p + 8, true), method = cd.getUint16(p + 10, true), crc = cd.getUint32(p + 16, true);
    const csize = cd.getUint32(p + 20, true), size = cd.getUint32(p + 24, true), lo = cd.getUint32(p + 42, true);
    const nl = cd.getUint16(p + 28, true), next = p + 46 + nl + cd.getUint16(p + 30, true) + cd.getUint16(p + 32, true);
    if (!nl || next > cdSize || flags & 0x41 || cd.getUint16(p + 34, true) || ![0, 8].includes(method)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid or unsupported ZIP member');
    }
    const name = dec.decode(new Uint8Array(cd.buffer, p + 46, nl));
    if (/[\\\u0000]/.test(name) || name.startsWith('/') || /^[a-z]:/i.test(name) ||
        name.replace(/\/$/, '').split('/').some((part) => !part || part === '..' || part === '.') || out.has(name)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid or duplicate ZIP member name');
    }
    expanded += size;
    if (method === 8) inflated += size;
    if (expanded > 0xffffffff || inflated > ZIP_INFLATE_TOTAL_LIMIT ||
        (method === 8 && size > ZIP_INFLATE_ENTRY_LIMIT) ||
        ((name.endsWith('project.json') || name === 'settings.json') && size > ZIP_JSON_LIMIT)) {
      throw new Error('ZIP uncompressed size exceeds the import limit');
    }
    if ((method === 0 && size !== csize) || lo + 30 + csize > cdOff) throw new Error('Invalid ZIP member size or offset');
    const entry = { lo, csize, end: cdOff };
    entries.push(entry);
    out.set(name, {
      async get() {
        bytesRead += size;
        if (method === 8) bytesInflated += size;
        if (bytesRead > 0xffffffff || bytesInflated > ZIP_INFLATE_TOTAL_LIMIT) throw new Error('ZIP read exceeds the import limit');
        const lh = new DataView(await blob.slice(lo, lo + 30).arrayBuffer());
        if (lh.getUint32(0, true) !== 0x04034b50 || lh.getUint16(6, true) !== flags || lh.getUint16(8, true) !== method ||
            (!(flags & 8) && (lh.getUint32(14, true) !== crc || lh.getUint32(18, true) !== csize || lh.getUint32(22, true) !== size))) {
          throw new Error('ZIP local header does not match directory');
        }
        const localNameSize = lh.getUint16(26, true), start = lo + 30 + localNameSize + lh.getUint16(28, true);
        if (localNameSize !== nl || start + csize > entry.end ||
            dec.decode(await blob.slice(lo + 30, lo + 30 + localNameSize).arrayBuffer()) !== name) {
          throw new Error('Invalid or overlapping ZIP member');
        }
        const raw = blob.slice(start, start + csize);
        if (method === 8) return inflateZipMember(raw, size, crc);
        if (await storedZipCRC(raw) !== crc) throw new Error(`ZIP CRC mismatch for ${name}`);
        return raw;
      },
    });
    p = next;
  }
  if (p !== cdSize) throw new Error('Invalid ZIP directory length');
  entries.sort((a, b) => a.lo - b.lo);
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < entries.length; i++) {
    const current = entries[i];
    current.end = entries[i + 1].lo;
    if (current.lo + 30 + current.csize > current.end) throw new Error('Overlapping ZIP members');
  }
  return out;
}
